This file is the plan for the hardest phase of the agent overhaul. It is written for an
executor that has not seen the analysis conversation. Read it top to bottom once before
starting any task: §2 (Contracts) defines every type the tasks use, and §3 (Migration
order) says in which order the tasks must land so the app keeps working after each one.

## 1. Phase overview

### 1.1 Goal

The conversation keeps flowing while tools and agents work in the background. Concretely,
after this phase:

1. Talking to the agent never pauses background work. Only the moment a background task
   is about to *change* something (send, create, modify) waits until the latest thing the
   user said has been understood.
2. "How's it going?" is answered in well under a second from the task ledger — which step
   is running, how long it has been going — with no model call.
3. Results come back as conversation: phrased by the same voice, at a natural pause
   (350 ms after the user and the agent both stopped talking), several results together
   if they finished together, never cutting the user off, never lost because the session
   closed.
4. The user can interrupt at any time. A provisional sound pauses (or ducks) the agent; a
   real turn stops it; nothing but an explicit "cancel that" stops work.
5. Coding tasks started by voice go to the coding agent (ACP) and can be asked about and
   cancelled by voice. Approvals can be given by voice, within the app's permission model.
6. The notch island shows the conversation, not whichever worker last touched it.
7. One reducer names the current turn and the current output; the ten ad-hoc epoch
   counters are gone.
8. (H-audit 2026-09-23, P3-10) Speech in the room that is not meant for the agent is not
   answered: the frontend model can decide a turn was not addressed to it, says nothing and
   starts nothing, and two such turns in a row (or a minute of them after a wake) close the
   session with "Going quiet."

### 1.2 What blocks it today (C1–C12)

One line each, with the verified anchor and the task that removes it.

| # | Blocking point (verified 2026-09-23) | Fixed by |
|---|---|---|
| C1 | A failed frontend turn leaves the effect barrier closed on purpose: `VoiceConversationCoordinator.finishFailure` (Coordinator:546-575, comment at :572-573 "Keep the effect barrier closed"), and the idle close is disabled while `hasActiveWork` (Capture:1272-1281), so work freezes and the mic stays open. | P0-07 (resolve on failure + write gate); P3-01 keeps it fixed with production-path tests |
| C2 | Every planner round and call waits for input resolution: `await waitForVoiceInput()` at ToolLoop:864 (round start), :915 (after completion), :954 (before each call), and inside `isStillValid` at :999-1002. The barrier rises on 120 ms of energy (`noteLevel`, Capture:887-901 → `inputActivityStarted`, Coordinator:196-199) and falls only after the whole answer is generated (`resolveInput(epoch:)` at Coordinator:497). | P3-02 |
| C3 | One running job for the whole process: `ComputeScheduler.running: ComputeJob?` (Core/Compute/ComputeScheduler.swift:87); FM and llama never overlap. | P3-07a |
| C4 | One llama native context (`NotesModelRuntime.context`, Formatting/LLM/NotesModelRuntime.swift:63) with one non-preemptible reservation (`reserveNativeContext`, :698-715; `releaseNativeContext`, :884-894): voice work waits behind meeting notes. | P3-07b |
| C5 | Results are spoken raw and can be lost: `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.flush` speaks the first two clauses verbatim or "There's an update from your background task…" (Agent/VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift:45-50), `enqueue` drops when no session (:12-15), `endSession` clears it (Capture:360), the 100 ms tick flushes without checking the barrier (Capture:1268). | P3-04 |
| C6 | Work status is `"Task N [running]: <prompt>"` (`frontendRequest`, Coordinator:666-671): "how's it going?" can only get "still working on it". | P3-03 |
| C7 | Voice never reaches the harness router — `RealtimeAgent.handle` returns into the coordinator first (Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift:207-211) — and there are two ledgers: `Coordinator.jobs` (:21) and `AgentTaskManager.tasks` (mirrored only via `beginVoiceObjective`, Agent/Tasks/AgentTaskManager.swift:88-96). | P3-03 |
| C8 | No spoken approval path (`PermissionGate` answers only cards; callers `UI/Agent/AgentView.swift:285-296`, `Agent/AgentService.swift:76`), and a spoken correction cancels the pending approval (`PermissionGate.shared.cancelPending(taskID:)` at Coordinator:347 and :525). | P3-08 |
| C9 | Output cross-talk: `VoicePlaybackDelivery.receive(.began)` binds `RealtimeAgent.shared.currentGeneration` (Agent/VoicePlaybackDelivery.swift:28-29); any `RealtimeAudioSession.speak`/`beginSpokenReply` clears the one buffer (Agent/RealtimeAudioSession.swift:146-175); `isThinking` can stick because `respond` returns without `finishVoiceFrontend` when superseded (Coordinator:459-461). | P3-04b, P3-06b |
| C10 | Voice workers drive the island: `beginWork` → `ActivationController.markWorking()` + `IslandState.showAgentWork` (Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift:516-521), reached from workers via `runDirectIntent` (ToolLoop:1112); `runTool` (ToolLoop:304) is typed-path only (verified in P3-09). | P3-09 |
| C11 | No conversation when Apple Intelligence is unavailable: the coordinator speaks `unavailableReply` (Coordinator:415-422); there is no fallback lane. | P3-07d |
| C12 | Ten independent epoch counters (`turnEpoch`, `turnGeneration` in Capture; `inputEpoch`, `preparationEpoch`, `responseID` in Coordinator; `generation`, `speechGeneration` in RealtimeAgent; `outputGeneration` in AgentSpeechSynthesizer; `stagingEpoch`, `sealedThroughEpoch` in LocalVoiceFrontend). Nothing names "the current turn" once. | P3-06 |

### 1.3 Target architecture

Reproduced from the decision document §4. Every box is explained below the diagram.

```
 mic ──► Audio I/O (AVAudioSinkNode capture, 10 ms IO, source-node playback)
          │  AcousticEchoProcessor (SpeexDSP / AEC3) with the exact playback reference
          ▼
 InputProcessor  = today's AgentCaptureController logic, emitting events instead of calling 4 objects
          │  Silero VAD (FluidAudio)  ── speech prob ≥ θ for ~120 ms while speaking ──► Interruption.provisional (duck)
          │  Parakeet EOU (resident)  ── raw EOU ──► provisional commit (speculative answer starts, audio gated)
          │  SmartTurnArbiter (Smart Turn v3.2, CoreML) ── decides WHEN, never WHETHER the model hears the user
          │  Apple SpeechAnalyzer / Parakeet partials ── ASR text
          ▼  VoiceEvent (AsyncStream)                                  Interruption (control channel, overtakes data)
 VoiceSession  (@MainActor, pure reducer: state = reduce(state, VoiceEvent))
          │  owns TurnID / OutputID, the floor, the input epoch; the only writer of IslandState/ActivationController
          ├──────────────► FrontendResponder (actor) = LocalVoiceFrontend + Coordinator.respond
          │                   one streamed decision: FrontendDecision { answer | capabilities | spawn | revise |
          │                   cancel | status | respondPermission }; prewarmed during speech
          │                   frontend tools (admission: < 2 s, one call, own state, low risk):
          │                     spawn_task(objective)  → receipt in ms, then ONE natural ack, no filler
          │                     task_status(id?)       → read from the ledger, no model call
          │                     cancel_task(id?)       → confirmed, "cancelling" until it is
          │                     respond_approval(id?, decision) → only while an approval is pending
          │                     cheap reads: time, next events, memory recall, knowledge search
          │                   ▲ phrase(result) for deliveries, under result-only instructions
          ▼                   │
 TaskBridge (actor) ── one ledger: voice jobs + AgentTaskManager + ACP tasks
          │   states: queued → running → (delegated → finalizing) → completed | failed | needsInput |
          │           needsPermission | cancelling → cancelled
          │   progress = observability only (never spoken unless ≤ 1 line/60 s narration)
          │   mayCommitEffect(task, revision) ← the ONLY place the input barrier applies
          ▼  BackendPort
 Workers (headless; never touch UI, speech or the input flag)
          LocalToolLoopWorker (llama, its own llama_context, its own lane class) · ACPAgentBackend · KnowledgeAsker
          each receives a self-contained objective (no persona, no chat history), uses AgentCapabilityManifest,
          native/constrained tool calls, repair loop, per-round budget
          │  TaskEvent
          ▼
 OutputScheduler (AgentDelivery) — the only component that starts audio
          modes: context | respond | interrupt   (≈ Gemini SILENT / WHEN_IDLE / INTERRUPT)
          window: user floor free ∧ no turn pending ∧ no audio queued, then ~350 ms quiet
          batch completions within 120 ms (≤ 8 items); frontend phrases them ("not a new request",
          open with the result, no "task complete" filler); ack on first audible frame;
          user barge-in ⇒ delivered-in-context; other cancel ⇒ bounded retry; status question consumes
          a pending delivery; undelivered results carry over to the next session
          ▼  OutputRequest(OutputID, kind, text stream, priority)
 Streaming TTS — one PocketTtsSession per reply, clause N+1 synthesised while N plays, engine kept running,
          first chunk at a comma / ~6–8 words, per-clause length cap ──► speaker (and back to AEC as reference)

 ComputeScheduler: lanes with capacity — realtimeASR exclusive; realtimeAgent (FM) may overlap background (llama)
          behind a measured flag; worker warmup never at the frontend's class; acquire is cancellable.
```

Component by component. "Today" is the code that plays the role now; "Phase 3 builds" is
what this phase adds or changes. Audio I/O, the input processor's VAD/EOU/Smart Turn, and
streaming TTS belong to Phase 2 and are only consumed here.

| Component | What it is responsible for | Today | Phase 3 builds |
|---|---|---|---|
| Audio I/O + AEC | Capture, echo cancellation with the exact playback reference | `Core/AudioCaptureHub.swift`, `Agent/AcousticEchoProcessor.swift` | nothing (P2-08) |
| **InputProcessor** | Turns audio into facts: activity, partials, discards, hesitations, commits. It never decides what the agent does. | `AgentCaptureController` (Capture), which today also calls into the coordinator, `RealtimeAgent.shared` and `RealtimeAudioSession` directly | The role name only. `AgentCaptureController` keeps its VAD/EOU/echo logic intact (AGENTS.md documents why each rule exists) and replaces its direct calls with `VoiceSession.shared.send(_:)` / `interrupt(_:)` (P3-06). No rename. |
| **SmartTurnArbiter** | Decides *when* a turn ends, never *whether* it is heard | fixed thresholds in Capture:96-108 | nothing (P2-04). Its output is the `.committed` event. |
| **VoiceSession** | The single owner of "who has the floor", "what is the current turn", "what is the current output", "are effects held". A pure reducer plus a thin shell that executes commands. The only writer of the island and `ActivationController` while a session is open. | Spread across `Coordinator.inputPending/inputEpoch/responseID`, `RealtimeAgent.voiceInputActive/isThinking/speechGeneration`, `RealtimeAudioSession.isSpeaking`, Capture's `heardSpeech/turnGeneration` | P3-06a (types, reducer, shadow mode), P3-06b (ownership switch), P3-09 (island) |
| **FrontendResponder** | Answers the user's turn: one streamed decision, then an answer stream, or a receipt, status, cancel or approval. Also phrases deliveries. | `LocalVoiceFrontend` (actor) + `Coordinator.respond` | P3-05 (a concrete `actor FrontendResponder`; P5-01 later turns it into a protocol for cloud frontends) |
| **TaskBridge** | One ledger for every background task a person can ask about: voice jobs, typed/ACP tasks. Owns task state, revisions, cancellation, approvals routing, delivery state, and the effect gate `mayCommitEffect`. | `Coordinator.jobs` + `AgentTaskManager` (two ledgers) | P3-03 (ledger, status), P3-02 (gate), P3-08 (approvals) |
| **Workers** | Do the work; headless | `RealtimeAgent(voiceWorker:)` running `runPlannedToolLoop`; `ACPAgentBackend`; `KnowledgeAsker` | P3-07c (`LocalToolLoopWorker` with its own llama context), P3-09 (headless) |
| **OutputScheduler / AgentDelivery** | The only component that starts audio. Queues answers, fixed replies, results, questions and approval prompts; opens the delivery window; batches; phrases through the frontend; acknowledges on first audible frame; applies the interruption rule; carries undelivered results over. | `VoiceAnnouncementQueue` + direct `RealtimeAudioSession.speak` callers + `VoicePlaybackDelivery` | P3-04a (deliveries), P3-04b (all audio) |
| Streaming TTS | Clause pipeline, Pocket session | `Agent/Speech/*` | nothing (P2-06); consumed through `RealtimeAudioSession` |
| **ComputeScheduler** | Lanes with capacity | one global slot | P3-07a (capacity + measured FM overlap flag), P3-07b (second llama context) |

## 2. Contracts

Every type below is defined **before** the tasks that use it. They live in new files under
`Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/` (a new folder; add it to the `README.md` tree under
`Agent/` with one line: `Duplex/ VoiceSession reducer, TaskBridge ledger, OutputScheduler,
FrontendResponder — the full-duplex spine`). P3-06a creates `VoiceContracts.swift` with the
identifier, event, decision and state types; P3-03 adds `TaskBridge.swift`; P3-04 adds
`OutputScheduler.swift`; P3-05 adds `FrontendResponder.swift`. Everything is Swift 6 strict
concurrency clean: value types are `Sendable`, and every event/command/decision type is
`Codable` so a session can be recorded and replayed.

### 2.1 Identifiers

```swift
/// One user turn, from its first acoustic activity to its classification.
struct TurnID: Hashable, Comparable, Sendable, Codable, CustomStringConvertible {
    let raw: UInt64
    static func < (lhs: TurnID, rhs: TurnID) -> Bool { lhs.raw < rhs.raw }
    var description: String { "turn#\(raw)" }
}

/// One audible output: an answer, a fixed reply, a delivery batch, an approval prompt.
struct OutputID: Hashable, Comparable, Sendable, Codable, CustomStringConvertible {
    let raw: UInt64
    static func < (lhs: OutputID, rhs: OutputID) -> Bool { lhs.raw < rhs.raw }
    var description: String { "output#\(raw)" }
}

/// One unit of background work in the ledger. `raw` is the `AgentTask.id` string, which is
/// a UUID string for every producer today (voice jobs use `VoiceConversationWork.id`,
/// `AgentTask` defaults to `UUID().uuidString`), so the ledger and `agent-tasks.json`
/// share ids. (The C report sketched `raw: UUID`; a String avoids a lossy conversion for
/// ids read back from disk.)
struct TaskID: Hashable, Sendable, Codable, CustomStringConvertible {
    let raw: String
    init(_ raw: String) { self.raw = raw }
    init(_ uuid: UUID) { self.raw = uuid.uuidString }
    var description: String { "task#\(raw.prefix(8))" }
}

/// Process-wide, monotonic, never reset by a session: a late event from an older session
/// can never match a newer id. Minted only by the VoiceSession shell and the
/// OutputScheduler — never by the reducer, which only compares ids.
@MainActor
final class VoiceIDMint {
    static let shared = VoiceIDMint()
    private var turn: UInt64 = 0
    private var output: UInt64 = 0
    func nextTurn() -> TurnID { turn &+= 1; return TurnID(raw: turn) }
    func nextOutput() -> OutputID { output &+= 1; return OutputID(raw: output) }
}

/// Time as the reducer sees it: milliseconds since the shell's reference instant. The
/// shell stamps every event; the reducer never reads a clock (so replay is exact).
/// Pure replay-relative milliseconds; distinct from existing latency VoiceInstant.
struct VoiceSessionInstant: Comparable, Sendable, Codable {
    let milliseconds: Int64
    static func < (l: VoiceSessionInstant, r: VoiceSessionInstant) -> Bool { l.milliseconds < r.milliseconds }
    func adding(_ d: Duration) -> VoiceSessionInstant { .init(milliseconds: milliseconds + d.wholeMilliseconds) }
}
// Add `extension Duration { var wholeMilliseconds: Int64 }` in the same file
// (components.seconds * 1000 + components.attoseconds / 1_000_000_000_000_000).
```

What they replace (C12):

| Counter today | Owner | Fate |
|---|---|---|
| `turnEpoch` | Capture (guards late EOU/partial callbacks, `makeEOUCallback` Capture:1071-1080) | Replaced by the `TurnID` of the turn the callback was created for (P3-06b) |
| `turnGeneration` | Capture (`activeTurnTask` ownership, Capture:1347-1360) | Replaced by `TurnID` |
| `inputEpoch` + `inputPending` | Coordinator:22,25 | Replaced by `VoiceSessionState.unclassified` / `effectsHeld` |
| `responseID` | Coordinator:24 | Replaced by the response's `TurnID` |
| `generation` | RealtimeAgent:43 | Kept for the typed path only; voice uses `TurnID` |
| `speechGeneration` | RealtimeAgent:50 | Replaced by `OutputID` |
| `outputGeneration` | AgentSpeechSynthesizer:69 | Kept private to the synthesizer; `OutputScheduler` maps it 1:1 to an `OutputID` |
| `preparationEpoch` | Coordinator:27 (speculation debounce) | Kept, private to `FrontendResponder` (speculation revision), renamed `speculationRevision` |
| `stagingEpoch`, `sealedThroughEpoch` | LocalVoiceFrontend:36,108 | Kept, private to the frontend actor |

After P3-06b the rule is: **the current turn is a `TurnID` and the current output is an
`OutputID`, both owned by `VoiceSession`. Frontend-internal revisions stay private and never
leave the frontend.**

### 2.2 Events and interruptions

```swift
enum VoiceSessionCloseReason: String, Sendable, Codable { case done, goodbye, idle, stopButton, error, notAddressed } // notAddressed: P3-10 (H-audit 2026-09-23)

enum InputDiscardReason: String, Sendable, Codable {
    case noWords, backchannel, playbackEcho, inFlightRepeat, revisedTranscript
}

enum ResponseOutcome: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case answered            // an answer stream completed
    case fixedReply          // a deterministic reply (gate, receipt ack, status, clarifier)
    case failed(code: String) // VoiceFrontendFailure.code raw value
    case cancelled           // superseded or explicitly cancelled
}

enum PlaybackPhase: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case began               // OutputScheduler handed the first clause to TTS
    case firstAudio          // first audible frame of this output (AgentSpeechSynthesizer .startAcknowledged of its first clause)
    case clauseCompleted
    case finished            // last clause played
    case interrupted(byUser: Bool)
}

enum OutputKind: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case answer(TurnID)
    case fixed(TurnID)
    case delivery([TaskID])           // one batch of results / failures
    case approvalPrompt(requestID: String, task: TaskID)
    case taskQuestion(TaskID)          // a worker's needsInput question
}

enum VoiceTimer: String, Sendable, Codable { case quietWindow, idleCheck, deliveryRetry }

/// The one "system frame". It overtakes data: `VoiceSession.interrupt(_:)` reduces it
/// immediately, before any queued work, and every command executor re-checks the current
/// TurnID/OutputID before acting.
enum Interruption: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    /// Pause (or duck, P2-05) the current output. Keep generating. Keep work running.
    case provisional(TurnID)
    /// Stop the current output and cancel the current response. Work survives.
    case committed(TurnID)
    /// The only thing that invalidates work. nil = the single cancellable session task,
    /// or the most recent one when several exist (the frontend disambiguates first).
    case explicitCancel(TaskID?)
}

enum VoiceEvent: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    // session
    case sessionOpened(sessionID: UUID)
    case sessionClosed(VoiceSessionCloseReason)
    // input (InputProcessor = AgentCaptureController)
    case inputActivity(TurnID)                    // ≥ 120 ms energy (today inputActivityStarted, Capture:896-901)
    case partial(TurnID, text: String, stable: Bool)
    case rawEndOfUtterance(TurnID, text: String)  // P2-03 provisional commit; reducer may ignore until P2-03
    case inputDiscarded(TurnID, InputDiscardReason)
    case inputWithdrawn                         // current global discardInput seam; not stale acoustic discard
    case hesitation(TurnID, text: String)
    case committed(TurnID, text: String)
    // frontend (FrontendResponder / Coordinator during migration)
    case decisionMade(TurnID, FrontendDecision)   // the route is known ⇒ barrier release point
    case responseStarted(TurnID, OutputID)
    case responseEnded(TurnID, ResponseOutcome)
    // output (OutputScheduler)
    case outputQueued(OutputID, OutputKind)
    case playback(OutputID, PlaybackPhase)
    // work (TaskBridge)
    case task(TaskEvent)
    case approvalPending(requestID: String, task: TaskID)
    case approvalResolved(requestID: String)
    // time and control
    case timer(VoiceTimer)
    case interruption(Interruption)               // recorded for replay; delivered through interrupt(_:)
}
```

### 2.3 VoiceSession state, commands, reducer

```swift
enum IslandProjection: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case none            // session closed: VoiceSession writes nothing
    case listening       // IslandState.showAgentListening(transcript:level:) — level/transcript pass through the shell
    case thinking        // the session's own turn is being answered: showAgentWork(title: "Thinking…")
    case speaking        // listening card while output plays (today's behaviour)
    case proposal(String) // PermissionGate owns the card; VoiceSession only records it
}

struct VoiceSessionState: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    enum Phase: String, Sendable, Codable { case closed, open }
    enum Floor: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
        case free
        case userProvisional(TurnID)   // activity or partials; not yet a turn
    }
    struct Response: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
        var turn: TurnID
        var decided: Bool              // decisionMade received
        var output: OutputID?
    }
    struct Output: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
        enum Status: String, Sendable, Codable { case queued, playing, paused }
        var id: OutputID
        var kind: OutputKind
        var status: Status
        var heard: Bool                // firstAudio received
    }

    var phase: Phase = .closed
    var sessionID: UUID?
    var floor: Floor = .free
    /// Turns whose meaning is not yet known. Effects wait while this is non-empty.
    /// Replaces inputPending/inputEpoch. A turn leaves it on decisionMade, discard,
    /// responseEnded, supersession, or session close. Failure clears the input/read
    /// state (C1), but preserves the separate consequential-effect hold below.
    var unclassified: Set<TurnID> = []
    /// Mirrors the current coordinator's effectHoldEpoch. Failure is not classification.
    var failedInputHold: TurnID?
    var effectsHeld: Bool { !unclassified.isEmpty || failedInputHold != nil }
    /// The committed turn whose reply is not finished (qwen's `turnPending`).
    var turnPending: TurnID?
    var lastCommittedTurn: TurnID? // duplicate/older commit cannot start another response
    var response: Response?            // at most one conversational response
    var output: Output?                // at most one audible output
    var lastUserSpeechAt: VoiceSessionInstant?
    var lastOutputEndAt: VoiceSessionInstant?
    var activeTasks: Set<TaskID> = []  // non-terminal tasks owned by this session (TaskBridge)
    var pendingDeliveries: Int = 0     // mirrored from OutputScheduler for the window rule
    var pendingApproval: String?       // PermissionRequest.id of a session-owned pending approval
    var island: IslandProjection = .none

    /// D1 in §2.6: the only definition of the delivery window.
    func deliveryWindowOpen(now: VoiceSessionInstant) -> Bool {
        guard phase == .open, floor == .free, turnPending == nil,
              response == nil, output == nil, unclassified.isEmpty else { return false }
        let last = max(lastUserSpeechAt ?? .init(milliseconds: 0), lastOutputEndAt ?? .init(milliseconds: 0))
        return now >= last.adding(AgentDeliveryTiming.quietWindow)
    }
}

enum VoiceCommand: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case setEffectsHeld(Bool)                         // TaskBridge.setEffectsHeld (mayCommitEffect waits while true)
    case pauseOutput(OutputID)                        // OutputScheduler.pause (duck when P2-05 is in)
    case resumeOutput(OutputID)
    case stopOutput(OutputID, byUser: Bool)           // OutputScheduler.stop — byUser drives delivery rule D5/D6
    case cancelResponse(TurnID)                       // FrontendResponder.cancel (today responseTask?.cancel())
    case startResponse(TurnID, text: String)          // FrontendResponder.respond (today Coordinator.handle)
    case speculate(TurnID, text: String)              // FrontendResponder.speculate (stable partial)
    case cancelTask(TaskID?)                          // TaskBridge.cancel
    case openDeliveryWindow                           // OutputScheduler.windowOpened()
    case scheduleTimer(VoiceTimer, after: Duration)
    case publishIsland(IslandProjection)
    case closeSession(VoiceSessionCloseReason)
}

enum VoiceSessionReducer {
    /// Pure. No clocks, no singletons, no I/O. Same inputs ⇒ same outputs.
    static func reduce(_ state: VoiceSessionState, _ event: VoiceEvent,
                       at now: VoiceSessionInstant) -> (VoiceSessionState, [VoiceCommand])
}
```

**Transition table.** Rows are evaluated top to bottom; "stale" means the event's id is not
the current one (an older `TurnID` than `response.turn`, an `OutputID` that is not
`output.id`). Every row compares effective `effectsHeld` before and after its full
mutation and emits `setEffectsHeld` only on a change. Removing the final unclassified
turn must not release a retained failed-input hold. These edge commands are omitted
for brevity. Every row that changes `island` emits `publishIsland`.

| # | State (guard) | Event | New state | Commands |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | closed | `sessionOpened(id)` | open, sessionID=id, all fields reset, island=.listening | `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, 350 ms)` (carry-over, D8), `scheduleTimer(.idleCheck, 1 s)` |
| 2 | open | `sessionOpened` | treat as row 3 then row 1 | — |
| 3 | open | `sessionClosed(r)` | closed; unclassified=[]; failedInputHold=nil; response=nil; output=nil; floor=.free; island=.none | `stopOutput(o, byUser:false)` if output; `cancelResponse(t)` if response. **Tasks untouched** (AGENTS: work survives) |
| 4 | open, floor free | `inputActivity(t)` | floor=.userProvisional(t); unclassified ∪= {t}; lastUserSpeechAt=now | — (energy alone never pauses output; Capture:890-893) |
| 5 | open | `partial(t, text, stable)` | lastUserSpeechAt=now; if floor free → floor=.userProvisional(t), unclassified ∪= {t} | `speculate(t, text)` if stable |
| 6 | open, output playing | `interruption(.provisional(t))` | output.status=.paused; floor=.userProvisional(t); unclassified ∪= {t} | `pauseOutput(o)` |
| 7 | open, no output or already paused | `interruption(.provisional(t))` | floor=.userProvisional(t); unclassified ∪= {t} | — |
| 8 | floor .userProvisional(t) | `inputDiscarded(t, _)` | floor=.free; unclassified −= {t} | `resumeOutput(o)` if output paused; `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, 350 ms)` |
| 8b | open | `inputWithdrawn` | floor=.free; unclassified=[]; failedInputHold=nil | preserve tasks/output; `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, 350 ms)` |
| 9 | floor .userProvisional(t) | `hesitation(t, _)` | unchanged (filler holds the floor and the barrier; AGENTS: a filler holds unfinished input without invalidating an answer) | — |
| 10 | open | `interruption(.committed(t))` | if output: output=nil, lastOutputEndAt=now; if response and response.turn < t: unclassified −= {response.turn}, response=nil | `stopOutput(o, byUser:true)`; `cancelResponse(response.turn)` |
| 11 | open, t newer than lastCommittedTurn | `committed(t, text)` | lastCommittedTurn=t; apply row 10 if not already applied; floor=.free; turnPending=t; unclassified ∪= {t}; response=Response(turn:t, decided:false); island=.thinking; lastUserSpeechAt=now | `startResponse(t, text)` |
| 12 | response.turn == t, !decided | `decisionMade(t, d)` | response.decided=true; unclassified −= {x ≤ t}; clear failedInputHold only if t > hold | — (the frontend executes d; reducer only records that meaning is known) |
| 13 | stale t | `decisionMade(t, _)` | unclassified −= {t} | — |
| 14 | response.turn == t | `responseStarted(t, o)` | response.output=o; output=Output(id:o, kind:.answer(t), status:.queued, heard:false) | — |
| 15 | stale t | `responseStarted(t, o)` | unchanged | `stopOutput(o, byUser:false)` (a late stream must not play) |
| 16 | response.turn == t | `responseEnded(t, outcome)` | on failure set failedInputHold=t before clearing input; response=nil; unclassified −= {t} (C1 reads resume; effects stay held); if no output for t → turnPending=nil; island=.listening | `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, 350 ms)` |
| 17 | stale t | `responseEnded(t, _)` | unclassified −= {t} | — |
| 18 | open, output nil | `outputQueued(o, kind)` (non-answer kinds from OutputScheduler) | output=Output(id:o, kind, .queued, heard:false) | — |
| 19 | output.id == o | `playback(o, .firstAudio)` | output.status=.playing, heard=true; island=.speaking | — (OutputScheduler acknowledges deliveries itself, D4) |
| 20 | output.id == o | `playback(o, .finished)` | output=nil; lastOutputEndAt=now; if kind == .answer(t)/.fixed(t) and turnPending == t → turnPending=nil; island=.listening | `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, 350 ms)` |
| 21 | output.id == o | `playback(o, .interrupted(byUser))` | output=nil; lastOutputEndAt=now | — |
| 22 | stale o | any `playback(o, _)` | unchanged | — |
| 23 | open | `timer(.quietWindow)` | unchanged | if `deliveryWindowOpen(now)` and pendingDeliveries > 0 → `openDeliveryWindow`; else if only the 350 ms is missing → `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, remaining)` |
| 24 | open | `task(.accepted(id, …))` with origin in this session | activeTasks ∪= {id} | — |
| 25 | open | `task(.completed/.failed/.cancelled(id))` | activeTasks −= {id} | — |
| 26 | open | `task(.deliveryQueued(count))` | pendingDeliveries=count | `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, 0)` |
| 27 | open | `approvalPending(req, task)` | pendingApproval=req; island=.proposal(req) | — (PermissionGate raised the card) |
| 28 | open | `approvalResolved(req)` | pendingApproval=nil; island=.listening | — |
| 29 | open | `interruption(.explicitCancel(id))` | unchanged | `cancelTask(id)` |
| 30 | open | `timer(.idleCheck)` | unchanged | if floor free ∧ response nil ∧ output nil ∧ turnPending nil ∧ activeTasks empty ∧ pendingApproval nil ∧ now − max(lastUserSpeechAt, lastOutputEndAt, sessionOpen) ≥ 25 s → `closeSession(.idle)`; else `scheduleTimer(.idleCheck, 1 s)` |
| 31 | closed | any other event | unchanged | — |

Notes on the table:

- **Producer-contract amendment, 2026-10-01:** row 16's failure clears response/input
  and releases planning/reads, but first sets `failedInputHold=t`. It does not release
  consequential effects. Row 12 clears that hold only for a later valid classified
  turn; stale decisions/end events and supersession alone cannot clear it. Session
  close resets it. Current-input withdrawal clears it through an explicit
  `.inputWithdrawn` event at the existing coordinator `discardInput` seam, including
  withdrawal after the floor/response ended. Keep stale acoustic `.inputDiscarded`
  separate: it cannot clear a newer failure hold. Delivery remains governed by
  floor/response/output/unclassified; the write-only failed hold does not suppress
  read results or repair replies. Rows 16/T4 now carry this distinction explicitly.
- Row 30 is the idle close with C1 fixed and C's ACP gap closed: `activeTasks` comes from the
  ledger and includes ACP tasks, and a pending approval keeps the session open. Pending
  *deliveries* do not keep it open — they carry over (D8).
- Rows 10–11: InputProcessor sends `interrupt(.committed(t))` first and then
  `send(.committed(t, text))`. When only `committed` arrives (an older call site, a test),
  row 11 applies row 10 itself. Both are idempotent; duplicate/older commits
  start no response, including duplicates after a reply ended.
- `setEffectsHeld` is the only barrier signal. `TaskBridge.mayCommitEffect` (§2.5) is the
  only reader.

### 2.4 FrontendDecision

```swift
/// A spoken answer to a pending approval. Named to avoid the existing
/// `PermissionDecision` (allow/ask/deny) in Agent/Permissions/PermissionRequest.swift:86.
enum SpokenPermissionDecision: String, Sendable, Codable { case approve, deny }

enum FrontendDecision: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case answer                                  // an answer stream follows
    case capabilities                            // VoiceCapabilitySnapshot.current().spokenSummary
    case spawn(objective: String, userWords: String, backend: AgentBackendKind?) // nil ⇒ TaskBridge chooses
    case revise(TaskID, text: String)
    case cancel(TaskID?)
    case status(TaskID?)                         // answered from the ledger, no model call
    case respondPermission(requestID: String?, decision: SpokenPermissionDecision, amendment: String?)
    case notAddressed                            // P3-10 (H-audit 2026-09-23): speech not meant for the agent; no speech, no work
}
```

Mapping from today's routes (`LocalVoiceRoute.Intent`, Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceDecision.swift:64-70,
emitted as envelopes by `LocalVoiceFrontend.streamSplitDecision`,
Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:516-550, parsed by `VoiceFrontendEnvelope.parse`,
Coordinator:782-807):

| Today | FrontendDecision | Added in |
|---|---|---|
| `answerQuestion` / `<answer/>…` | `.answer` | P3-05 (typed mapping) |
| `describeCapabilities` / `<capabilities/>` | `.capabilities` | P3-05 |
| `startExternalTask` / `<use_tools/>`; tool-shape gate (Coordinator:371-378); pending-intent ack (:359-367); denial backstop (:484-495) | `.spawn(objective:userWords:backend:)` | P3-03 (receipt), P3-05 (objective + ack fields) |
| `reviseRunningTask` / `<revise id="N"/>`; naming turn with an active job (:341-350) | `.revise(TaskID, text:)` | P3-03 |
| `cancelRunningTask` / `<cancel id="N"/>`; `isExplicitWorkCancellation` with one job (:332-337; :379 on 2026-09-24) — (H-audit 2026-09-23) with any number of jobs, newest first, four more exact phrases (P3-03 step 15a) | `.cancel(TaskID?)` | P3-03 |
| (none — "how's it going" is `answerQuestion` today, LocalVoiceDecision.swift:129-131) | `.status(TaskID?)` | P3-03 |
| (none) | `.respondPermission(…)` | P3-08 |
| (none — side talk is answered today) | `.notAddressed` | P3-10 (H-audit 2026-09-23) |

Deterministic gate outcomes that are not decisions (garble clarifier, unavailable reply,
empty input) stay as they are and produce an `OutputRequest` of kind `.fixed(turn)`.

### 2.5 TaskBridge

**Isolation.** The decision document draws TaskBridge as an actor. This plan makes it
`@MainActor final class TaskBridge` — isolated to the main global actor — because every
producer and consumer is already main-actor-isolated (`AgentTaskManager`,
`VoiceConversationCoordinator`, `RealtimeAgent`, `PermissionGate`, `AgentActivityStore`),
and a custom actor would add a hop to every effect check plus a mirror for SwiftUI.
"Actor" in the decision doc means "single owner of the ledger"; that property holds. Every
public method is `async` or cheap-synchronous and the API below is written so the
isolation can move to a dedicated actor later without changing call sites.

```swift
enum TaskState: String, Sendable, Codable, CaseIterable {
    case queued, running, delegated, finalizing, needsInput, needsPermission,
         cancelling, completed, failed, cancelled

    var isTerminal: Bool { self == .completed || self == .failed || self == .cancelled }
    var isCancellable: Bool { !isTerminal && self != .cancelling }

    /// The only legal transitions. Anything else is a programming error:
    /// assert in DEBUG, log and ignore in release.
    static let legal: [TaskState: Set<TaskState>] = [
        .queued:          [.running, .cancelled, .failed],
        .running:         [.delegated, .finalizing, .needsInput, .needsPermission, .cancelling, .completed, .failed],
        .delegated:       [.finalizing, .needsInput, .needsPermission, .cancelling, .completed, .failed],
        .finalizing:      [.cancelling, .completed, .failed],
        .needsInput:      [.running, .cancelling, .failed],
        .needsPermission: [.running, .delegated, .cancelling, .failed],
        .cancelling:      [.cancelled, .failed],
    ]
}
```

| State | Meaning | Maps to `AgentTaskStatus` (persisted, Agent/Tasks/AgentTask.swift:3-12) |
|---|---|---|
| queued | accepted, not started | `.queued` |
| running | a local worker is planning or calling tools | `.running` |
| delegated | an ACP backend owns the turn (external process) | `.running` |
| finalizing | the result is being assembled (ACP final synthesis; local: never used) | `.running` |
| needsInput | the worker ended with a question for the person | `.waitingForInput` |
| needsPermission | a `PermissionGate` request owned by this task is pending, or ACP compatibility approval | `.waitingForPermission` / `.waitingForCompatibilityCLI` |
| cancelling | stop requested, not yet confirmed (an in-flight write still completes — ToolLoop:1032-1036) | `.running` |
| completed / failed / cancelled | terminal | same names |

```swift
enum TaskOrigin: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case voice(sessionID: UUID, turn: TurnID)
    case typed
    case remote(ActionOriginContext) // reuse existing transport/identity/chat/message/asleep context
    case scheduled          // routines: never announced into the conversation (AgentTaskManager.swift:65-68)
    case meeting(UUID)
    case selfTest
}

enum TaskDelivery: String, Sendable, Codable {
    case none               // nothing to say (running, or a silent origin)
    case pending            // waiting for a delivery window
    case delivering         // an OutputID is playing it
    case delivered          // first audible frame played (D4)
    case deliveredInContext // the user talked over it; it is in the conversation (D5)
    case consumedByStatus   // a status answer already told them (D7)
    case silent             // scheduled/meeting/typed-without-session: feed and island only
    case failed             // retries exhausted (D6); still visible in the feed
}

struct TaskProgress: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    var stepIndex: Int              // 1-based, from AgentActivityStore.steps(taskID:)
    var stepTitle: String           // consumer title (AgentActivityProjector), never a tool id
    var updatedAt: Date
}

struct TaskRecord: Identifiable, Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    let id: TaskID
    var title: String               // short consumer title (≤ 60 chars), derived from the objective
    var objective: String           // self-contained instruction the worker receives
    var userWords: String           // exactly what the person said
    var origin: TaskOrigin
    var backend: AgentBackendKind
    var state: TaskState
    var revision: Int               // VoiceConversationWork.revision for voice jobs
    var createdAt: Date
    var startedAt: Date?
    var finishedAt: Date?
    var progress: TaskProgress?
    var latestUpdate: String?       // ≤ 1,000 chars, observability only
    var result: String?             // ≤ 1,200 chars (P1-10 cap); full text stays in AgentSession/feed
    var failure: String?
    var question: String?           // needsInput
    var pendingRequestID: String?   // needsPermission
    var delivery: TaskDelivery
    var deliveryAttempts: Int
}

enum TaskReceiptStatus: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case accepted
    case duplicate                  // same normalized objective already spawned for this TurnID
    case refusedApprovalPending     // an approval is pending: the turn must answer it first (qwen)
    case refusedNeedsConfirmation   // a named coding harness is missing; ACPConfirmationGate card raised
    case refusedUnavailable(String) // no worker can run it (plain words)
}

struct TaskReceipt: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    let id: TaskID?
    let status: TaskReceiptStatus
    let title: String
}

struct TaskStatusReport: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    let id: TaskID
    let title: String
    let state: TaskState
    let elapsed: Duration
    let stepTitle: String?
    let stepIndex: Int?
    let resultGist: String?         // first sentence of result, ≤ 160 chars, completed only
    let failureGist: String?
    let question: String?
}

enum TaskEvent: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case accepted(TaskID, title: String, origin: TaskOrigin)
    case stateChanged(TaskID, from: TaskState, to: TaskState)
    case progress(TaskID, step: Int, title: String)     // observability only; never spoken
    case revised(TaskID, revision: Int)
    case needsInput(TaskID, question: String)
    case needsPermission(TaskID, requestID: String)
    case permissionResolved(TaskID, requestID: String, approved: Bool)
    case completed(TaskID, result: String, artifacts: [String])
    case failed(TaskID, reason: String)
    case cancelled(TaskID)
    case deliveryChanged(TaskID, TaskDelivery)
    case deliveryQueued(count: Int)                    // OutputScheduler's pending count, for the reducer
}

@MainActor
final class TaskBridge {
    static let shared = TaskBridge()

    // Intake — returns in milliseconds; never awaits a model, a backend round trip or ASR.
    func spawn(objective: String, userWords: String, origin: TaskOrigin,
               backend: AgentBackendKind? = nil) -> TaskReceipt

    // Queries — no model call, no barrier.
    func record(_ id: TaskID) -> TaskRecord?
    func sessionTasks(_ sessionID: UUID, includeRecentTerminal: Duration = .seconds(600)) -> [TaskRecord]
    func status(_ id: TaskID?, sessionID: UUID?) -> [TaskStatusReport]   // nil id ⇒ running first, then most recent
    func workStatusLines(sessionID: UUID?) -> String                     // for the frontend prompt (replaces Coordinator:667-671)
    var hasActiveSessionWork: Bool { get }                               // replaces Coordinator.hasActiveWork (:40)

    // Control.
    func revise(_ id: TaskID, text: String, turn: TurnID) -> Bool        // false if not running/needsInput
    func cancel(_ id: TaskID?, reason: CancelReason) async -> TaskState  // confirmed: returns .cancelled or .failed; .cancelling if it did not confirm within 2 s
    func respondApproval(requestID: String?, decision: SpokenPermissionDecision,
                         amendment: String?, turn: TurnID) -> ApprovalOutcome   // P3-08

    // The effect gate (§2.5.1).
    func setEffectsHeld(_ held: Bool)                                    // from VoiceSession only
    func mayCommitEffect(_ id: TaskID, revision: Int, risk: AgentRisk) async -> Bool

    // Delivery bookkeeping (OutputScheduler is the only caller).
    func setDelivery(_ ids: [TaskID], _ state: TaskDelivery)

    // Events.
    func subscribe() -> AsyncStream<TaskEvent>                           // one continuation per subscriber
}

enum CancelReason: String, Sendable, Codable { case spoken, stopButton, typed, sessionPolicy }
enum ApprovalOutcome: Sendable, Equatable {
    case approved, denied, deniedAndRevised,
         refusedNotSpeakable(String), noPendingRequest, notSessionOwned, notReady(String)
}
```

`subscribe()` returns a fresh `AsyncStream` per call; keep a
`[UUID: AsyncStream<TaskEvent>.Continuation]` and remove the entry in `onTermination`
(the broadcaster at Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:731-761 is a working pattern).

#### 2.5.1 `mayCommitEffect` — from P0-07 to P3-02

P0-07 introduced the write-time gate so that C1 could be fixed safely: after a failed
frontend turn the barrier is released, planning and reads resume, and a *write* waits for
the next classified epoch. **Before starting P3-02, read the P0-07 section of
`01-PHASE-0-*.md` and run `grep -rn "mayCommitEffect" Sources/NextNotes` to see the
signature it actually shipped with; keep that signature if it differs from the one here
and adapt the steps.** The semantics this phase relies on:

| | P0-07 (Phase 0) | P3-02 (this phase) |
|---|---|---|
| Who calls it | `RealtimeAgent.mayCommitEffect(risk:)` from the voice worker's `isStillValid` closure (ToolLoop:999-1002); it only waits for `risk > .read` | Same closure, for every task in the ledger whose effect goes through `AgentToolExecutor.run` → `ActionOrchestrator.execute` (Agent/ActionRuntime.swift:518) — voice jobs now, local ledger tasks when they gain a session origin |
| Planning rounds | Still wait at ToolLoop:864/915/954 | **Never wait.** The three round-boundary waits are removed |
| Reads (`risk <= .read`) | The gate returns `true` immediately (P0-07 Target 3), but the round-boundary waits at ToolLoop:864/915/954 still hold reads back | Return immediately (only `isCurrent(owner) && revision == work.revision` is checked) and no round wait remains |
| Barrier source | `Coordinator.inputPending` (20 ms poll) | `TaskBridge.setEffectsHeld(_:)`, driven by `VoiceSession` (`unclassified` non-empty). Until P3-06b switches ownership, `Coordinator.inputPending` sets it |
| Barrier release | End of answer generation (Coordinator:497) or failure (P0-07) | As soon as the route is known (`decisionMade`), and on any `responseEnded` |
| Wait mechanism | poll | continuation list resumed by `setEffectsHeld(false)`; no polling |

`mayCommitEffect(id, revision, risk)`:

1. If `risk <= .read` → return `record(id)?.revision == revision && !record.state.isTerminal`.
2. If the task is not session-owned (origin not `.voice`) → same as 1 (typed/ACP effects
   already have their own approval; the voice barrier is about spoken corrections).
3. Else, while `effectsHeld` and the session is open and the task is not cancelled: suspend
   on a continuation (with a `withTaskCancellationHandler` that removes the waiter). The
   time spent here is excluded from every compute budget (AGENTS.md: "Model/read budgets
   exclude waiting for the user's floor or an approval").
4. After the wait: return `record.revision == revision && record.state` is not
   `.cancelling/.cancelled`. A revision that arrived during the wait makes the effect
   planned against the old words return `false`; `ActionOrchestrator.execute` then throws
   `CancellationError` before `fire` (Agent/ActionRuntime.swift:518), the loop's
   `revision != work.revision` branch (ToolLoop:1073-1077) drops the call, and the next
   round re-plans with the correction.
5. Session close releases the wait (AGENTS.md: "Session close/cancellation breaks the
   wait") — `setEffectsHeld(false)` is emitted by reducer row 3.

The gate runs **after** approval and **before** fire, which is where
`ActionOrchestrator.execute` already calls `isStillValid` (Agent/ActionRuntime.swift:514-518,
"Do this check after every permission await so a late approval cannot fire a stale
prepared action"). Do not move it.

#### 2.5.2 What Phase 6 adds to this contract (added 2026-09-25)

Phase 6 (`07-PHASE-6-DURABLE-JOBS.md`) makes the ledger **durable**. It is an **extension**, and
it changes nothing in §2.5. Concretely:

| Added | Where it is specified |
|---|---|
| `TaskState.recovering` — reachable only from `.running`/`.delegated`, and only from the recovery planner at launch | Phase 6 §2.1 |
| `TaskDurability` (heartbeat, attempt, retries, `resumePolicy`, `leaseOwner`, `runtimeClass`, `receiptIDs`, `parentTaskID`) as **optional fields with a hand-written `init(from:)`** | Phase 6 §2.1 |
| `TaskEvent` becomes durable: each event is written to a `task_event` journal **in the same transaction** as the state change | Phase 6 §2.3 |
| A pure `TaskRecoveryPlanner.plan(_:)`, modelled on `MeetingStore.resumeAction` | Phase 6 §2.4 |
| A retry policy keyed on `AgentRisk`, and the rule that a recovered task **never** auto-fires a consequential action — it reads its `ActionReceipt`s first | Phase 6 §2.6 |

Two things Phase 6 explicitly does **not** touch, and a reader of either file should hold both:

- **`TaskBridge` remains the single ledger.** After P3-03b `VoiceConversationCoordinator.jobs` is
  deleted; Phase 6 must not bring it back, and must not add a `TaskBridge`-alike.
- **`mayCommitEffect` still runs after approval and before fire.** A recovered task re-checks
  `revision` before its next effect, and recovery grants no exemption.

`TaskRecord`'s new durability fields follow the `Meeting` rule: a synthesized `init(from:)` does
**not** use a property default as a fallback for a missing key, so without a hand-written
conformance a ledger row written by an older build — or imported from `agent-tasks.json`, or
seeded by a fixture — fails to decode and the task vanishes. `AgentTask` already does this
correctly at `Agent/Tasks/AgentTask.swift:115`; reuse the pattern, do not add a second one.

### 2.6 OutputScheduler and AgentDelivery

```swift
enum AgentDeliveryMode: String, Sendable, Codable {
    case context    // record in the conversation only (AgentSession row); never spoken (≈ Gemini SILENT)
    case respond    // speak at the next delivery window (≈ WHEN_IDLE); results, questions, approvals
    case interrupt  // stop the agent's own current output and speak now (≈ INTERRUPT). Never cuts the
                    // user: it still waits for floor == .free. No producer uses it in Phase 3.
}

enum OutputPriority: Int, Comparable, Sendable, Codable {
    case turnReply = 0, approvalPrompt = 1, taskQuestion = 2, result = 3
    static func < (l: Self, r: Self) -> Bool { l.rawValue < r.rawValue }
}

struct OutputRequest: Sendable {
    let id: OutputID
    let kind: OutputKind
    let mode: AgentDeliveryMode
    let priority: OutputPriority
    let text: AsyncStream<String>      // deltas; a fixed reply yields once and finishes
}

enum DeliveryKind: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    case result, failure, cancelled, question, approval(requestID: String)
}

struct DeliveryItem: Sendable, Equatable, Codable {
    let task: TaskID
    let kind: DeliveryKind
    let title: String
    let facts: String                  // result/failure/question text, already ≤ 1,200 chars (P1-10)
    let enqueuedAt: Date
    var attempts: Int
    var carriedOver: Bool              // true when it is being offered in a later session (D8)
}

/// Returned by beginAnswer; the only way an answer stream reaches TTS.
@MainActor
protocol OutputHandle: AnyObject {
    var id: OutputID { get }
    func append(_ delta: String)       // RealtimeAudioSession.appendSpokenReply under the hood
    func finish()                      // finalizeSpokenReply
    func cancel()
}

@MainActor
final class OutputScheduler {
    static let shared = OutputScheduler()
    func beginAnswer(turn: TurnID) -> OutputHandle              // starts now: it is the reply to the user's turn
    func speakFixed(_ text: String, turn: TurnID) -> OutputID?  // nil when the turn is stale or the text has no spoken form
    func enqueue(_ item: DeliveryItem)                          // results, questions, approval prompts
    func windowOpened()                                         // VoiceSession command openDeliveryWindow
    func pause(_ id: OutputID); func resume(_ id: OutputID)
    func stop(_ id: OutputID, byUser: Bool)
    func receive(_ event: AgentSpeechSynthesizer.PlaybackEvent) // bound to the current OutputID
    func sessionOpened(); func sessionClosed()
    var pendingCount: Int { get }
}

enum AgentDeliveryTiming {
    static let quietWindow: Duration = .milliseconds(350)   // decision doc §3.5 #2 (qwen config.mjs:438, measured there)
    static let batchWindow: Duration = .milliseconds(120)   // qwen announcement-manager.mjs constructor
    static let maxBatchItems = 8                            // qwen
    static let maxBatchCharacters = 2_400                   // estimate: qwen uses 6,000 for cloud models; the on-device
                                                            // frontend's window (read at runtime, P1-10a; 8,192 measured on the
                                                            // dev Mac, 4,096 the old assumption) is shared with instructions
    static let phraseBudget: Duration = .milliseconds(1_500) // C §5.4 step 3
    static let phraseMaxTokens = 120
    static let retryBase: Duration = .seconds(1)            // qwen retryBaseMs
    static let retryMax: Duration = .seconds(10)            // qwen retryMaxMs
    static let maxRetries = 8                               // qwen maxRetryAttempts
    static let acknowledgementTimeout: Duration = .seconds(120) // qwen; never fires while delivery is blocked
    static let carryOverMaxAge: Duration = .seconds(12 * 3600)  // estimate; older results stay in the feed only
}
```

**Delivery rules.** Each rule is testable and is asserted by `--selftest-voice-delivery`
after P3-04.

- **D1 — Window.** A delivery may start only when `VoiceSessionState.deliveryWindowOpen(now)`
  is true: the user does not hold the floor, no committed turn is still waiting for its
  reply to finish (`turnPending == nil`), no response is generating, no audio is queued or
  playing, no turn is unclassified, **and** ≥ 350 ms have passed since the later of the
  user's last speech and the end of the last output. (qwen:
  `server/src/voice/announcement/announcement-window.mjs` `isBlocked()`.)
- **D2 — Batching.** An item becomes eligible 120 ms after it is enqueued. Items that became
  eligible while blocked form one batch when the window opens, oldest first, at most 8
  items and 2,400 characters of facts; the rest wait for the next window.
- **D3 — Phrasing.** A batch is phrased by `FrontendResponder.phrase(batch)` under the
  result-only rules (P3-04 step 5 / P3-05 prompt set): "not a new request", open with the
  result, cover every item, no filler, no ids/paths/URLs, no tools, never call unfinished
  work finished. Budget 1.5 s and 120 tokens; on timeout, error or unavailability the
  deterministic `DeliveryFallbackRenderer` is spoken instead. Approval prompts and status
  answers are never model-phrased (§P3-08, §P3-03).
- **D4 — Acknowledged on first audible frame.** A batch is `delivered` when its output's
  first clause reports `.startAcknowledged` (Agent/Speech/AgentSpeechSynthesizer.swift:317-322,
  `didBeginAudio`; after P2-06 that is the first nonzero rendered sample). Not on enqueue,
  not on generation, not on the last clause (decision doc §3.5 #4).
- **D5 — User barge-in ⇒ delivered in context.** If the user interrupts a delivery
  (`stopOutput(o, byUser: true)`, from a committed turn) at any point, every item of the
  batch becomes `deliveredInContext`. It is not replayed: the result is already in the
  conversation as an `AgentSession` row with `contextKind == backgroundTask` (written at
  completion by `AgentTaskManager.announce`, AgentTaskManager.swift:311-316), so the
  frontend can use it and the user can ask. (Decision doc §3.5 #3; qwen
  `realtime-presentation-runtime.mjs` `cancelPlayback`, lines 606-619.) A *provisional*
  interruption only pauses; if it is discarded, playback resumes.
- **D6 — Other cancellation ⇒ bounded retry.** If a delivery stops for any other reason
  (session closed mid-delivery, engine error, a `turnReply` preempting a not-yet-heard
  delivery), items go back to `pending`, `attempts += 1`, and become eligible again after
  `min(retryBase × 2^(attempts−1), retryMax)`. The retry is **re-phrased** from the facts,
  never a replayed fragment. After 8 attempts the item becomes `failed` (still in the feed
  and on the island).
- **D7 — A status question consumes a pending delivery.** When `TaskBridge.status` answers a
  question about a task whose delivery is `pending`, the answer includes its result gist and
  the delivery becomes `consumedByStatus` (qwen `agent-task-runtime.mjs`
  `consumesTaskNotification`, lines 897-934). It is never said twice.
- **D8 — Carry-over.** Pending items survive `sessionClosed` (persisted in the ledger via the
  `AgentTask.delivery` field). On the next `sessionOpened`, after the first quiet window,
  they are offered first, phrased with the carried-over framing ("While you were away, …").
  Items older than 12 h are marked `silent` instead.
- **D9 — Modes.** Results, failures, questions and approval prompts use `respond`. Progress
  uses `context` only: in Phase 3 no progress line is ever spoken (the "≤ 1 line per 60 s"
  narration from qwen's `progress-announcement-manager.mjs` is deferred; the constant is not
  added). Tasks with origin `.scheduled` or `.meeting`, and typed tasks with no open
  session, use `context` (feed + island only), as today.
- **D10 — Priority.** When a window opens: approval prompts, then task questions, then
  results. A `turnReply` (answer or fixed reply to the user's turn) never waits for a
  window; it supersedes any delivery that has not yet been heard (that delivery goes back
  to `pending` without counting an attempt).
- **D11 — Only the scheduler starts audio.** After P3-04b, `RealtimeAudioSession.speak`,
  `beginSpokenReply`, `appendSpokenReply` and `finalizeSpokenReply` require an
  `OutputToken` whose initializer is `fileprivate` to `OutputScheduler.swift`. No other
  file can start audio; the compiler enforces it.
- **D12 — Binding by OutputID.** `VoicePlaybackDelivery` binds a conversation row to the
  `OutputID` that played it, not to `RealtimeAgent.shared.currentGeneration`
  (Agent/VoicePlaybackDelivery.swift:28-29). An announcement and the next answer can no
  longer share a ledger (C race 12).
- **D13 — After the session: a heads-up on the island, and still pending** (H-audit 2026-09-23,
  H3 #20). A `respond`-mode item that becomes eligible while no voice session is open — it
  finished after "Done" or the idle close, or it was being delivered and was not yet heard when
  the session closed — is shown at once as an island card with the deterministic
  `DeliveryFallbackRenderer` text (no model call: no session means no frontend turn to phrase
  it), and its delivery stays `pending` so D8 still offers it at the next session. The session's
  own closing card ("Going quiet." / "Stopped." / the last reply, Capture:389-392) does not
  suppress it; a visible approval card does (the item is then only in the conversation and D8).
  One card per batch (D2).

#### 2.6.1 Durable results (added 2026-09-25)

Phase 6 adds persistence **behind** this scheduler. It adds no channel, no priority and no new
mode. The rules above already cover the case the multi-agent roadmap called "no backend
independently decides to speak" — that is **D11**, enforced by the compiler, and this phase must
not weaken it.

What Phase 6 changes:

- **D8 already covers the case.** It says pending items survive `sessionClosed`, "persisted in the
  ledger via the `AgentTask.delivery` field". Phase 6 makes that field durable across a *process*
  restart rather than a session close, so a result that finished while the app was quitting is
  offered at the next `sessionOpened` instead of being lost.
- **A recovered task's result enters through `enqueue`,** with mode `respond` and the same
  `OutputPriority.result`. It does not get a faster path, and a recovered result never interrupts
  a turn (D1 still governs the window).
- **`TaskBridge.setDelivery` stays the only writer of `TaskDelivery`.** Phase 6 persists what that
  method sets; it does not set it.
- **D13 still governs a result with no session open** — island card now, delivery still pending.
  A durable job that nobody is listening to behaves exactly as a live one does.

One thing to hold onto: a job that was `.running` at quit and recovers to `.completed` while no
voice session is open produces **an island card and a pending delivery, not a notification and not
silence**. D13 was written for "finished after 'Done'"; Phase 6 widens it to "finished while the
app was not running", and the wording of the island card should not change.

### 2.7 What each new type replaces

From the decision document §4, restricted to this phase:

| New | Replaces or absorbs | Task |
|---|---|---|
| `TurnID`, `OutputID`, `TaskID` | the ten epoch counters (table in §2.1) | P3-06 |
| `VoiceEvent`, `Interruption` (`provisional`, `committed`, `explicitCancel`) | direct calls between `AgentCaptureController`, the coordinator, `RealtimeAgent.shared` and `RealtimeAudioSession` | P3-06 |
| `VoiceSession` (reducer) | the cross-object flags `inputPending`, `voiceInputActive`, `isThinking` (voice), `heardSpeech` as a floor signal | P3-06, P3-09 |
| `InputProcessor` (role) | `AgentCaptureController`'s outgoing calls (its VAD/EOU/echo logic is kept intact) | P3-06b |
| `FrontendResponder`, `FrontendDecision` | `LocalVoiceFrontend` + `Coordinator.respond`; `LocalVoiceRoute` maps one-to-one, plus `status` and `respondPermission` | P3-05 (+P3-03, P3-08) |
| `TaskBridge`, `TaskEvent` | `VoiceConversationCoordinator.jobs` + `AgentTaskManager`'s voice mirror | P3-03 |
| `OutputScheduler` (`AgentDelivery` modes), `OutputRequest` | `VoiceAnnouncementQueue`, the direct `RealtimeAudioSession.speak` callers, `VoicePlaybackDelivery`'s generation binding | P3-04 |
| `LocalToolLoopWorker` | `RealtimeAgent(voiceWorker:)` + `runPlannedToolLoop`, now with its own `llama_context` | P3-07c |
| `mayCommitEffect` | `waitForVoiceInput()` at every round/call boundary | P0-07, P3-02 |

### 2.8 Invariants that must survive (AGENTS.md)

These are the rules in AGENTS.md's closing sections. Every task below lists the ones it can
break in its "Do not" section. The right-hand column is the self-test that guards each.

| # | Invariant (AGENTS.md) | How the new design keeps it | Guarded by |
|---|---|---|---|
| I1 | **Output and work have separate lifetimes.** Yielding speech never invalidates work. | `Interruption.committed` stops output and cancels the *response*; only `explicitCancel` reaches `TaskBridge.cancel` (rows 10, 29) | `--selftest-concurrent-voice`, `--selftest-voice-work-lifecycle`, `--selftest-voice-session-reducer` |
| I2 | **Provisional input pauses; a committed turn stops.** Provisional novel recognition pauses the current reply reversibly; only a committed user turn stops output. | rows 6–8 pause/resume; rows 10–11 stop | `--selftest-voice-turns` (`reversibleListeningSelfTestFailures`), `--selftest-voice-barge`, `--selftest-duplex` |
| I3 | **Only explicit cancellation invalidates work.** | same as I1; revisions bump `revision` but never cancel | `--selftest-concurrent-voice`, `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` |
| I4 | **Effects wait until the latest input is classified.** A correction must not allow an old effect. | `mayCommitEffect` waits while `unclassified` is non-empty and re-checks `revision` (§2.5.1) | `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` A2, `--selftest-concurrent-voice` |
| I5 | **Deterministic gates may only add, never subtract.** A gate that suppresses the model matches exact signatures only and fires once per distinct utterance per session; the repeat escalates. | The new status gate (P3-03) and approval gate (P3-08) are *routing* gates: they answer from facts and only fire when the ledger has something to answer about; exact signature lists; a repeat within 5 s escalates to the model. The positive corpus in `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` must still reach the model | `--selftest-voice-turn-routing`, `--selftest-voice-turns` |
| I6 | **Model/read budgets exclude waiting for the floor or an approval.** | `mayCommitEffect` waits are outside every budget; approvals are outside (writes are never bounded, ToolLoop:1032-1036) | `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` |
| I7 | **The on-device model's native context is reserved before its scheduler ticket** (lock order native → lane; FM takes only the lane). | P3-07b keeps the order per context slot; a job never holds two slots | `--selftest-voice-scheduling`, `--selftest-residency` |
| I8 | **A self-test never writes the user's real stores** (RunLog, tasks, audit log, memory, conversation). | Every new self-test uses `SelfTest.isRunning` stores and the P0-11 seams; `AgentTaskManager.persist` is already guarded (AgentTaskManager.swift:291-294) | each test's own setup |
| I9 | **A self-test fails when the thing it names did not happen**; a missing model/grant prints `*_ABSENT`, never `*_OK`. | stated per test | — |
| I10 | **The agent searches without asking and never sends without asking; send-risk shows the full message first.** | Spoken approvals (P3-08) never approve `.purchase`/`.destructive`/`.privileged`, and approve `.send` only after the full message was spoken | `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` approval block |
| I11 | **The Agent's name is never in a prompt.** | New prompt text (P3-04, P3-05) describes a role, never a name | `--selftest-persona` |

## 3. Migration order

The strangler pattern: every new component first runs **behind** the old call sites (the
old path still does the work), then the call sites **switch** to it, then the old code is
**deleted**. The app must build, install and pass `make acceptance TIER=core` after every
step. Do the steps in this order — it is not the numeric order of the task ids.

| Step | Task | Behind / switch / delete | What the user notices | Self-tests that must be green after the step |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **P3-01** tests describe production | delete `appendVoiceFollowUp`; port tests | nothing | `--selftest-voice-work-lifecycle`, `--selftest-voice-conversation`, `--selftest-concurrent-voice`, `--selftest-toolloop-production`, `--selftest-local-model-stream` |
| 2 | **P3-02** barrier at write time | switch: round waits removed; release at route | talking no longer pauses work | + new `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` |
| 3 | **P3-06a** contracts + reducer in shadow mode | behind: VoiceSession receives events from today's call sites and only *observes*; divergences are logged | nothing | + new `--selftest-voice-session-reducer` |
| 4 | **P3-03a** TaskBridge ledger behind the coordinator | behind: coordinator's `submit/cancel` delegate to TaskBridge; `jobs` becomes a computed view; status gate + route | "how's it going?" answered with the step | `--selftest-tasks`, `--selftest-voice-turn-routing`, `--selftest-concurrent-voice` |
| 5 | **P3-03b** one ledger incl. ACP; delete `jobs` | switch + delete | coding tasks by voice go to ACP | + `--selftest-acp`, `--selftest-acp-confirm` |
| 6 | **P3-09** island owned by VoiceSession | switch: worker writes rejected while a session is open | the island stops flipping to "working" | `--selftest-island`, `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` (island assertion) |
| 7 | **P3-04a** OutputScheduler for deliveries | switch + delete `VoiceAnnouncementQueue` | results are phrased, batched, never lost | rewritten `--selftest-voice-delivery`, `--selftest-duplex`, `--selftest-voice-barge` |
| 8 | **P3-04b** all audio through OutputScheduler | switch: `OutputToken` required | no clipped/cross-wired speech | `--selftest-playback-ledger`, `--selftest-voice-delivery`, `--selftest-tts-pocket-session` |
| 9 | **P3-05** receipt-first frontend contract | switch coordinator to `FrontendDecision`; new prompts | one-clause acknowledgements, no filler | `--selftest-voice-turn-routing`, `--selftest-persona`, `--selftest-voice-frontend`, P1-01 live eval |
| 9b | **P3-10** not-addressed route + side-talk close (H-audit 2026-09-23) | switch: one more `FrontendDecision` case and route rule; close check in `tick` | side conversation is not answered; the session closes after two such turns | `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` (not-addressed block), new `--selftest-voice-turn-routing-live` (3 runs; EXPERIMENTAL), `--selftest-persona`, `--selftest-voice-frontend` |
| 9c | **P3-11** a typed message during a run is a turn (OpenMuse 2026-09-27) | switch: the typed pane stops calling `interrupt()`; revise / spawn / typed cancel through TaskBridge | typing a correction mid-run adjusts the work instead of restarting it | `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` (typed block), `--selftest-agent-panes` |
| 10 | **P3-08** spoken approvals | behind a gate that only fires with a session-owned pending request | "yes" approves by voice | `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` approval block, `--selftest-tool-review` |
| 11 | **P3-06b** reducer owns the state | switch ownership; delete flags, polls and counters | nothing (it is the proof the rest is coherent) | every voice flag above |
| 12 | **P3-07a** capacity lanes + overlap measurement | behind a constant that stays `false` until measured | nothing, or faster turns during work | `--selftest-scheduler`, new `--selftest-voice-overlap`, `--selftest-voice-scheduling` |
| 13 | **P3-07b** second llama context | switch voice work to its own slot | voice work no longer waits behind notes | `--selftest-residency`, `--selftest-llm-metal`, `--selftest-voice-overlap` |
| 14 | **P3-07c** LocalToolLoopWorker | switch the voice worker type | smaller worker prompts | `--selftest-voice-duplex-work`, `--selftest-toolloop` |
| 15 | P3-07d (optional) fallback conversational lane | behind a constant, default off | conversation without Apple Intelligence | `--selftest-voice-conversation` |

**Why P3-10 is step 9b** (H-audit 2026-09-23): it adds one case to the `FrontendDecision` that
P3-05 introduces, and its close relies on P3-04a's carry-over (D8/D13) so a result finishing
after "Going quiet." is never lost — so it cannot go earlier. It goes before P3-08 so the
spoken-approval block is graded with the not-addressed route already in the route set (a
sentence the model judged not meant for it must never reach the approval gate as a "yes"), and
before P3-06b, which moves its counter into the reducer. It uses the step number 9b so the
existing step numbers in task headers stay valid.

**Why P3-11 is step 9c** (OpenMuse 2026-09-27): it needs P3-05's route set to tell a revision
from a new request, and typed tasks in the one ledger (P3-03b). It goes before P3-08 so a typed
"yes" during a pending approval is already a turn rather than an interrupt when spoken approvals
arrive.

Phase exit: every flag in the right-hand column reports `*_OK` (or `*_ABSENT` where a model
is genuinely missing), `make acceptance TIER=core` has not regressed, the new deterministic
flags (`--selftest-voice-duplex-work`, `--selftest-voice-session-reducer`) are added to the
CORE tier in `Scripts/acceptance.sh`, and AGENTS.md's closing sections are updated with the
new rules (listed in each task's "Done when"). (H-audit 2026-09-23) `--selftest-voice-turn-routing-live`
(P3-10) stays EXPERIMENTAL because it runs the live on-device model, but the phase does not
exit until three runs of it are recorded in STATUS.md with side talk ≥ 8/10 and positive
suppressed 0/10 each (an `_ABSENT` run blocks exit rather than passing it).

**Shared files.** `NextNotesApp.swift` (`runRequestedSelfTest`) is touched by several tasks:
add each new flag as one contiguous `if arguments.contains("--selftest-…") { … }` block next
to the existing voice flags (around NextNotesApp.swift:1415-1480) and nothing else in that
file. `AGENTS.md`'s flag list and `Scripts/acceptance.sh` get one line per new flag.

**Verification commands used by every task** (repeated in each task's Verify):

```bash
make build                      # never bare `swift build`; on "input file was modified during the build", wait and retry
make install OPEN=0             # never omit OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-<name>   # foreground, one at a time; never bare-invoke the binary
make acceptance TIER=core       # the phase gate: must not regress
```

Only TCC-sensitive tests use `Scripts/run-selftest.sh --via-open …`; none of this phase's
tests need the microphone, so none use it. Never loop `--via-open`.

---

## 4. Tasks

Tasks are listed by id. The **migration step** in each header is the order to execute them
(§3). Do not start a task until every task in its "Depends on" is ticked in STATUS.md.

### P3-01 — Step 0: tests describe production

*Migration step 1.*

**Finding(s):** C1 (guard), decision doc §6 "Tests that grade a path production never takes", C report Q5 second bullet  **Effort:** 1–1.5 days  **Depends on:** P0-07, P0-11  **Unblocks:** every other P3 task

**Why (what the user experiences today):** Nothing directly — this task exists because the
voice lifecycle tests are green while production does something else, which is how the C1
stall ("after one failed voice reply, background work silently stops and the mic stays
open") shipped next to `VOICE_WORK_LIFECYCLE_OK`. `--selftest-voice-work-lifecycle` and the
first half of `--selftest-voice-conversation` install `RealtimeAgent.localModelProviderForTesting`,
which makes `RealtimeAgent.handle` skip the coordinator and run the legacy voice branch plus
`appendVoiceFollowUp` — a function that returns `false` in production. Every later task in
this phase changes the coordinator path; without tests on that path, none of them can be
proven.

**Files you will change:**
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceWorkLifecycleSelfTest.swift` (rewrite onto the coordinator path)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationSelfTest.swift` (port the `for afterRead in [false, true]` block)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgentToolLoopSelfTest.swift` (the two `source: .voice` cases)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgentLocalModelSelfTest.swift` (the `source: .voice` cases)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift` (one test seam: `workerProviderForTesting`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift` (delete `appendVoiceFollowUp`; voice always routes to the coordinator; legacy-path tripwire)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift` (delete the `appendVoiceFollowUp` call)
- `AGENTS.md` (one sentence under the voice self-test notes)

**Read for context (do not change):**
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest.swift` — the working pattern: `streamForTesting` + `workerForTesting` + `agent.handle(text, source: .voice)`.
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationSelfTest.swift` lines 130-214 — `VoiceConversationProbeState`/`Provider` (reused as a worker provider).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgentToolLoopSelfTest.swift` — `ToolLoopTestProvider`/`ToolLoopTestState`.
- `roadmap/done/agent-analysis-2026-09-23/C-concurrency.md` §3 Q5 and §6 Step 0.

**Current behaviour:**
- `RealtimeAgent.handle` (Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift:207-211) routes voice to the coordinator
  only when no provider seam is installed:
  ```swift
  if source == .voice, localModelProviderForTesting == nil,
     !SelfTest.isRunning || VoiceConversationCoordinator.shared.streamForTesting != nil
          || CommandLine.arguments.contains("--selftest-voice-pipeline") {
      return await VoiceConversationCoordinator.shared.handle(utterance)
  }
  ```
  Otherwise it runs the legacy `.localModel`/`.toolLoop` branches (RealtimeAgent.swift:284-359),
  which production voice never reaches.
- `RealtimeAgent.appendVoiceFollowUp` (RealtimeAgent.swift:77-97) begins
  `guard localModelProviderForTesting != nil, isThinking, let voiceWork else { return false }`
  — unreachable in production. Its only caller is `emitTurn` (Capture:1338-1341).
- Tests grading the legacy path:

  | Flag | File:line | Seam | What it grades |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `--selftest-voice-work-lifecycle` | VoiceWorkLifecycleSelfTest.swift:15, :29, :52 | `agent.localModelProviderForTesting` | held floor, explicit cancel — on the legacy branch |
  | `--selftest-voice-conversation` (first block) | VoiceConversationSelfTest.swift:40-78 | same | correction keeps work identity and retained reads — via `appendVoiceFollowUp` |
  | `--selftest-toolloop-production` (voice cases) | RealtimeAgentToolLoopSelfTest.swift:604-633 | same | voice summary of a listing; streamed first clause; interrupt |
  | `--selftest-local-model-stream` (voice cases) | RealtimeAgentLocalModelSelfTest.swift:50-97 | same | "ask the local model …" by voice |
- `ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest.swift:73` asserts `conversation.inputPending` stays true after an
  unparseable reply ("unclassified input released pending effects"). After P0-07 that is the
  pre-fix contract; P0-07 should already have updated it — check.

**Target behaviour:**
1. `handle(_, source: .voice)` always goes to `VoiceConversationCoordinator.handle`. Under the
   self-test harness, reaching it with no `streamForTesting` and outside the real-model voice
   flags prints `VOICE_LEGACY_PATH_REACHED: <utterance prefix>` and sets `SelfTest.failed`.
   Audit the real-model flag list against current app registrations, including
   the already-present `--selftest-voice-latency`; moving this work earlier must
   not reject that production-path measurement as an unconfigured fixture.
2. `appendVoiceFollowUp` and its call site no longer exist.
3. The four flags above grade the coordinator path and still print their `*_OK` markers.
4. With the C1 bug reintroduced (P0-07's failure-path epoch resolution removed),
   `--selftest-voice-work-lifecycle` prints `VOICE_WORK_LIFECYCLE_FAILED`.

**Steps:**
1. **Worker provider seam.** In `VoiceConversationCoordinator` add, next to `workerForTesting` (Coordinator:36):
   ```swift
   /// Runs the real planned tool loop with a scripted provider (no model weights).
   var workerProviderForTesting: (any LLMProvider)?
   ```
   In `submit(_:)` (Coordinator:688-708), after `let worker = RealtimeAgent(voiceWorker: work)`:
   `if let provider = workerProviderForTesting { worker.localModelProviderForTesting = provider }`.
   Clear it in `resetForTesting()` (:720-729). This keeps `runVoiceObjective` →
   `runPlannedToolLoop` real: `runPlannedToolLoop(_:speech:voice:)` already prefers the
   instance's `localModelProviderForTesting` (ToolLoop:772-773).
2. **Delete the dead follow-up path.** Remove `appendVoiceFollowUp` (RealtimeAgent.swift:76-97)
   and the block `if continueSession, turnHandlerForTesting == nil, RealtimeAgent.shared.appendVoiceFollowUp(text) { return }`
   (Capture:1338-1341). `grep -rn appendVoiceFollowUp Sources/` must return nothing.
3. **Route voice unconditionally.** Replace the condition at RealtimeAgent.swift:208-211 with:
   ```swift
   if source == .voice {
       if SelfTest.isRunning, VoiceConversationCoordinator.shared.streamForTesting == nil,
          !VoiceRealModelSelfTests.isRunning {
           // A voice self-test without a frontend seam used to fall through to the legacy
           // branch below, which production never takes. Fail loudly instead.
           print("VOICE_LEGACY_PATH_REACHED: \(String(utterance.prefix(60)))")
           SelfTest.failed = true
           return AgentTurn(reply: "", delegated: false)
       }
       return await VoiceConversationCoordinator.shared.handle(utterance)
   ```

   and add, in the same file:
   ```swift
   /// Self-test flags whose subject is the real on-device voice model. They may reach the
   /// production frontend without a stream seam.
   enum VoiceRealModelSelfTests {
       static let flags = ["--selftest-voice-pipeline", "--selftest-voice-latency", "--selftest-voice-frontend",
                           "--selftest-voice-local", "--selftest-voice-capabilities-live"]
   ```
