   Leave the legacy `.localModel`/`.toolLoop` code in `handle` — the typed path still uses it.
   Do not delete `voiceWork`-related code in those branches in this task (P3-06b removes what
   becomes unreachable once the reducer owns the state).
4. **Rewrite `VoiceWorkLifecycleSelfTest.run()`** on the coordinator path (pattern:
   `ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest`). Use `RecordingSpeechBacking`, `AgentSession.shared.clear()`
   (self-test sessions do not write disk), `conversation.resetForTesting()` first and last.
   Script `conversation.streamForTesting` per latest message suffix. Four scenarios, each
   with its own failure string:
   - **L1 work survives a side question.** "Check the active app and its running sessions."
     → `<use_tools/>`; worker = `workerProviderForTesting` with the existing
     `VoiceWorkLifecycleProvider`, parked before its first answer. Then
     `speak("What is a haiku?")` → `<answer/>A haiku is a short poem.` Assert: reply is the
     haiku sentence; `conversation.jobs.first?.status == "running"`;
     `jobs.first?.work.revision == 0`; the provider is still parked (not cancelled).
     Failure: `"side question revised, cancelled or waited for the worker"`.
   - **L2 explicit cancel.** With exactly one running job parked, `speak("Cancel that")`.
     Assert: reply `"I stopped that task."`; job status `"cancelled"`; after releasing the
     provider and waiting 250 ms, `recorder.spoken` does not contain the provider's answer and
     `AgentTaskManager.shared.task(id:)?.status == .cancelled`. Failure:
     `"cancelled producer spoke or finished"`.
   - **L3 C1 — a failed turn does not freeze work.** Start a job whose scripted worker
     provider records the wall-clock time each round starts (add a `roundStarts: [Date]`
     array to the provider state) and parks at the start of round 2 until released.
     Release it, then immediately `speak("Unclear instruction.")` with a `streamForTesting`
     that never yields and `conversation.responseDeadlineForTesting = .milliseconds(200)`.
     Record `failedAt = Date()` when `handle` returns. Assert round 2 started within 200 ms
     of `failedAt` (or before it) and the job finishes. Failure:
     `"failed turn froze background work (round started \(delay) s after the failure)"`.
   - **L4 correction keeps identity.** See step 5; do not duplicate it here.
   Print `VOICE_WORK_LIFECYCLE_WRONG: <failure>` per failure and the final
   `VOICE_WORK_LIFECYCLE_OK` / `VOICE_WORK_LIFECYCLE_FAILED` (unchanged markers).
5. **Port `VoiceConversationSelfTest` lines 38-78** (the `for afterRead in [false, true]`
   block). Keep capture-driven input (`capture.simulateSpeech`, `simulateSilence`,
   `considerEndpoint`) — that is production. Replace
   `agent.localModelProviderForTesting = VoiceConversationProbeProvider(state:)` with
   `conversation.workerProviderForTesting = VoiceConversationProbeProvider(state:)` and a
   `streamForTesting` that answers "Check the active app and its running sessions." with
   `<use_tools/>` and "No, Claude Code. C L A U D E. Check Claude instead." with
   `<revise id="1"/>`. Assertion mapping (old → new):

   | Old check | New check |
   |---|---|
   | "producer never reached the suspended boundary" | unchanged (`await state.parked`) |
   | "starting speech cancelled work" (`agent.isThinking && agent.voiceWork?.id == workID`) | after `agent.userSpeechStarted()`: `jobs.first?.status == "running"` and the job id is unchanged |
   | "starting speech failed to stop playback" | unchanged (`AgentSpeechSynthesizer.shared.didStop`) |
   | "a completed response spoke over unfinished input" | unchanged (`recorder.spoken.isEmpty` while the floor is held) |
   | "unfinished input completed the work" | **replace** with "speech onset revised or cancelled the job": revision still 0 before the correction commits |
   | "follow-up was not committed" / "follow-up replaced the work item" | `considerEndpoint()` true; `jobs.count == 1`; `jobs.first?.work.revision == 1` |
   | "original objective or correction was lost" | unchanged (`state.sawAmendedObjective`) |
   | "stale plan executed or correction was lost" (`agent.lastReply == …`) | wait for the job to finish (poll ≤ 3 s); `jobs.first?.result == "I retained the request and applied your correction."` |
   | afterRead: retained result / read once | unchanged |

   The announcement block (lines 81-93) stays until P3-04a rewrites it.
6. **`RealtimeAgentToolLoopSelfTest` voice cases** (lines 604-633). Keep the typed-path
   checks above them. Rewrite the two voice scenarios on the coordinator path:
   - *Listing result:* `streamForTesting` routes "tell me which app is frontmost" to

     wait for the job to finish; then `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.shared.flush(userHasFloor: false)`.
     Assert: the job result contains `/private/`; `recorder.spoken` is non-empty and no
     spoken string contains `/private/`. Drop the `rounds == 3` check if the worker path
     does not use the voice-summary round (print the observed count as a diagnostic).
   - *Streamed first clause:* `streamForTesting` yields `<answer/>First answer. ` at once
     and `Second answer.` after 400 ms. At 100 ms assert `recorder.spoken == ["First answer."]`;
     then `agent.userSpeechStarted()`; after the stream ends assert `"Second answer."` was not

7. **`RealtimeAgentLocalModelSelfTest`** (lines 50-97): change the `source: .voice` calls to
   `source: .text` (they grade the typed "ask the local model" branch, which is production
   for typed turns) and add one case: with `streamForTesting` installed and counting calls,

8. Run every voice flag listed under Verify. Any `VOICE_LEGACY_PATH_REACHED` line names a

9. **AGENTS.md**: under the self-test notes add: "Voice self-tests drive the coordinator
   (`streamForTesting`, `workerForTesting`, `workerProviderForTesting`). A voice turn that
   reaches `RealtimeAgent.handle` under the harness without a stream seam prints
   `VOICE_LEGACY_PATH_REACHED` and fails."

**Do not:**
- Do not delete the typed `.localModel`/`.toolLoop` branches of `RealtimeAgent.handle`; typed turns use them.
- Do not call `RunLog.record`, persist `AgentSession`, write `agent-tasks.json`, the audit log or memory from any test (AGENTS.md; use `SelfTest.isRunning` stores and the P0-11 seams). `AgentTaskManager.persist` is already guarded (AgentTaskManager.swift:291-294) — keep it that way.
- Do not weaken an assertion to make a port pass; if production behaves differently from the legacy path, the new assertion describes production and the difference is written in the test as a comment.
- Do not make a self-test pass when the thing it names did not happen: every scenario must fail if its producer never ran (check "parked" / "stream called" before asserting outcomes).
- Do not install `localModelProviderForTesting` on `RealtimeAgent.shared` in any voice test again.

**Success threshold:** all nine flags print `*_OK`; `grep -rn "appendVoiceFollowUp" Sources/`
is empty; voice reply fixtures live on the coordinator frontend/worker-provider seams;
per-worker provider injection remains legitimate, and a shared typed override is
used solely as an adversarial assertion that it cannot reroute voice (never to
supply a voice reply); the C1-reintroduced run is red; `make acceptance TIER=core` has no new FAIL.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: a real-model voice flag not in `VoiceRealModelSelfTests.flags`
now fails with `VOICE_LEGACY_PATH_REACHED` — add it (step 8). Risk: ported timing
assertions flake on a loaded machine — use polling with a 3 s ceiling, never fixed sleeps
for completion, and keep the 200 ms bound only for L3 (it is the C1 contract). Rollback:
revert the four test files and the three source edits; nothing persistent changes.

### P3-02 — C2 full: the input barrier applies only when an effect commits

**Completed foundation, 2026-10-01: `7e3aba1`.** The actual old producer fails
on blocked planning/reads and stale correction disposition. Three final installed
duplex runs and related voice/tool/approval regressions pass; removing the actual
post-wait revision check fails on obsolete action fire. The repaired existing
capture fixture retains its original utterances and uses real approval/validity,
replacing only the backing effect. [Report](../../../Tests/Reports/voice-production/2026-10-01-p3-02.md).
Core remains 16/18 with wake/dictation failures unresolved. No model, phase or
IM-16 readiness promotion. The implementation recipe below is retained as its contract.

*Migration step 2.*

**Finding(s):** C2 (planning half; P0-07 did the write half), C report §4 ranks 3 and 4  **Effort:** 1–2 days  **Depends on:** P3-01, P0-07  **Unblocks:** P3-03, P3-06a

**Why (what the user experiences today):** "Talking pauses background work; 'how's it
going?' slows it." Every planner round and every tool call of a background job first waits
until the frontend has classified the user's latest input, and the frontend releases that
barrier only after it has generated its whole answer (Coordinator:497). A chatty session
therefore starves the job: each question costs the job the full answer time (route 0.66–1.31 s
plus answer 0.85–1.17 s measured, A-latency Hop 6) at every round boundary, and a 3–4 round
plan (each round 2.5–5.2 s of prefill, B report) is paused several times.

**Files you will change:**
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent+ToolLoop.swift` (remove round-boundary waits; `isStillValid` → gate by risk)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift` (release at route; barrier via continuation, not a 20 ms poll)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Tools/AgentToolExecutor.swift` (test-only fire override, only if P0-07 did not add one)
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/VoiceDuplexWorkSelfTest.swift`
- `Sources/NextNotes/NextNotesApp.swift` (register `--selftest-voice-duplex-work`)
- `Scripts/acceptance.sh`, `AGENTS.md` (flag list + one rule), `README.md` (tree: new `Agent/Duplex/` folder)

**Read for context (do not change):**
- §2.5.1 of this file (the gate's semantics).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/ActionRuntime.swift:395-540` — `ActionOrchestrator.execute`; the `isStillValid` check is at :518, after approval and before `fire`.
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest.swift` (P0-07's barrier assertions).
- The P0-07 section in `01-PHASE-0-*.md` and `grep -rn mayCommitEffect Sources/NextNotes`.

**Current behaviour:**
- `runPlannedToolLoop` (private overload, ToolLoop:790) waits for input resolution three
  times per round, independent of what the round does:
  ```swift
  while rounds < maxRounds {
      await waitForVoiceInput()                        // ToolLoop:864
      let revision = work?.revision ?? 0
      …
      remainingBudget -= completionBegan.duration(to: clock.now)
      await waitForVoiceInput()                        // ToolLoop:915
      guard isCurrent(owner) else { return "I stopped the tool plan." }
      if revision != (work?.revision ?? 0) { continue }
      …
      for call in parsedCalls {
          await waitForVoiceInput()                    // ToolLoop:954
  ```
  and once more inside the executor's validity check, for reads and writes alike:
  ```swift
  isStillValid: {
      await self.waitForVoiceInput()                   // ToolLoop:1000 (P0-07 may have changed this)
      return self.isCurrent(owner) && revision == (work?.revision ?? 0)
  }
  ```
- For a worker, `waitForVoiceInput()` (RealtimeAgent.swift:99-108) calls
  `VoiceConversationCoordinator.waitForInputResolution()` (Coordinator:259-263), a
  `Task.sleep(20 ms)` loop on `inputPending`.
- `inputPending` rises in `inputActivityStarted()` (Coordinator:196-199; called from
  `noteLevel` after 120 ms of energy, Capture:896-901, and from
  `considerSpeechInterruption`, Capture:1133-1137) and in `handle` (:299). On the answer
  route it falls only in `resolveInput(epoch:)` at Coordinator:497, **after** the whole
  answer has streamed; control routes (`newWork`, `revise`, `cancel`, `capabilities`) fall
  at :502-532 once the envelope is parsed.

**Target behaviour:**
1. A background job's planning rounds and read calls never wait for the user's input to be
   classified. Its rounds advance while the user and the frontend are talking.
2. A call with `risk > .read` waits, at the moment it would commit (after approval, before
   fire), while any user input is unclassified; if the task was revised meanwhile, the call
   is dropped and the next round re-plans (I3, I4).
3. The barrier is released as soon as the route of the latest turn is known (first answer
   delta or control envelope), not after the answer finishes.
4. No polling: waiting uses continuations resumed when the barrier falls.
5. New `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` proves 1–3 and fails on today's code.

**Steps:**
1. **Barrier with continuations.** In `VoiceConversationCoordinator` replace the poll:
   ```swift
   private var barrierWaiters: [UUID: CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>] = [:]
   private func setInputPending(_ value: Bool) {
       inputPending = value
       guard !value else { return }
       let waiters = barrierWaiters; barrierWaiters = [:]
       for (_, c) in waiters { c.resume() }
   }
   func waitForInputResolution() async {
       while inputPending, AgentCaptureController.shared.isSessionActive, !Task.isCancelled {
           let id = UUID()
           await withTaskCancellationHandler {
               await withCheckedContinuation { c in
                   if !inputPending || Task.isCancelled { c.resume() } else { barrierWaiters[id] = c }
               }
           } onCancel: { Task { @MainActor in self.barrierWaiters.removeValue(forKey: id)?.resume() } }
       }
   }
   ```
   Route every write of `inputPending` (`inputActivityStarted`, `speechStarted`,
   `discardInput`, `resolveInput`, `closeSession`, `noteHesitation`, `handle`) through
   `setInputPending`. `closeSession` must resume all waiters (session close breaks the wait).
   (P3-06b later moves the flag into `VoiceSession`; the waiter list moves with it into
   `TaskBridge.setEffectsHeld`.)
2. **Release at route.** In `respond` (Coordinator:438-457) add `var routeReleased = false`
   outside the bounded wait. Inside the `for try await delta` loop, right after
   `let parsed = VoiceFrontendEnvelope.parse(snapshot)`:
   ```swift
   if !routeReleased, case .answer = parsed {
       routeReleased = true
       await MainActor.run { self.resolveInput(epoch: inputEpoch) }   // the answer route cannot create or revise work
   }
   ```
   (The closure is already main-actor; use a direct call if the compiler allows.) If P2-02
   replaced the envelope with a streamed structured decision, release when the decision's
   intent field arrives and equals `answer`. Keep the existing `resolveInput` at :497 — it
   is now a no-op for this epoch but harmless. Control routes already release at parse time.
3. **Remove the round-boundary waits** at ToolLoop:864, :915 and :954 (delete the three
   `await waitForVoiceInput()` lines). Keep every `revision != (work?.revision ?? 0)` check —
   they fold a correction in at the next boundary without waiting.
4. **Gate by risk in `isStillValid`.** Replace the closure at ToolLoop:999-1002 with:
   ```swift
   isStillValid: { [tool] in
       guard self.isCurrent(owner), revision == (work?.revision ?? 0) else { return false }
       guard await self.mayCommitEffect(risk: tool.risk) else { return false }   // P0-07
       return self.isCurrent(owner) && revision == (work?.revision ?? 0)       // re-check after the wait
   }
   ```
   where `mayCommitEffect(risk:)` is P0-07's `RealtimeAgent` function (Phase 0 file, P0-07
   steps 3–4: it returns `true` at once for reads and for the typed agent, and otherwise awaits
   the coordinator's `mayCommitEffect()`). Grep what actually shipped and keep that signature.
   In this task change the coordinator body it delegates to so that it implements §2.5.1
   steps 1–5: return immediately for
   reads; otherwise `await waitForInputResolution()`, then re-check that the job still exists,
   is running and has the same revision. The wait is outside every budget: writes already run
   unbounded (`if tool.risk > .read { execution = await execute() }`, ToolLoop:1035-1036),
   so no budget change is needed.
5. **Non-worker `waitForVoiceInput`** (RealtimeAgent.swift:104-107, the 50 ms poll on
   `voiceInputActive` for the shared agent) is left alone: `voiceInputActive` is set and
   cleared in consecutive statements in `emitTurn` (Capture:1303, :1307), so it is a no-op
   in production (C report Q5), and P3-06b deletes it.
6. **Write the self-test first** (step 7), watch it fail on the unmodified code, then do
   steps 1–4.
7. **`--selftest-voice-duplex-work`** in `Agent/Duplex/VoiceDuplexWorkSelfTest.swift`
   (`enum VoiceDuplexWorkSelfTest { @MainActor static func run() async -> Bool }`):
   - Setup: `capture.beginSession(captureAudio: false)`, `AgentSession.shared.clear()`,
     `conversation.resetForTesting()`, `RecordingSpeechBacking`.
   - Worker: `conversation.workerProviderForTesting = DuplexWorkerProvider(state:)`, a
     scripted `LLMProvider` whose `stream`/`complete` returns, per round, (round 1) a read
     call `{"name":"computer.active_app","arguments":{}}`, (round 2) a write call — see
     below — and (round 3) the final answer `"Done with the duplex check."`. Each round
     records `(roundIndex, startedAt, revisionSeen)` and sleeps 150 ms before returning
     (simulated prefill), so rounds take measurable time.
   - Write fixture: a `.modify`-risk call that must not touch the machine. Reuse P0-07's
     write fixture if `ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest` has one. Otherwise add to
     `AgentToolExecutor`:
     ```swift
     /// Self-test only: replaces the final side effect; broker, card and validity checks still run.
     @MainActor static var fireOverrideForTesting: ((AgentTool, [String: String]) async throws -> AgentToolResult)?
     ```
     and, inside the `fire` closure passed to `ActionOrchestrator.execute`, first line:
     `if SelfTest.isRunning, let override = fireOverrideForTesting { return try await override(tool, prepared.executionPlan.arguments) }`.
     Use `computer.press_key` with `{"key":"escape"}` and the job objective "Press escape
     in the front window." The override records `(firedAt, revisionAtFire)`. The card this
     raises is approved by a helper task that polls `PermissionGate.shared.pending` every
     10 ms and calls `respond(id:approved: true)`. If `respond` returns false because the
     review has blockers, print them as `VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_DIAGNOSTIC: blockers …` and fail
     with `"write fixture blocked by review"` (pick another `.modify` tool whose arguments
     all appear in the objective).
   - Conversation: `conversation.streamForTesting` parks each answer 300 ms (simulating
     generation) before finishing; five turns via the `speak(_:)` helper
     (`agent.userSpeechStarted(); agent.userSpeechEnded(); await agent.handle(text, source: .voice)`):
     "Press escape in the front window." (→ `<use_tools/>`), "What is a haiku?",
     "Tell me a fun fact.", "Actually, press tab instead." (→ `<revise id="1"/>` after a
     500 ms park), "Thanks." Record for each turn `handleCalledAt` and `streamCalledAt`.
   - Assertions (failure string in quotes):
     - **A1 interleave:** at least 2 worker rounds started inside some frontend stream's
       `[streamCalledAt, streamFinishedAt]` window — `"worker rounds did not advance during conversation (\(n) of ≥2)"`.
     - **A2 no stale write:** no `firedAt` lies between the revision turn's `handleCalledAt`
       and the moment its `<revise>` was parsed; and no fire happened with
       `revisionAtFire == 0` after the revision was committed — `"write committed between a revising turn's commit and its classification"`.
     - **A3 frontend never waits on the worker:** for every turn `streamCalledAt − handleCalledAt ≤ 50 ms` —
       `"answer route waited \(ms) ms on background work"`.
     - **A4 C1 regression:** one extra turn "Unclear instruction." with a stream that never
       yields and `responseDeadlineForTesting = .milliseconds(200)`; the worker's next round
       starts ≤ 200 ms after the failure reply — `"failed turn froze background work"`.
     - **A5 completion:** the job ends `"finished"` with result `"Done with the duplex check."` within 10 s —
       `"worker never finished"` (so a test in which the worker never ran cannot pass).
   - Output: one `VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_WRONG: <reason>` per failure, timestamps as
     `VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_TRACE: …` lines, final `VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_OK` or
     `VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_FAILED: <first reason>`. Reset every seam (`streamForTesting`,
     `workerProviderForTesting`, `fireOverrideForTesting`, `responseDeadlineForTesting`) in a
     `defer`.
8. Register the flag in `NextNotesApp.runRequestedSelfTest` beside `--selftest-concurrent-voice`
   (NextNotesApp.swift:1135-1142 is the pattern: `writeSelfTest(SelfTest.failed ? "…_FAILED" : "…_OK")`).
   Add it to AGENTS.md's flag list and to `Scripts/acceptance.sh` EXPERIMENTAL now; move it to
   CORE at the phase exit.
9. AGENTS.md, in "Voice work and output have separate lifetimes", replace nothing and add:
   "A background job's planning and reads never wait for the user; only an effect with
   `risk > .read` waits, after approval and before it fires, until the latest input is
   classified (`mayCommitEffect`). The barrier falls as soon as the route is known."

**Do not:**
- Do not drop the `revision != work.revision` checks: they are what folds a correction in (I3).
- Do not bound a write's wait with a timeout or charge it to the round budget — a write may be waiting for a person, and "stopped" must never be said while the write later commits (ToolLoop:1031-1033; AGENTS: budgets exclude the floor and approvals).
- Do not move the validity check before approval: ActionRuntime.swift:514-518 is placed after every permission await on purpose.
- Do not release the barrier on a partial parse of a control envelope (`.pending`); only a parsed `.answer` or a parsed control route releases it.
- Do not let `closeSession` leave waiters suspended (session close breaks the wait).
- Do not touch `RealtimeAgent.shared`'s `waitForVoiceInput` behaviour for typed turns.
- Do not write user stores from the test (I8); the fire override prevents any real key press.

**Red-first self-test:** `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` (new, above). Confirm red on the
unmodified code (after P3-01): expected output includes
`VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_WRONG: worker rounds did not advance during conversation (0 of ≥2)` and
`VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_FAILED: …`. Save the output in STATUS.md, then implement.

**Verify:**
```bash
make build
make install OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-duplex-work
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-concurrent-voice
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-work-lifecycle
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-conversation
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-toolloop
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-toolloop-production
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-tool-review
make acceptance TIER=core
```

**Success threshold:** `VOICE_DUPLEX_WORK_OK` in 3 consecutive runs; A1 shows ≥ 2
interleaved rounds; A3 max ≤ 50 ms; A4 ≤ 200 ms; regression flags OK; no CORE regression.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: a write planned before a correction still fires because the
correction arrived after the gate returned — acceptable by design (the gate is the last
check before fire, AGENTS "effects wait until the latest input is classified" means at
commit time); A2 pins the window that matters. Risk: more GPU contention during speech
(the worker no longer parks) — the scheduler still preempts it at checkpoints for the
frontend (`.realtimeAgent` beats `.background`); measure with `--selftest-voice-pipeline`
if turns slow down. Rollback: restore the three `waitForVoiceInput()` lines and the old
closure; the continuation-based barrier can stay.

**Done when:**
- [ ] round-boundary waits removed; reads return from the gate immediately
- [ ] writes wait in `mayCommitEffect` after approval, before fire
- [ ] barrier released at route; no 20 ms poll left in the coordinator
- [ ] `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` red before, green after (3 runs), registered, in AGENTS.md and acceptance.sh
- [ ] AGENTS.md rule added; README tree lists `Agent/Duplex/`

**Finding(s):** C6, C7, C10 (ledger half), C report §4 ranks 8, 9, 15; qwen patterns 1, 3, 7 (E §1.8)  **Effort:** 3–4 days (a: 2 d, b: 1.5 d)  **Depends on:** P3-02, P3-06a (for `TaskID`/`TurnID` types in `VoiceContracts.swift`)  **Unblocks:** P3-04, P3-05, P3-08, P3-09

**Why (what the user experiences today):** "How's it going?" gets "still working on it":
the frontend's only view of a job is `Task 1 [running]: <first 650 characters of the
prompt>` (Coordinator:667-671), with no step, tool or elapsed time, and answering it costs a
full route + answer pass (1.5–2.5 s measured, A Hop 6) during which the job was paused
(before P3-02). "Have Claude Code fix the tests" by voice runs the 4B local planner instead,
because voice never reaches `AgentHarnessRouter` (RealtimeAgent.swift:207-211), and such a
coding task, when started by typing, cannot be asked about, cancelled or heard by voice —
the voice frontend's work status lists only the coordinator's `jobs`. The session can also
idle-close while an ACP task is running, because `hasActiveWork` counts coordinator jobs only
(Coordinator:40, Capture:1274), which strands its result (C report Q4).

- qwen reference, read in this order:
  `roadmap/references/qwen-audio-agent-main/server/src/task/task-state.mjs` (states, legal transitions, `publicTask` — compare with §2.5);
  `server/src/frontend/tools/agent-task-runtime.mjs` lines 288-505 (`executeSpawnThinkingToolCall`: pending-permission refusal, cached availability, per-turn duplicate detection, the receipt) and 840-935 (`getAgentTaskStatus`: status read from the record, `consumesTaskNotification`);
  `server/src/frontend/tools/features/agent-task-tools.mjs` (tool descriptions for status/cancel);

- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Backend/AgentHarnessRouter.swift` (`choose(for:)` :113, `explicitHarness(in:)` :222), `Agent/ACPConfirmation.swift` (a missing CLI needs the card).

**Current behaviour:**
- Two ledgers. `VoiceConversationCoordinator.jobs: [Job]` (Coordinator:13-21: `status` is the
  string `"running"`/`"finished"`/`"cancelled"`, no failure or needs-input state) and
  `AgentTaskManager.tasks` (persisted, `AgentTaskStatus`), linked only by
  `beginVoiceObjective`/`finishVoiceObjective`/`cancelVoiceObjective`
  (AgentTaskManager.swift:88-114).
- `submit(_:)` (Coordinator:688-708) creates `VoiceConversationWork` + `RealtimeAgent(voiceWorker:)`,
  appends a `Job`, mirrors it, and runs `worker.runVoiceObjective()`; a finished job's string
  result is always "finished", even an error sentence (C report §2.2 Region C).
- `AgentTaskManager.cancel(_:)` (:116-130) special-cases coordinator jobs.
- Status: the route rules make "how's it going?" an `answerQuestion`
  (LocalVoiceDecision.swift:129-131, "A question about the progress or purpose of an already
  running task is answerQuestion"), answered by the FM answer pass from the work-status line.
- Step titles already exist: `AgentToolExecutor.run` projects every fired tool into
  `AgentActivityStore.update(taskID:kind:title:…)` with the voice job's id
  (`taskID: work?.id.uuidString`, ToolLoop:997), so `AgentActivityStore.steps(taskID:)` holds
  consumer-worded steps for voice jobs today — nothing reads them for voice.

**Target behaviour:**
1. `TaskBridge.shared` is the only owner of task state for every task a person can ask
   about. The coordinator no longer has a `jobs` array (after P3-03b). `AgentTaskManager`
   remains the executor for typed/ACP backends and the persistence layer; it reports state
   changes to TaskBridge instead of deciding them.
2. `spawn` returns a `TaskReceipt` synchronously (target ≤ 5 ms, never awaits a model,
   backend or ASR).
3. "How's it going?" with a running session task is answered by `TaskStatusRenderer` with
   the current step and elapsed time, **with no model call**, in < 50 ms from commit.
   With no task in the ledger it still reaches the model (I5 positive corpus).
4. The frontend's work status lists ledger tasks — voice and ACP — with state words, step
   and elapsed time; no internal ids.
5. A voice request naming a coding harness ("have Claude Code fix the tests") becomes an ACP
   task when that harness is available, is refused with the existing confirmation card when
   it is not, and is otherwise a local worker.
6. Idle close counts every non-terminal session task, including ACP ones.
7. Task states follow §2.5's legal-transition table; an illegal transition asserts in DEBUG.
8. (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V21) "Cancel", "cancel it", "cancel that task" and "stop that task"
   cancel session work without a model call, like the seven phrases today; bare "stop" does
   not (it means "stop talking"). With **more than one** running session task, the newest one
   is cancelled and the reply names it ("I stopped <title>."), instead of the phrase going to
   the model, which then needs a task number.
9. (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V19) The work-status block given to the frontend is fitted to the
   Foundation Models window (4,096 tokens, `FoundationModelLLMProvider.contextTokens` @
   `Formatting/LLM/FoundationModelLLMProvider.swift:13`): the newest finished task's result gets
   up to ~1,200 characters, every other task ≤ 250, so "what did it find?" is answered from
   the result rather than from its first 600 characters.

**6A note (2026-09-27, owner-approved Phase 6 split).** If Phase 6A has landed, `AgentTaskManager`
persists through `TaskStore` (`agent-tasks.sqlite`), and `AgentTask` already carries
`TaskDurability` and the `.recovering` status. `TaskBridge` builds on that same canonical `AgentTask`/SQL ledger; it must not write
`agent-tasks.json` directly or maintain a second mutable task mirror. Its `TaskRecord`
and status values project the Manager's existing cached rows. Optional origin/session,
revision and delivery fields belong on those same rows with legacy `decodeIfPresent`.
Independently testable producer work may proceed under 00-README §4.1 with serial,
explicit ownership of shared Manager/store functions. Parent P6-04a remaining contracts
still block the live recovery/ownership changes that require them; the task's open
status is not a blanket prohibition on a disjoint bounded source repair.

**Steps — P3-03a (ledger behind the coordinator):**
1. Implement the §2.5 `TaskBridge` interface over the SAME `AgentTaskManager` and
   canonical `AgentTask`/SQL rows. Existing `TaskID`/`TurnID`, `TaskState`, `TaskOrigin`,
   `TaskDelivery` and event contracts remain required. `TaskRecord`, receipts and status
   reports are value projections, never a separately persisted record set. Do not add
   `records`, `order`, `voiceJobs`, `Manager.mirror`, or another ledger/result bus.
   Preserve the existing Coordinator/Manager worker slots as transitional owners until
   the actual migration and P3-06a dependencies qualify; expose the existing testing
   seams rather than duplicating them. P3-03b removes transitional Coordinator ownership
   only after its prescribed local/ACP contracts and actual effect/receipt checks pass.
2. Keep canonical state mutation and primary admission in the responsible existing
   Manager transition/save functions. Map §2.5 `TaskState` truthfully onto stored
   `AgentTaskStatus`; unknown legacy/restart state cannot be reconstructed as running.
   Enforce the legal transition and current ownership contracts at those producers,
   emitting the prescribed events through the existing seam. Same-row optional metadata
   and rollback must travel in the accepted primary transaction. Do not announce a
   successful transition or start a worker after refused admission. User cancellation
   immediately revokes future worker/effect admission even if persistence fails; retain
   and report the uncertain durable result without inventing successful stored state.
   These steps preserve all later origin/session, revision, progress, receipt, delivery,
   duplicate-admission, needs-input/permission and live-command acceptance requirements.
3. `spawn(objective:userWords:origin:backend:)` for a **local voice job** (P3-03a handles
   only this; ACP in P3-03b):
   - If `origin` is `.voice(sessionID, turn)` and a record with the same `turn` and the same
     normalized objective (`VoiceTranscriptCanonical.key`) exists → return
     `TaskReceipt(id: existing, status: .duplicate, title:)`.
   - If a session-owned approval is pending (`PermissionGate.shared.pending?.taskID` belongs
     to a record of this session) → `.refusedApprovalPending` (qwen refuses new work while a
     permission is pending; the user's "yes" must answer the card — P3-08).
   - Otherwise build `VoiceConversationWork(objective)`, the worker (copying
     `workerProviderForTesting`), a `TaskRecord` (`state: .queued`, `title:
     TaskTitle.make(objective)`, `delivery: .none`), call
     `AgentTaskManager.shared.beginVoiceObjective(id:objective:)`, emit `.accepted`, then
     `transition(.running)` and start the `Task { @MainActor in … }` exactly as
     `Coordinator.submit` does today (Coordinator:694-706), but on return call
     `finishVoiceJob(id, outcome:)` instead of writing `jobs`.
   - `TaskTitle.make(_:)`: the objective's first clause, trimmed to ≤ 60 characters at a
     word boundary, no trailing punctuation; never an id.
4. `finishVoiceJob(_ id:, result: String)`: ignore if the record is `.cancelling/.cancelled`
   (then confirm `.cancelled`). Classify the worker's string: if P1-04 introduced a typed
   outcome for the tool loop, use it; otherwise treat a result that begins with one of the
   tool loop's failure sentences (`"The tool planner failed"`, `"I stopped the tool plan"`,
   `"The tool planner requested an unavailable tool"`, `"I can’t plan tool use"`,
   `"The local tool catalogue is unavailable."`, `"The tool "` + `" did not run: "`) as
   `.failed(reason:)`, a result for which `AgentEntityResolver.askedForAName(result)` is true
   or which ends with `?` and is ≤ 200 characters as `.needsInput(question:)`, and anything
   else as `.completed`. Then set `result`/`failure`/`question`, `finishedAt`, transition,
   and call `AgentTaskManager.shared.finishVoiceObjective(id:result:)` (it records the
   `backgroundTask` conversation row and, until P3-04a, enqueues the announcement).
5. `revise(_ id:, text:, turn:)`: allowed in `.running`, `.needsInput`, `.needsPermission`.
   `work.append(text)`; `record.revision = work.revision`; emit `.revised`. From
   `.needsInput`, restart the job: transition `.running` and run
   `worker.runVoiceObjective()` again on the same `VoiceConversationWork` (the prompt now
   carries the original request plus the answer — VoiceConversationWork.swift:20-33). This
   replaces the "asked for a name → `submit("open \(named)")`" new-job branch
   (Coordinator:351-355) with a continuation of the same task (qwen `respond_agent_input`).
   The approval interaction (`PermissionGate.cancelPending(taskID:)` at :347/:525) is left
   as it is here; P3-08 changes it.
6. `cancel(_ id:, reason:) async -> TaskState`: resolve `nil` to the single cancellable
   session task, else the newest one. For a voice job: `transition(.cancelling)`,
   `worker.cancelVoiceObjective()`, `task.cancel()`,
   `PermissionGate.shared.cancelPending(taskID:)`, then await the job's `Task` value with a
   2 s bound (`withBoundedWait`); if it ended, `transition(.cancelled)` and
   `AgentTaskManager.shared.cancelVoiceObjective(id:)`; if not, return `.cancelling` (an
   in-flight write is allowed to finish — ToolLoop:1031-1036 — and the job's own return
   path confirms `.cancelled` later). For a typed/ACP task call the existing
   `AgentTaskManager.cancel(_:)` path (its `execute` catches `CancellationError` → `.cancelled`,
   AgentTaskManager.swift:226-228) and mirror the result.
7. Progress: in `AgentActivityStore.update(taskID:kind:title:detail:avatar:)`
   (AgentActivityStore.swift:61-75) add one line at the end:
   `TaskBridge.shared.noteProgress(taskID: taskID, step: steps.count, title: title)`, which
   updates `record.progress` for a known id and emits `.progress` (observability only —
   never spoken). No other code path writes progress.
8. Queries: `status(_:sessionID:)` builds `TaskStatusReport`s (`elapsed` from `startedAt`;
   `stepTitle` from `record.progress?.stepTitle` passed through
   `AgentActivityProjector.isPublic` and with a trailing "…" removed; `resultGist` = first
   sentence of `result` ≤ 160 characters). `workStatusLines(sessionID:)` renders up to five
   session tasks (running first, then the most recent terminal ones from the last 10
   minutes), numbered 1…n in creation order, e.g.
   `Task 2 [working, step 3: reading a file, 40 s]: Find the Q3 budget spreadsheet` — the
   number is the one the route model uses for `taskNumber`; keep `indexedSessionTasks()` so
   `<revise id="2"/>` maps back to the same `TaskID`. Replace the body of
   `frontendRequest(_:)`'s `workContext` (Coordinator:667-671) with this.
9. **Status gate** (deterministic, additive — I5). In `VoiceTurnPolicy` add:
   ```swift
   /// Exact status-question signatures (normalizedKey form). A question outside this list
   /// still reaches the model, which can route it to checkTaskStatus.
   static let statusQuestionSignatures: Set<String> = [
       "how s it going", "how is it going", "hows it going", "how s that going", "how is that going",
       "is it done", "is it done yet", "are you done", "are you done yet", "is that done",
       "any update", "any updates", "any news", "what s the status", "what is the status", "status",
       "how far along are you", "is it finished", "are you finished", "what are you working on",
       "are you still working on it", "still working on it", "how long will it take",
   ]
   static func isStatusQuestion(_ text: String) -> Bool { statusQuestionSignatures.contains(normalizedKey(text)) }
   ```
   (Check `normalizedKey("How's it going?")` in a unit line of `VoiceTurnPolicy.selfTestFailures`
   and adjust the apostrophe forms to what it actually produces.) In `Coordinator.respond`,
   **after** the pending-intent block (:359-367) and **before** the tool-shape gate
   (:368-378), insert:
   ```swift
   if VoiceTurnPolicy.isStatusQuestion(text),
      let sessionID = AgentCaptureController.shared.sessionID,
      !TaskBridge.shared.status(nil, sessionID: sessionID).isEmpty,
      !statusGate.isRepeat(text, within: TaskBridge.statusRepeatEscalation) {
       let reports = TaskBridge.shared.status(nil, sessionID: sessionID)
       TaskBridge.shared.consumeDeliveries(for: reports)          // D7
       resolveInput(epoch: inputEpoch)
       AgentAuditLog.shared.record(kind: .reply, title: "Status from the task list", detail: "status_gate")
       return agent.finishVoiceFrontend(TaskStatusRenderer.spoken(reports), turn: turn, streamed: false)
   }
   ```
   `statusGate.isRepeat` remembers the last gate-answered text and time; the same words again
   within 5 s escalate to the model (the ledger facts are already in the work status). With
   no session task the gate does not fire and the turn reaches the model.
10. **Status route.** Add `case checkTaskStatus` to `LocalVoiceRoute.Intent`
    (LocalVoiceDecision.swift:64-70) and change the route rule sentence at :129-131 to:
    "A question about how running or recent work is going, whether it is done, or what is
    being worked on is checkTaskStatus; it must not start the same task again." In
    `LocalVoiceFrontend.streamSplitDecision` (LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:516-550) map it to the
    envelope `<status id="N"/>` (or `<status/>` without a number); extend
    `VoiceFrontendEnvelope` (Coordinator:782-807) with `case status(Int?)` and in `respond`
    answer it exactly like the gate (no answer pass). If P2-02 replaced envelopes with a typed
    decision, add the case there instead. `TaskStatusRenderer` with no task returns
    "Nothing is running right now." — spoken, one clause.
11. `TaskStatusRenderer.spoken(_ reports: [TaskStatusReport]) -> String` — deterministic,
    plain words, no ids, no tool ids, ≤ 2 sentences:
    - running: "Still on \(title) — \(step), \(elapsedWords)." (`elapsedWords`: < 10 s "just
      started", < 60 s "about N seconds in" rounded to 5, else "about N minutes in"); no step
      yet → "Still on \(title), \(elapsedWords)."
    - needsPermission: "\(title) is waiting for your OK." (P3-08 turns this into a spoken
      prompt.)
    - needsInput: "\(title) needs an answer: \(question)"
    - completed (delivery pending): "\(title) is done: \(resultGist)"
    - failed: "\(title) didn't work: \(failureGist)"
    - several: first two as above joined with " Also, ", then "and N more" if any.
    Run the output through `RealtimeAgent.voiceSafeReply` (RealtimeAgent.swift:178-197).

    AgentCaptureController.shared.sessionID ?? UUID(), turn: currentTurnID), backend: .local)`
    (use `TurnID(raw: UInt64(inputEpoch))` as a placeholder until P3-06b supplies real

13. `AgentTaskManager.cancel(_:)` (:116-121): replace the coordinator special case with

15. `AgentTask`: add `var delivery: TaskDelivery?` — add `case delivery` to `CodingKeys`

    `init(from:)` (AgentTask.swift:109-134). **Never make it non-optional or skip the

15a. **Unambiguous cancel phrases; the newest task when several run** (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V21).
    Today, re-verified 2026-09-24:
    ```swift
    // VoiceTurnPolicy.isExplicitWorkCancellation @ Activation/VoiceTurnPolicy.swift:32-41
    return Set([
        "cancel that", "cancel the task", "cancel what you are doing",
        "stop working on that", "never mind", "nevermind", "forget that request"
    ]).contains(normalized)
    // VoiceConversationCoordinator.respond @ Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift:379
    if VoiceTurnPolicy.isExplicitWorkCancellation(text), active.count == 1 {
    ```
    (The line moved from the :332 the audit and §2.4 cite; grep the symbol.) So "Cancel it",
    "cancel", "stop that task" go to the model, and with two jobs even "cancel that" does.
    - Add exactly `"cancel"`, `"cancel it"`, `"cancel that task"`, `"stop that task"` to the set
      (normalized form: lowercase, punctuation → space, trimmed — the function's own rule). Do
      **not** add `"stop"`, `"stop it"` or anything with a trailing object ("cancel my 3 pm",
      "cancel the meeting with Anna" are new work for the calendar, not a work cancellation).
    - In `respond`, the gate becomes `isExplicitWorkCancellation(text), !active.isEmpty` and
      calls `await TaskBridge.shared.cancel(nil, reason: .spoken)` — step 6 already resolves
      `nil` to the single cancellable session task, else the newest. Reply:
      one task → "I stopped \(title)." (`.cancelled`) or "Stopping \(title) now." (`.cancelling`);
      several → the same sentence plus " \(otherTitle) is still going." for the newest remaining
      one (and "and N more" when N > 1). Titles come from `TaskTitle.make` — never an id or a
      number.
    - With **no** non-terminal session task the gate does not fire and the turn reaches the
      model exactly as today (a gate may only add).
    - Keep `RealtimeAgent.appendVoiceFollowUp`'s call of the same function (RealtimeAgent.swift:80)
      unchanged; P3-01 deletes that branch as unreachable.
    - Extend `VoiceTurnPolicy.selfTestFailures` (the positive list at :139 and the ambiguous list
      at :144): positives `"Cancel."`, `"Cancel it."`, `"Cancel that task"`, `"Stop that task."`;
      negatives `"Stop."`, `"stop it"`, `"cancel my three pm meeting"`, `"cancel the email one"`
      (the last goes to the model, which resolves "the email one" to a task number).
15b. **Fit the work-status block to the Foundation Models window** (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V19).
    Today `frontendRequest(_:)` @ Coordinator:720-738 takes `jobs.suffix(5)` and writes each as
    `"Task \(n) [\(status)]: \(prompt.prefix(650))"` plus `"Verified result: \(result.prefix(600))"`
    (:722-724). Step 8's `workStatusLines(sessionID:)` replaces it; give it these budgets as named
    constants in `TaskStatusRenderer.swift`:
    ```swift
    enum WorkStatusBudget {
        /// FoundationModelLLMProvider.contextTokens is 4,096. The route + answer prompt, the
        /// persona card, history (1,400 characters) and the answer (≤ 256 tokens) share it;
        /// these two caps keep the block under ~2,500 characters (≈ 625 tokens, estimate at
        /// 4 characters per token) with five tasks.
        static let newestResultCharacters = 1_200
        static let otherCharacters = 250            // per task: title + step + result gist together
    }
    ```
    - The newest task in a terminal state with a non-empty `result` gets
      `Result: <result cut at the last sentence end ≤ 1,200 characters>` (if there is no sentence
      end, cut at the last word boundary and add "…").
    - Every other listed task (running first, then recent terminal ones, as step 8 orders them)
      is one line ≤ 250 characters in total: title, state words, step, elapsed, and for a
      terminal one its first-sentence gist.
    - The objective itself is never repeated past its title: `TaskTitle.make` already carries it
      in ≤ 60 characters, and the full prompt text is the worker's, not the frontend's.
    - Results pass through the P1-10 renderer (no internal ids) before they are cut; if P1-10 is
      not done, strip UUID-shaped tokens (`[0-9A-Fa-f]{8}-[0-9A-Fa-f]{4}-…`) and registered tool
      ids (the exact ids from the tool registry, never a shape rule) before cutting.

**Steps — P3-03b (one ledger including ACP; delete `jobs`):**
16. `spawn` chooses the backend when `backend == nil` or `.local` was not forced:
    ```swift
    let choice = AgentHarnessRouter.shared.choose(for: userWords)
    if AgentHarnessRouter.explicitHarness(in: userWords) != nil, choice.backend == .acp {
        if choice.needsACPConfirmation {
            Task { @MainActor in   // raises the existing card; spawn never awaits it
                if await ACPConfirmationGate.shared.request(choice, utterance: userWords) == .confirmedOnce {
                    _ = TaskBridge.shared.spawn(objective: objective, userWords: userWords, origin: origin, backend: .local)
                }
            }
            return TaskReceipt(id: nil, status: .refusedNeedsConfirmation, title: title)
        }
        let task = AgentTaskManager.shared.submit(objective: objective, contextReferences: AgentContext.current.references,
            meetingID: MeetingContextStore.shared.current?.meetingID, backend: .acp, acpCLI: choice.acpCLI, source: "voice")
        // record with origin .voice, backend .acp, state .running → .delegated once execute reports running
    }
    ```
    (`ACPConfirmationGate.request(_:utterance:)` is at Agent/ACPConfirmation.swift:96 and suspends
    until the card is answered, so it runs in a detached task: "Run once" starts the same
    objective as a local ledger task, "Cancel" starts nothing.) Receipt reply for
    `.refusedNeedsConfirmation`: "That needs your OK on screen first." Record the routing with `AgentHarnessRouter.shared.record(choice, snippet:intent:)`
    as `RealtimeAgent.delegate` does (RealtimeAgent.swift:490-515).
17. `AgentTaskManager.execute` (:188-276) reports every state change to
    `TaskBridge.shared.backendStateChanged(TaskID(id), status:, result:, failure:)`, which maps
    `AgentTaskStatus` → `TaskState` (running → `.delegated` for ACP, `.running` for local;
    waitingForPermission/waitingForCompatibilityCLI → `.needsPermission`; waitingForInput →
    `.needsInput`) and emits events. Records are created for **every** `submit`, with origin
    `.typed`, `.scheduled` (from `beginScheduledRun`), or `.voice` as passed; only `.voice`
    records of the current session appear in voice status and work status by default, and
    `status(nil, sessionID:)` falls back to the most recent typed/ACP task of the last 10
    minutes when the session has none ("is the coding task done?").
18. Delete `Coordinator.jobs`, `Job`, the forwarding seams, and the naming branch's
    `jobs.last` lookups; port `ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest`, `VoiceWorkLifecycleSelfTest`,
    `VoiceConversationSelfTest` and `VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest` to read
    `TaskBridge.shared.records` (state `.running`/`.completed`/`.cancelled`).
19. AGENTS.md: add a paragraph "**One task ledger.** `TaskBridge` owns the state of every
    task a person can ask about… status is read from it without a model call… a voice
    request naming a coding harness goes to ACP through the harness router and its
    confirmation card." README: list `TaskBridge.swift`, `TaskStatusRenderer.swift` under
    `Agent/Duplex/`.

**Do not:**
- Do not call a model, a backend, or await ASR inside `spawn` (qwen: "this receipt only acknowledges intake").
- Do not speak progress (D9); `.progress` events are observability only.
- Do not put a task id, tool id, file path or URL in any spoken status (P1-10 renderer rule; `--selftest-ui-strings` bans developer words in UI strings — status sentences also appear in the feed).
- Do not make the status gate match by shape, length or keywords — exact signatures only, and only when the ledger has something to report; a repeat within 5 s escalates to the model (AGENTS: "a deterministic gate may only add"). Keep the positive corpus in `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` reaching the model.
- Do not start ACP when the named CLI is missing without the confirmation card (`ACPConfirmation`: "A missing CLI is never 'run local' or 'start ACP' until [Run once] / [Cancel]").
- Do not add a non-optional stored field to `AgentTask` or skip `decodeIfPresent` (tasks on disk would vanish).
- Do not let a self-test write `agent-tasks.json` or the audit log (I8; P0-11 seams).
- Do not let a revision cancel work (I3). Cancellation is only `TaskBridge.cancel`.
- Do not add bare "stop" (or "stop it", "wait") to the cancel phrases, and do not match cancel phrases by prefix, substring or word count — "stop" means stop talking, and "cancel my 3 pm" is new work (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V21; AGENTS: exact signatures only).
- Do not grow the work-status block past `WorkStatusBudget` to "give the model more": the Foundation Models window is 4,096 tokens and an overflow fails the whole turn (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V19).

- (H-audit 2026-09-23) `--selftest-voice-turns` (`AgentCaptureController.turnPolicySelfTestFailures`,

- (H-audit 2026-09-23) `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` gains a **cancel block** next to the status
  block: (5) two parked jobs spawned ("Check my calendar for tomorrow.", then "Find the budget
  spreadsheet."); `before = streamCalls`; `handle("Cancel it.")` → `streamCalls == before`, the
  newer record is `.cancelling`/`.cancelled`, the older is still `.running`, and the reply contains

  user message (`probe.seenMessages.last`) contains "Sentence 30 ends here." and its work-status
  section is ≤ 2,500 characters; no other task line exceeds 250 characters. Failures:
  `"newest result cut below its budget"`, `"work status overflowed its budget"`. **Red today:**
  case 5 makes a model call (the gate requires `active.count == 1`); case 7 fails on
  "newest result cut below its budget" (`result.prefix(600)`).
- `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` (VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.swift `runTurnRouting`,
  :695) gains a **status block**, using the existing `TurnRoutingProbe` (its `streamCalls`
  counts frontend model calls):
  1. Spawn a job via `coordinator.handle("Check my calendar for tomorrow.")` with a
     `<use_tools/>` script and a `workerForTesting` that parks; then call
     `AgentActivityStore.shared.update(taskID: <job id>, kind: .reading, title: "Reading your calendar…")`.
     Record `before = await probe.streamCalls`; `handle("How's it going?")`. Assert:
     `streamCalls == before` (**no model call**); the reply contains "calendar" and matches

     no tool id. Failure strings: `"status question made a model call"`,
     `"status did not name the current step"`.
  2. With no tasks (`resetForTesting`), `handle("How's it going?")` must increment
     `streamCalls` by 1 (positive corpus — the gate must not eat small talk). Failure:

  3. A completed job whose delivery is pending: `handle("Is it done?")` → reply contains the

  4. Same status words twice within 5 s → second reaches the model. Failure:
     `"repeated status question never escalated"`.
  Marker unchanged: `VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_OK` / `VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_FAILED`.
- `--selftest-tasks` (`runTasksSelfTest`, NextNotesApp.swift:4613) gains voice rows: spawn
  a voice task through `TaskBridge.shared.spawn(… origin: .voice(…))` with

- **Confirm red:** before implementing, add the status block and run
  `--selftest-voice-turn-routing`: expect `status question made a model call` (today the

- **P3-03b** adds an ACP case to `--selftest-acp` (it already runs the stdio fixtures,

Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-tasks

**Success threshold:** status answer with 0 model calls and ≤ 50 ms from `handle` to reply
(measure in the test and print `VOICE_STATUS_LATENCY_MS=`); receipt ≤ 5 ms; all Verify flags
OK; `grep -n "private(set) var jobs" Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift`
empty after P3-03b. (H-audit 2026-09-23) Cancel block cases 5–7 pass: 0 model calls for the four
new phrases with 1 or 2 tasks, the newest task cancelled and named; the newest result's first
1,150 characters reach the frontend and the whole work-status section is ≤ 2,500 characters.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: the failure/needs-input classification of worker strings
misfires (a completed answer ending in "?" becomes needsInput) — acceptable: the next user
turn is routed as a revision and the job continues; log each classification in the audit
detail so it can be tuned. Risk: tasks persisted by an older build lack `delivery` — decoded
as nil, treated as `.none`. Risk: ACP by voice starts a coding agent the user did not mean —
only an explicit harness name routes there (`explicitHarness(in:)`), exactly the typed rule.
Rollback: P3-03a can be reverted file by file (the coordinator's old `submit` is restored);
P3-03b's deletion of `jobs` is one commit — revert it alone if needed.

- [ ] P3-03a: `--selftest-tasks` voice rows green; `AgentTask.delivery` optional with `decodeIfPresent`

- [ ] P3-03b: ACP by voice through the router and the confirmation card; `--selftest-acp` case green

### P3-04 — OutputScheduler / AgentDelivery: the only thing that starts audio

*Migration steps 7 (P3-04a, deliveries) and 8 (P3-04b, all audio).*

**Finding(s):** C5, C9, C report §4 rank 7 and races 11–13, decision doc §3.5 #2–#4, E §1.4  **Effort:** 3–4 days (a: 2–2.5 d, b: 1.5 d)  **Depends on:** P3-03a, P3-06a (reducer state with zero divergences on floor/turnPending/output), P2-06 (first-audible-frame ack; see step 9 if not done)  **Unblocks:** P3-05, P3-08, P3-06b

**Why (what the user experiences today):** A finished background task speaks its raw result,
first two clauses verbatim, or the canned "There's an update from your background task. The
details are in the conversation." (VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift:45-50) — so "it's ready"
never sounds like conversation. It can start in the gap between the user's committed turn
and the frontend's `beginVoiceFrontend`, and is then cut off by the answer (C report Q4:
the flush runs on the 100 ms tick, Capture:1268, without checking the input barrier). A
result that finishes after the user said "Done" is dropped (`enqueue` guard, :12-15;
`endSession` clears the queue, Capture:360). An interrupted announcement replays its
remaining clauses verbatim (:29-33). Separately, a late non-streamed reply can truncate a
streaming answer, because any `RealtimeAudioSession.speak`/`beginSpokenReply` resets the one
speech buffer (RealtimeAudioSession.swift:146-175), and an announcement can inherit the
delivery ledger of whichever turn is current (VoicePlaybackDelivery.swift:28-29).

**Files you will change:**
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/OutputScheduler.swift` (types from §2.6, the class, `OutputToken`)
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/AgentDeliveryPrompts.swift` (result rules, facts formatter, fallback renderer)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/TaskBridge.swift` (enqueue deliveries; delivery state; carry-over load)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift` (`phraseDelivery`, serialized with the other FM calls)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Tasks/AgentTaskManager.swift` (`announce` no longer enqueues audio)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoicePlaybackDelivery.swift` (forward to OutputScheduler; bind by OutputID in 04b)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAudioSession.swift` (04b: token-gated output API)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent+ToolLoop.swift` (04b: `AgentToolSpeechTracker` uses an `OutputHandle`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift` (04b: `speakWithFirstAudioTrace` → `speakFixed`; `userSpeechStarted` marks a user barge-in)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift` (remove the three `VoiceAnnouncementQueue` calls: :360, :1256, :1268)
- delete `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift`
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceDeliverySelfTest.swift` (rewritten assertions), `VoiceConversationSelfTest.swift` (announcement block :81-93), `ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest.swift` (:66)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Persona/PersonaSelfTest.swift` (add the delivery prompt to `productionPaths`, :118-130)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/ActionRuntime.swift` (the `IslandState` extension, :888-918: new `showSessionlessResult(_:)` beside `showBackgroundAgentReply`) — H-audit 2026-09-23, D13
- `AGENTS.md`, `README.md` (replace the `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift` tree line, README.md:382)

**Read for context (do not change):**
- §2.6 of this file (every rule D1–D12 is implemented here).
- qwen reference: `server/src/voice/announcement/announcement-manager.mjs` (batching window,
  retries, `formatWorkResults` at the end of the file), `announcement-window.mjs` (the
  window predicate), `server/src/voice/realtime-presentation-runtime.mjs` lines 600-625

  (`resultResponseInstructions`), `server/src/delivery/agent-delivery.mjs` (the four modes;
  this plan drops `handle`, which is "deliver nothing to the model"), `server/src/core/config.mjs`
