- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Speech/AgentSpeechSynthesizer.swift` (`PlaybackEvent` :23-29; `.began` from `prepareForStream` :161; `.startAcknowledged` from `didBeginAudio` :317-322).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAudioSession.swift` (`noteUserSpeech` :435-451, `pauseForListening` :389, `resumeAfterListening` :411).

**Current behaviour:**
```swift
// Agent/VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift:38-52
func flush(userHasFloor: Bool) -> Bool {
    guard AgentCaptureController.shared.isSessionActive, !userHasFloor,
          !RealtimeAgent.shared.voiceInputActive, !RealtimeAgent.shared.isThinking,
          !RealtimeAudioSession.shared.isSpeaking,
          !AgentSpeechSynthesizer.shared.isSpeaking,
          inFlight == nil, !pending.isEmpty else { return false }
    let text = pending.removeFirst()
    let brief = AgentSpeechPolicy.spokenClauses(text).prefix(2).joined(separator: " ")
    let spoken = !brief.isEmpty && brief.count <= 300
        ? brief : "There's an update from your background task. The details are in the conversation."
    inFlight = AgentSpeechPolicy.spokenClauses(spoken)
    RealtimeAudioSession.shared.speak(spoken)
    return true
}
```
Producers of audio today: `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.flush` (:50), `RealtimeAgent.speakWithFirstAudioTrace`
(RealtimeAgent.swift:786-790, every non-streamed reply including "I'm on it."), and
`AgentToolSpeechTracker.receive/finish` (ToolLoop:120-136, the streamed answer). The only
enqueuer is `AgentTaskManager.announce` (AgentTaskManager.swift:311-320).
`VoiceDeliverySelfTest` currently asserts the old contract: an interrupted announcement is
retried with exactly its remaining clause (VoiceDeliverySelfTest.swift:68-77) and an old
session's announcement does **not** reach a new session (:79-85). Both assertions change.

**Target behaviour:**
1. Every rule D1–D12 of §2.6, and D13 (H-audit 2026-09-23), holds and is asserted by `--selftest-voice-delivery`.
2. A result that completes while the agent is answering is spoken only after that answer's
   playback has finished **and** 350 ms of quiet.
3. Two results that complete within 120 ms of each other are one utterance.
4. A result that completes after `endSession` is offered at the next `beginSession`, once.
5. A user who talks over a result is not told it again; any other stop re-phrases and retries
   (≤ 8 attempts, 1 s → 10 s backoff).
6. After P3-04b, no file other than `OutputScheduler.swift` can start audio (compile-time).
7. (H-audit 2026-09-23, D13) A result that finishes while no session is open appears on the
   island within one run-loop turn of `enqueue`, even during the session's closing card, and is
   still offered once at the next session (target 4). Today it can vanish from both: the
   island line is suppressed while the closing card is up, and speech drops it.

**Steps — P3-04a (deliveries):**
1. Create `Agent/Duplex/OutputScheduler.swift` with §2.6's types and constants. State:
   ```swift
   private var queue: [DeliveryItem] = []                  // pending, ordered by enqueuedAt
   private var active: (id: OutputID, items: [DeliveryItem], heard: Bool, byUser: Bool)?
   private var phraseTask: Task<Void, Never>?
   private var retryTimer: Task<Void, Never>?
   private var eligibleAt: [TaskID: Date] = [:]            // enqueuedAt + batchWindow (D2)
   ```
2. `enqueue(_:)`: append (replace an existing item for the same `TaskID`), set
   `eligibleAt = now + 120 ms`, tell TaskBridge `setDelivery([id], .pending)`, send
   `VoiceSession.shared.send(.task(.deliveryQueued(count: queue.count)))`, and schedule a
   one-shot 120 ms timer that sends `.timer(.quietWindow)` to VoiceSession (the reducer
   decides; row 23).
3. `windowOpened()` (from the reducer's `openDeliveryWindow`): return if `active != nil` or
   `phraseTask != nil`. Build the batch: eligible items (`eligibleAt <= now`), priority
   order approval → question → result (D10; approvals arrive in P3-08), oldest first, ≤ 8
   items and ≤ 2,400 characters of `facts` (always at least one item). Mark them
   `.delivering`. Start `phraseTask`:
   - approval prompt / question: text is deterministic (P3-08 / the question itself).
   - results: `let text = await phrase(batch)` with a 1.5 s bound (`withBoundedWait`); on
     timeout/error/empty → `DeliveryFallbackRenderer.spoken(batch)`.
   - Before speaking, re-check `VoiceSession.shared.state.deliveryWindowOpen(now:)`; if it
     closed meanwhile (the user started talking), put the items back to `.pending` without
     counting an attempt and return (D1).
   - Speak (step 6).
4. `phrase(_ batch:) async -> String?` calls
   `LocalVoiceFrontend.shared.phraseDelivery(instructions: AgentDeliveryPrompts.resultInstructions(carriedOver:),
   facts: AgentDeliveryPrompts.facts(batch), maxTokens: AgentDeliveryTiming.phraseMaxTokens)`.
   Add `phraseDelivery` to `LocalVoiceFrontend`: a fresh `LanguageModelSession(instructions:)`,
   `respond(to: facts, options: GenerationOptions(temperature: 0.3, maximumResponseTokens: maxTokens))`,
   run **through the same serialization as `stream`** (`waitForPriorInference` +
   `ComputeScheduler.acquire(.realtimeAgent)` + release, LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:280-316,
   :388) — FM inference is strictly serial in this actor and a phrase must not break that.
   Return nil when `FoundationModelFormatter.unavailableReason != nil`. Pass the result
   through `RealtimeAgent.voiceSafeReply` and reject it (→ fallback) if
   `AgentSpeechPolicy.spokenClauses(text)` is empty or it contains "task", "background",
   "tool", a digit run of ≥ 6, or `://`.
5. `AgentDeliveryPrompts` (new file):
   ```swift
   enum AgentDeliveryPrompts {
       static let resultRules = """
           This is the result of work the person asked for earlier. It is not a new request.
           Tell them the result in one or two short spoken sentences, as your own work.
           Open with the actual result, finding, problem or question. Never open with "okay",
           "got it", "done", "all set", "task complete" or anything like it.
           If there are several results, cover every one of them.
           If a result asks them for something, ask it plainly and stop.
           If the full details are on screen, give the gist and say where to look.
           Do not read out identifiers, file paths, web addresses or long numbers.
           Do not mention background work, tasks, tools or how the work was done.
           Do not add anything that is not in the results. Never call unfinished work finished.
           """
       static let carriedOverRule = """
           They were away when this finished. Begin with a short "While you were away," and continue.
           """
       static func resultInstructions(carriedOver: Bool) -> String {
           AgentPromptContext.assemble(.voiceAnswer,
               rules: carriedOver ? resultRules + "\n" + carriedOverRule : resultRules).system
       }
       /// The facts block, adapted from qwen's formatWorkResults: no ids, numbered items.
       static func facts(_ batch: [DeliveryItem]) -> String
       // "Results of work you started earlier (not a new request):\n1. Work: <title>. Outcome: done. Result: <facts>\n
       //  2. Work: <title>. Outcome: did not work. Problem: <facts>"
   }
   ```
   The rules describe a role and never name the Agent (I11); `AgentPromptContext.assemble(.voiceAnswer, …)`
   adds the persona short card and grounding, which is what makes the result sound like the
   same voice. Add `("voice delivery (Apple)", .voiceAnswer, AgentDeliveryPrompts.resultInstructions(carriedOver: false))`
   to `PersonaSelfTest`'s `productionPaths` so `--selftest-persona` and `PersonaCareEval`
   cover it.
   `DeliveryFallbackRenderer.spoken(_ batch:) -> String`: per item, "\(title) is done:
   \(first sentence of facts ≤ 160 chars)" / "\(title) didn't work: \(first sentence)" /
   the question verbatim; joined with " Also, "; carried-over prefix "While you were away, ";
   through `voiceSafeReply`. It never says "background task".
6. Speaking (04a): `let id = VoiceIDMint.shared.nextOutput()`; `active = (id, batch, false, false)`;
   send `.outputQueued(id, .delivery(batch.map(\.task)))` to VoiceSession; call
   `RealtimeAudioSession.shared.speak(text)` (04b replaces this with the token API). Record
   the synthesizer's `outputGeneration` observed in the following `.began(gen)` event as
   this output's generation.
7. `receive(_ event:)`: `VoicePlaybackDelivery.receive` (VoicePlaybackDelivery.swift:26-48)
   replaces its last line `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.shared.receive(event)` with
   `OutputScheduler.shared.receive(event)`. For the active delivery:
   - first `.startAcknowledged` → `heard = true`; `TaskBridge.setDelivery(ids, .delivered)` (D4);
     send `.playback(id, .firstAudio)`.
   - last `.completed` (no pending clauses) → send `.playback(id, .finished)`; `active = nil`.
   - `.interrupted` → if `active.byUser` → `.deliveredInContext` (D5); else → back to
     `.pending`, `attempts += 1`, `eligibleAt = now + min(1 s × 2^(attempts−1), 10 s)`,
     `.failed` after 8 (D6). Send `.playback(id, .interrupted(byUser:))`. `active = nil`.
8. **Who says "by user".** In `RealtimeAgent.userSpeechStarted()` (RealtimeAgent.swift:52-63),
   before `RealtimeAudioSession.shared.noteUserSpeech()`, call
   `OutputScheduler.shared.noteUserBargeIn()` which sets `active?.byUser = true`. Session
   close calls `OutputScheduler.shared.sessionClosed()` (from `AgentCaptureController.endSession`,
   replacing `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.shared.clear()` at Capture:360): an active delivery that
   was not heard goes back to `.pending` without an attempt; one that was heard is
   `.delivered`; nothing is dropped. The goodbye path (Capture:1256) does the same.
9. **D4 before P2-06.** If P2-06 has not moved `.startAcknowledged` to the first nonzero
   rendered sample, `.startAcknowledged` still fires from `didBeginAudio` (the backing's
   first-audio callback), which is at most one 80 ms frame late (A-latency Hop 10) — use it
   as is.
10. **Carry-over (D8).** TaskBridge persists `delivery` through `AgentTaskManager.mirror`
    (P3-03 step 2). On app launch (`TaskBridge.init`), for every `AgentTaskManager.tasks`
    row with `delivery == .pending` and a result/failure, finished less than 12 h ago:
    create a `DeliveryItem` with `carriedOver: true` and keep it queued; older → `.silent`.
    `sessionOpened()` (called from `beginSession`) marks every queued item `carriedOver: true`
    if it was enqueued in a previous session, and sends `.timer(.quietWindow)`.
11. **Producers.** In `TaskBridge.finishVoiceJob` and `backendStateChanged` (terminal or
    needsInput), decide the mode (D9): origin `.voice` → `respond` → `OutputScheduler.enqueue`;
    origin `.typed` with an open voice session → `respond`; `.scheduled`, `.meeting`,
    `.selfTest`, or no session → `context` (delivery `.silent`). `AgentTaskManager.announce`
    (:311-320) keeps its `recordAssistant(… contextKind: backgroundTask)` row (that row is
    what makes D5 "delivered in context" true), its audit row and its island line, and loses
    the `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.shared.enqueue(trimmed)` line.
12. Delete `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift`, the tick flush (Capture:1268-1271 — the next line's
    idle check stays), and the clears at Capture:360 and :1256. Update `ConcurrentVoiceSelfTest.swift:66`
    to assert `OutputScheduler.shared.pendingCount == 1` while the floor is held and that
    nothing was spoken, and `VoiceConversationSelfTest` lines 81-93 to the new rules (below).
12b. **After-session heads-up (D13)** (H-audit 2026-09-23, H3 #20). Verified 2026-09-24:
    `AgentTaskManager.announce` (AgentTaskManager.swift:311-320) calls
    `IslandState.shared.showBackgroundAgentReply(trimmed)` (:318), which returns early when
    `hasForegroundVoiceActivity` (kind is `.agentListening`, `.agentWorking` or `.agentReply`) or
    a `PermissionGate` request is pending (`IslandState` extension in `Agent/ActionRuntime.swift`,
    `showBackgroundAgentReply` at :909-913). `AgentCaptureController.endSession`
    (Capture:358) ends by pushing `showAgentReply("Going quiet." / "Stopped." / lastReply)`
    (:389-392), an `.agentReply` notice that lives `DS.Motion.islandNotice` = 8 s
    (`UI/DesignSystem.swift:784`). So a result finishing in those 8 s never reaches the island,
    and `VoiceAnnouncementQueue.enqueue` drops it from speech (`guard … isSessionActive`,
    VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift:12-15). Add:
    ```swift
    // Agent/ActionRuntime.swift, IslandState extension, beside showBackgroundAgentReply
    /// D13: a result that finished with no voice session open. The session's own closing
    /// card is not foreground activity; an approval card is.
    @MainActor
    func showSessionlessResult(_ text: String) {
        guard !AgentCaptureController.shared.isSessionActive,
              PermissionGate.shared.pending == nil else { return }
        showAgentReply(text)
    }
    ```
    In `OutputScheduler`: (i) in `enqueue`, when the item's mode is `respond` and
    `!AgentCaptureController.shared.isSessionActive`, schedule the batch as usual (120 ms, D2)
    and, when it becomes eligible, call
    `IslandState.shared.showSessionlessResult(DeliveryFallbackRenderer.spoken(batch))` instead of
    opening a window; leave every item `.pending` (D8 still offers it). (ii) In `sessionClosed()`
    (step 8), an active delivery that was not heard goes back to `.pending` **and** gets the same
    heads-up. Record one audit row per heads-up (`kind: .reply`, `detail: "delivery_heads_up"`).
    `AgentTaskManager.announce` keeps its `showBackgroundAgentReply` line for everything else (a
    typed task with no session is `context` mode, D9, and is not a D13 item). When P4-04's
    `agentHeadsUp` card and `DS.Motion.islandNoticeFor(text:)` land, `showSessionlessResult`
    switches to them; until then it uses `.agentReply` and the fixed 8 s. Never red: the island
    card is not a recording.

**Steps — P3-04b (all audio through the scheduler):**
13. In `OutputScheduler.swift` define:
    ```swift
    /// Only this file can mint one; RealtimeAudioSession's output methods require it (D11).
    struct OutputToken: Sendable { let output: OutputID; fileprivate init(_ output: OutputID) { self.output = output } }
    #if DEBUG
    extension OutputScheduler { static func tokenForTesting() -> OutputToken { precondition(SelfTest.isRunning); return OutputToken(VoiceIDMint.shared.nextOutput()) } }
    #endif
    ```
    (If self-tests run on release builds in this repo — `make install` builds release — drop
    the `#if DEBUG` and keep the `precondition(SelfTest.isRunning)`.)
    Change `RealtimeAudioSession.beginSpokenReply()`, `appendSpokenReply(_:)`,
    `finalizeSpokenReply()` and `speak(_:)` (RealtimeAudioSession.swift:146-175) to take
    `_ token: OutputToken` as their first parameter. The compiler now lists every producer.
14. `beginAnswer(turn:) -> OutputHandle`: if a delivery is active and not heard → stop it and
    requeue without an attempt (D10); if it was heard → treat as a user barge-in
    (`deliveredInContext`) only when the answer follows a committed user turn (it always
    does). Mint an `OutputID` and return it in the handle; the coordinator (later
    `FrontendResponder`) sends `.responseStarted(turn, handle.id)` to VoiceSession, whose row 14
    creates the output entry (`.outputQueued` is only for non-answer outputs). Return a
    handle whose `append`/`finish` call the token-gated `RealtimeAudioSession` methods.
    `AgentToolSpeechTracker` (ToolLoop:74-160) receives its `OutputHandle` from the
    coordinator for voice-frontend turns and calls `handle.append(delta)` / `handle.finish()`
    instead of `RealtimeAudioSession.shared.beginSpokenReply()/appendSpokenReply/finalizeSpokenReply`.
    Typed turns never get a handle (they never speak while a session is open — asserted in
    `--selftest-toolloop-production`).
15. `speakFixed(_ text:, turn:) -> OutputID?`: drop it (return nil) when `turn` is older than
    the reducer's current `response?.turn`/`turnPending` (a late reply for a superseded turn
    must not truncate the current answer — C race 13). Otherwise, if an answer for the same
    turn is streaming, append after it; else start it now. `RealtimeAgent.speakWithFirstAudioTrace`
    (RealtimeAgent.swift:786-790) calls it.
16. **Bind by OutputID (D12).** `VoicePlaybackDelivery.bind(messageID:turn:)`
    (VoicePlaybackDelivery.swift:59-63) becomes `bind(messageID: UUID, output: OutputID)`;
    `receive(.began)` no longer reads `RealtimeAgent.shared.currentGeneration`; OutputScheduler
    tells it which `OutputID` a `.began(gen)` belongs to (`noteBegan(output:generation:)`).
    `RealtimeAgent.finish` (RealtimeAgent.swift:758-760) passes the `OutputID` that
    `speakFixed`/the answer handle returned.
17. The self-tests that call `session.speak(...)` directly (`VoiceDeliverySelfTest`,
    `RealtimeAudioSession.runSelfTest` for `--selftest-duplex`) use `OutputScheduler.tokenForTesting()`.

**Do not:**
- Do not start audio anywhere but `OutputScheduler` (D11) — not even "just this one fixed reply".
- Do not speak progress (D9) and do not add a progress-narration timer in this phase.
- Do not replay a fragment after an interruption: a user interruption is delivered-in-context; any other stop is re-phrased from the facts (decision doc §3.5 #3).
- Do not acknowledge a delivery on enqueue, on generation or on the last clause; only on the first audible frame (§3.5 #4).
- Do not deliver while any turn is unclassified or the user holds the floor (D1) — `interrupt` mode never cuts the user.
- Do not name the Agent or use developer words in the delivery prompt or fallback text (I11; `--selftest-ui-strings`, `PersonaCareEval` plain-words rule).
- Do not break FM serialization: `phraseDelivery` goes through the frontend actor's drain barrier and `.realtimeAgent` lane like every other FM call (AGENTS: "The on-device model's single native context must be reserved before acquiring its compute scheduler ticket"; FM takes only the lane).
- Do not remove the `backgroundTask` conversation row in `announce` — D5 depends on it.
- Do not drop pending deliveries on session close (D8).
- Do not mark a D13 heads-up as `delivered`: an island card is not proof anybody read it, and D8 must still offer the item once (H-audit 2026-09-23). Do not call the frontend model to phrase a heads-up when no session is open, and do not open the microphone or a session to deliver it.
- Do not show a D13 card over a pending approval card, and do not use `DS.Color.record` or any red on it (red means recording only).

**Red-first self-test:** `--selftest-voice-delivery` (`Agent/VoiceDeliverySelfTest.swift`),
rewritten. Keep the first block (clause ledger, :24-54), P0-09's per-clause-cap case (600-character
reply fully enqueued) and P2-06's fake streaming-stage case (synth-ahead order); lines will have
moved since this was written, so find the old announcement cases by grepping
`VoiceDeliverySelfTest.swift` for the remaining-clause retry and the cross-session assertions.
Replace only those old announcement cases (:56-86 on 2026-09-23) with these cases, each
driven through the real `OutputScheduler` + `RecordingSpeechBacking` with
`synth.notifyTestingFirstAudio/AudioFinished(token:)` to simulate playback, a stubbed
`OutputScheduler.phraseForTesting: (([DeliveryItem]) async -> String?)?` seam (returns
"Your calendar check found two meetings." etc. or nil for the fallback case), and a
`VoiceSession`-driven clock (`VoiceSession.shared.nowForTesting`) so 350 ms is simulated, not
slept:
1. **Mid-answer completion waits for playback + 350 ms.** Start an answer output for turn t
   (two clauses). Enqueue a result. Assert nothing new is spoken while the answer plays;
   finish the answer at T; at T+300 ms nothing; at T+360 ms the phrased result is spoken.
   Failure: `"result spoke before playback end + 350 ms"`.
2. **Batching.** Two results enqueued 50 ms apart → exactly one spoken output whose phrase
   input contains both titles. Failure: `"simultaneous completions were not batched"`.
3. **Delivered on first audible frame.** Before `notifyTestingFirstAudio` the record is
   `.delivering`; after it `.delivered`. Failure: `"delivery acknowledged before first audio"`.
4. **User barge-in ⇒ delivered in context.** During a delivery, `agent.userSpeechStarted()`;
   the record becomes `.deliveredInContext` and no later window re-speaks it. Failure:
   `"user-interrupted result was replayed"`.
5. **Other stop ⇒ retry, re-phrased.** During a delivery, `capture.endSession()` then
   `beginSession()`; the item returns to `.pending` with `attempts == 1` (not heard) or is
   `.delivered` (heard) and, when pending, is spoken again from a new phrase call (the phrase
   seam's call count increments). Failure: `"non-user stop dropped or replayed a fragment"`.
6. **Carry-over.** Enqueue a result while no session is open (`endSession` first): nothing
   spoken; `beginSession()` → after the quiet window it is spoken exactly once, and the phrase
   instructions contain "While you were away". Failure: `"post-endSession result was not offered at the next session"`.
7. **Status consumes.** A pending result + a status answer (`TaskBridge.consumeDeliveries`) →
   `.consumedByStatus` and never spoken. Failure: `"status-consumed result was spoken again"`.
8. **Fallback.** Phrase seam returns nil → the fallback sentence is spoken and contains the
   title and no "background task". Failure: `"no fallback when phrasing failed"`.
9. (04b) **Only the scheduler speaks.** A fixed reply for an older turn while a newer answer
   streams is dropped and the answer's clauses are all spoken. Failure: `"late fixed reply truncated the current answer"`.
10. (04b) **Distinct ledgers.** An answer following a delivery binds a different `OutputID`
    and its conversation row's `speechDelivery` is unaffected by the delivery's clauses.
    Failure: `"delivery and answer shared a playback ledger"`.
11. (04a, H-audit 2026-09-23, D13) **After-session heads-up.** `await capture.endSession(source: .idle)`
    (the closing card "Going quiet." is now up); then enqueue a voice-origin result "Checked your
    calendar: two meetings tomorrow." Assert, after the 120 ms batch (simulated clock):
    `IslandState.shared.kind == .agentReply(text)` with `text` containing "calendar"; the record's
    delivery is still `.pending`; the recorder spoke nothing; one audit row with
    `delivery_heads_up`. Then `beginSession()` → spoken exactly once after the quiet window
    (shares case 6's assertion). Also: with a `PermissionGate` request pending (use the test
    seam P3-08 uses, or `PermissionGate.shared.present` with a fixture request and cancel it in
    `defer`), no heads-up is shown and the item is still `.pending`. Failures:
    `"post-session result was not shown on the island"`, `"heads-up marked the result delivered"`,
    `"heads-up covered an approval card"`. Reset the island with `IslandState.shared.apply(.hidden)`
    in `defer`.
Markers unchanged: `VOICE_DELIVERY_OK` / `VOICE_DELIVERY_FAILED: <reason>`.
**Confirm red:** before implementing, add cases 1, 2, 4 and 6 to the *current* test using
today's API (`VoiceAnnouncementQueue.enqueue` / `flush(userHasFloor:)`, with the answer's
playback simulated by the recorder tokens). They fail on the unmodified code, each for a
measured reason: case 1 — `flush(userHasFloor: false)` speaks on the first call after the
answer finishes, with no 350 ms hold; case 2 — two enqueued results produce two outputs;
case 4 — the interrupted announcement is retried (today's contract, :68-77); case 6 — the
post-`endSession` result is dropped (`enqueue` guard, VoiceAnnouncementQueue.swift:12-15).
Record the four failure lines in STATUS.md, then replace the API and port the cases.
(H-audit 2026-09-23) Case 11 is red on today's API too: `await capture.endSession(source: .idle)`,
then `IslandState.shared.showBackgroundAgentReply("Checked your calendar: two meetings tomorrow.")`
(what `AgentTaskManager.announce` does) leaves `kind == .agentReply("Going quiet.")` —
`"post-session result was not shown on the island"`.

**Verify:**
```bash
make build
make install OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-delivery
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-playback-ledger
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-duplex
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-conversation
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-concurrent-voice
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turns
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-toolloop-production
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-persona
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-tts-pocket-session
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-barge <wav>     # the file the Phase 2 tasks use; barge-in must stay green
make acceptance TIER=core
```

**Success threshold:** all ten delivery cases pass, plus case 11 (H-audit 2026-09-23: heads-up shown, still pending, spoken once at the next session, never over an approval card); `grep -rn "VoiceAnnouncementQueue" Sources/`
empty; after 04b, `grep -rn "RealtimeAudioSession.shared.speak(\|beginSpokenReply(" Sources/NextNotes | grep -v OutputScheduler.swift | grep -v SelfTest`
empty; `--selftest-voice-barge` onset→pause unchanged within ±20 ms of its Phase 2 value.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: a phrased result invents facts — the rules forbid it and the
fallback triggers on suspicious output, but a 3B model can still embellish; the facts block
is short and numbered to reduce it; review a sample in the P1-01 live eval. Risk: a
delivery never fires because the reducer's window predicate is stuck (e.g. `turnPending`
never cleared) — the 120 s acknowledgement timeout constant is logged when exceeded, and
`--selftest-voice-session-reducer` pins the clearing rows. Risk: the token API change touches
many call sites — the compiler lists them; do not add a public initializer to get around it.
Rollback: 04a and 04b are separate commits; revert 04b alone to return to direct speaking
with scheduled deliveries.

- [ ] P3-04a: OutputScheduler delivers results per D1–D10; VoiceAnnouncementQueue deleted; carry-over persisted
- [ ] P3-04a (H-audit 2026-09-23): D13 after-session heads-up (`showSessionlessResult`); `--selftest-voice-delivery` case 11 red → green
- [ ] P3-04a: delivery prompt in PersonaSelfTest; `--selftest-persona` OK
- [ ] P3-04b: `OutputToken` required; tracker, fixed replies and deliveries all go through the scheduler; ledger bound by OutputID
- [ ] `--selftest-voice-delivery` ten cases green; regression flags OK
- [ ] AGENTS.md: replace the announcement description with the delivery rules (D1, D4, D5, D8 in one paragraph); README tree updated

### P3-05 — Receipt-first frontend contract and no-filler prompt rules

**Bounded update, 2026-10-04:** actual324 reports59 missing-feature/critical-fidelity failures across84.325 passes84 but introduces legacy D8 original-field corruption, retained as FAILED. The SAME corrected F05 checks exact original AND raw-containing prompt before326/327, leaving all84 inputs and D8 unchanged.326 RED2 →327 same84/whole routing PASS and9 focused related flags including authority/durability/isolation. SAME Work retains immutable optional raw words and formats only its existing prompt; no added model pass/Task/store. Model-backed324/327 both fail the same two calendar inputs; measured answer first-text-41…612ms is not isolated100ms route overhead, and full adapter/four-tool/revision/answer/cloud/live/P2 acceptance remains open. [Evidence](../../../Tests/Reports/agent-overhaul-closure/p3-task-bridge-production/manager-voice-worker-ownership-production/verification.md).


*Migration step 9.*

**Finding(s):** E §1.2, §1.7, §1.8 patterns 1, 6, 7; decision doc §2 ("a receipt-first tool contract with prompt discipline") and §3.5 #7 (filler)  **Effort:** 2 days  **Depends on:** P3-03a (receipts), P3-04a (result rules), P2-02 (one streamed decision — see step 3 if not done)  **Unblocks:** P3-08, P3-10 (H-audit 2026-09-23), P5-01

**Why (what the user experiences today):** Delegation replies are a fixed "I'm on it."
(Coordinator:354, :366, :377, :494, :515) that never says *what* the agent is doing, and the
background worker receives the user's raw words with no resolution of "it"/"that"
(`submit(text)`, Coordinator:514), so "send it to Marcus" reaches the planner without the
thing "it" was. The answer lane is told to be warm and helpful but not told to avoid filler;
under the self-test harness answers open with "Hey there, I hear you. That's a beautiful way
to think —" (A-latency Hop 9), which delays meaningful audio by one or two clauses. Status
and cancellation contracts ("only say *cancelling* until it is confirmed", "never guess
status from history") exist nowhere.

- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/FrontendResponder.swift` (`actor FrontendResponder`, `FrontendDecision` adapter, `ReceiptAck`, `ObjectiveFidelity`, `FrontendToolContract`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceDecision.swift` (route fields and rules; answer rules)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift` (emit one `<decision>{json}</decision>` control chunk)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift` (`respond` switches on `FrontendDecision`; `VoiceFrontendEnvelope` gains `.decision`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/TaskBridge.swift` (objective + user words into the worker's work item)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.swift` (receipt block in `runTurnRouting`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontendSelfTest.swift` (live routing fixtures for the new fields)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/LiveEval/ToolLoopLiveEvalCases.swift` (P1-01's live eval: delegation-reply cases, appended with `scored: false`)
- `AGENTS.md`

**Read for context (do not change):**
- qwen reference: `roadmap/references/qwen-audio-agent-main/config/frontend-agent/PROMPT.md`
  (sections "Routing", "Background work", "Permission requests", "Voice interaction" — in
  Chinese; E §1.7 has a translation), `config/frontend-agent/ASSISTANT.md` ("say the key point
  first"), `server/src/frontend/tools/spawn-thinking-tool.mjs` (the objective's field
  description: faithful, complete, self-contained; the backend never sees chat history),
  `server/src/frontend/tools/features/agent-task-tools.mjs` (status/cancel/permission tool
  descriptions), `server/src/frontend/frontend-tools.mjs` (`resultResponseInstructions`,
  `permissionResponseInstructions`), `docs/frontend-agent-roadmap.md` §三 (the admission rule
  for a frontend tool: < 2 s, no multi-step orchestration, frontend-owned state, low risk).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Persona/AgentPromptContext.swift` (`AgentPromptPath.voiceRoute` carries
  no persona; `.voiceAnswer` carries the short card).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Persona/PersonaCareEval.swift` (plain words, no flattery — the answer
  rules below must still pass it).

**Current behaviour:**
- `LocalVoiceRoute` (LocalVoiceDecision.swift:61-77) has `intent` and `taskNumber` only; the
  route rules (:118-137) do not ask for an objective or an acknowledgement.
- `LocalVoiceFrontend.streamSplitDecision` (LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:483-551) collects the
  route and yields an envelope string (`<use_tools/>`, `<revise id="N"/>`, …) that the
  coordinator re-parses (`VoiceFrontendEnvelope.parse`, Coordinator:782-807).
- Every work route replies "I'm on it." and submits the raw text.
- `answerRules` (LocalVoiceDecision.swift:145-156) ask for natural, brief, warm answers and
  forbid technical words, but say nothing about openings, filler, repeating the question,
  or reading ids/paths/URLs aloud.

**Target behaviour:**
1. One structured decision per turn carries the intent, and for work also a self-contained
   `objective` and a one-clause `acknowledgement`. No extra model pass for the receipt.
2. On `.spawn`, the coordinator calls `TaskBridge.spawn` (receipt in ms) and speaks exactly
   one clause: the guarded model acknowledgement, or "I'm on it." when the guard rejects it.
3. The worker receives the objective only when it passes `ObjectiveFidelity`; otherwise the
   user's own words. Both are always recorded on the task.
4. Answers open with the answer, with no filler; no ids, paths or URLs are spoken.
5. `FrontendToolContract` defines the four frontend tools (`spawn_task`, `task_status`,
   `cancel_task`, `respond_approval`) as JSON schemas, used by Phase 5's cloud frontends and
   by the on-device route through the same `FrontendDecision`.
6. P1-01's live eval shows the reply to a delegation is ≤ 1 clause with no waiting filler.

**Steps:**
1. **Admission rule** — (the §1.3 diagram's "cheap reads" are not built in this phase; if they are
   added later they come from `AgentCapabilityManifest.frontendTools` (P1-03), never from a second
   list) — write it as the doc comment of `FrontendToolContract` and in
   AGENTS.md: "The conversational frontend may do something itself only when it takes
   < 2 s, is one call, reads or changes only the frontend's own state, and is low risk.
   Everything else is a task (`spawn_task`)." The four frontend tools qualify; mail,
   calendar, files and every write do not.
2. **Route fields.** Extend `LocalVoiceRoute` (or the P2-02 decision type):
   ```swift
   @Generable(description: "The route for one voice turn in this app.")
   struct LocalVoiceRoute {
       @Generable(description: "What the application should do with the latest turn.")
       enum Intent { case answerQuestion, describeCapabilities, startExternalTask,
                     reviseRunningTask, cancelRunningTask, checkTaskStatus }   // P3-08 adds answerApproval
       @Guide(description: "See the routing rules.") var intent: Intent
       @Guide(description: "The exact task number from the work status, only for revise, cancel or status of one task. Empty otherwise.")
       var taskNumber: Int?
       @Guide(description: "Only for startExternalTask or reviseRunningTask: one self-contained instruction that says faithfully what the person wants, with words like it, that, him or there replaced by what they refer to. Empty otherwise.")
       var objective: String
       @Guide(description: "Only for startExternalTask: one short natural clause, at most eight words, confirming what you are doing. Empty otherwise.")
       var acknowledgement: String
   }
   ```
   Raise `LocalVoiceSplitResponse.routeMaximumResponseTokens` (:158) from 64 to 128 (the
   objective needs room; answer turns still stop early because both strings are empty for
   them). Record the route latency before/after with `--selftest-voice-frontend
   --voice-frontend-routing-only` in STATUS.md; the answer route must not slow by more than
   100 ms (estimate of the cost of two empty strings).
3. **One control chunk.** In `streamSplitDecision` replace the envelope yields
   (LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:516-533) with one JSON chunk:
   ```swift
   struct FrontendDecisionPayload: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
       var intent: String            // "answer" | "capabilities" | "spawn" | "revise" | "cancel" | "status" | "approval"
       var taskNumber: Int?
       var objective: String?
       var acknowledgement: String?
       var approval: String?         // P3-08: "approve" | "deny"
       var amendment: String?        // P3-08
   }
   // yield "<decision>" + JSON + "</decision>"
   ```
   For `answerQuestion`, yield `<decision>{"intent":"answer"}</decision>` and then the answer
   stream as today (the coordinator speaks what follows the closing tag). Extend
   `VoiceFrontendEnvelope.parse` (Coordinator:782-807) with
   `case decision(FrontendDecisionPayload, trailing: String)`: when the snapshot starts with
   `<decision>`, return `.pending` until `</decision>` is present, then decode the JSON with
   `JSONDecoder` (invalid JSON → `.invalid`), and return the text after the tag as `trailing`.
   Keep the old envelopes as accepted aliases so scripted tests keep working. If P2-02
   already streams a typed decision, emit this same payload from it instead.
4. **FrontendDecision adapter** in `FrontendResponder.swift`:
   `static func decision(from payload: FrontendDecisionPayload, userWords: String, indexed: [TaskRecord]) -> FrontendDecision?`
   — maps `taskNumber` through `TaskBridge.indexedSessionTasks()` to a `TaskID`
   (out of range → nil, which the coordinator answers with "That task has already finished.
   What would you like me to do next?" as today, Coordinator:521), and `"spawn"` to
   `.spawn(objective: payload.objective ?? "", userWords: userWords, backend: nil)`.
5. **`actor FrontendResponder`** — a thin owner over `LocalVoiceFrontend` so the coordinator
   stops touching the engine directly:
   ```swift
   actor FrontendResponder {
       static let shared = FrontendResponder()
       func stream(system: String, messages: [LLMChatMessage], maxTokens: Int,
                   commitRevision: UInt64) async -> AsyncThrowingStream<String, Error>   // today's LocalVoiceFrontend.stream
       func speculate(system: String, messages: [LLMChatMessage], maxTokens: Int, revision: UInt64) async
       func cancelSpeculation(through revision: UInt64) async
       func phrase(_ batch: [DeliveryItem], carriedOver: Bool) async -> String?          // moves from P3-04a's call site
   }
   ```
   Replace the coordinator's `LocalVoiceFrontend.shared.stream/speculate/cancelSpeculation`
   calls (Coordinator:174, :180, :191, :433) and OutputScheduler's `phraseDelivery` call with
   it. (P5-01 later turns `FrontendResponder` into a protocol with this surface for cloud
   frontends and renames this actor `LocalFrontendResponder`; do not add the protocol now.)
6. **Coordinator on decisions.** In `respond`, where the envelope is resolved
   (`VoiceFrontendResponseOutcome.resolve`, :472-536), handle `.decision` by converting to
   `FrontendDecision` and switching:
   - `.answer` → as today (stream `trailing` + following deltas to the tracker).
   - `.capabilities` → as today (:500-504).
   - `.spawn(objective, userWords, backend)` →
     ```swift
     let accepted = ObjectiveFidelity.accepts(objective, userWords: userWords) ? objective : userWords
     let receipt = TaskBridge.shared.spawn(objective: accepted, userWords: userWords, origin: .voice(…), backend: backend)
     resolveInput(epoch: inputEpoch)
     return agent.finishVoiceFrontend(ReceiptAck.reply(for: receipt, modelAck: payload.acknowledgement),
                                      turn: turn, streamed: false)
     ```
   - `.revise(id, text)` → `TaskBridge.shared.revise(id, text: text, turn:)`; reply
     "Got it. I'll use that correction." (unchanged).
   - `.cancel(id)` → `let state = await TaskBridge.shared.cancel(id, reason: .spoken)`; reply
     "I stopped that task." when `.cancelled`, "Stopping that now." when `.cancelling` (never
     claim it stopped before it did — qwen PROMPT.md "Background work"); the later
     `.cancelled` event is recorded in context only (mode `context`).
   - `.status(id)` → `TaskStatusRenderer.spoken(TaskBridge.shared.status(id, …))`, no answer pass.
   - `.respondPermission` → P3-08 (until then: treat as `.answer` path fallback — re-run as
     an ordinary answer is not possible without a second pass, so reply "Please use the
     buttons on screen for that one." and log).
   The deterministic gates (pending intent, tool shape, denial backstop, naming turn) produce
   `.spawn(objective: text, userWords: text, backend: nil)` with no model acknowledgement
   ("I'm on it.").
7. **`ReceiptAck.reply(for:modelAck:)`**:
   ```swift
   enum ReceiptAck {
       static let maxWords = 8
       static let fallback = "I'm on it."
       static let banned = ["wait", "hold on", "moment", "second", "minute", "shortly", "soon",
                            "let you know", "get back to you", "stay tuned", "thanks for", "thank you",
                            "great question", "done", "finished", "complete", "all set",
                            "task", "background", "tool", "://"]
       static func accept(_ ack: String?) -> String? {
           guard let text = ack?.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines), !text.isEmpty else { return nil }
           let lower = text.lowercased()
           guard text.split(whereSeparator: \.isWhitespace).count <= maxWords,
                 AgentSpeechPolicy.spokenClauses(text).count == 1, !text.hasSuffix("?"),
                 !banned.contains(where: lower.contains),
                 lower.range(of: #"\d{3,}"#, options: .regularExpression) == nil else { return nil }
           return text
       }
       static func reply(for receipt: TaskReceipt, modelAck: String?) -> String {
           switch receipt.status {
           case .accepted: accept(modelAck) ?? fallback
           case .duplicate: "I'm already on that."
           case .refusedApprovalPending: "First, there's something waiting for your OK."   // P3-08 replaces with the spoken prompt
           case .refusedNeedsConfirmation: "That needs your OK on screen first."
           case .refusedUnavailable(let reason): reason
           }
       }
   }
   ```
8. **`ObjectiveFidelity.accepts(_ objective:, userWords:) -> Bool`** — true only when:
   objective is non-empty and ≤ 400 characters; every *significant* token of the user's
   words appears in the objective (case-insensitive; a token is ≥ 3 letters/digits, not in
   `["the","and","for","with","please","can","could","would","you","your","me","my","it","its","that","this","these","those","them","him","her","his","there","then","just","also","now","again"]`;
   compare after stripping a trailing "s"); and it contains no `://`. Otherwise false
   (audit detail `objective_rejected`). This is the safety net for qwen's rule "do not omit,
   infer or change the user's meaning" on a 3B model.
9. **Worker input.** In `TaskBridge.spawn`, when `objective != userWords`, construct the
   work item as `VoiceConversationWork("\(objective)\n(The person's exact words: \(userWords))")`
   so the planner sees both; store both on the `TaskRecord`. (Dropping chat history from the
   worker is P3-07c.)
10. **Prompt text.** Replace `LocalVoiceSplitResponse.routeRules` (:118-137) with the route
    rules below and append the no-filler block to `answerRules` (:145-156). The texts are
    adapted from qwen's `PROMPT.md` (translated, shortened, and fitted to this app's rules:
    no Agent name, plain words, no promise of speed).

    **Route rules** (`AgentPromptPath.voiceRoute`: no persona, no memory):
    ```text
    You are the routing layer of a voice assistant on this Mac that can talk and work at the same time.
    Classify the latest user turn into exactly one route.
    - answerQuestion: an ordinary question, small talk, advice, or a question about one feature.
    - describeCapabilities: only when they ask for an overview of what the assistant can do.
    - startExternalTask: they want something done or looked up on this Mac or in their accounts —
      mail, calendar, files, apps, web pages, reminders, what to remember — including requests
      phrased as questions, such as "what's on my calendar tomorrow?". If it can be done, route it
      here; never answer that it cannot be done.
    - checkTaskStatus: they ask how running or recent work is going, whether it is done, or what is
      being worked on. Never start the same work again.
    - reviseRunningTask: a correction or addition to a running task. Give its task number.
    - cancelRunningTask: an explicit request to stop a running task. Give its task number.
    Use only task numbers from the supplied work status; never invent one.
    For startExternalTask and reviseRunningTask, write objective: one self-contained instruction
    that says faithfully what they want. Replace words like it, that, him or there with what they
    refer to in the conversation. Keep every name, date, time, number and condition they gave.
    Do not add steps, tools or details they did not ask for.
    For startExternalTask, write acknowledgement: one short natural clause, at most eight words,
    saying what you are doing, for example "Checking your calendar for tomorrow." Do not say how
    long it will take, do not ask them to wait, and do not say it is done.
    Leave objective and acknowledgement empty for every other route.
    ```

    **Answer rules — append to `answerRules`** (`AgentPromptPath.voiceAnswer`):
    ```text
    Open with the answer itself. Do not greet, do not say "sure" or "great question", do not repeat
    the question, do not thank them for waiting and do not promise updates. When there is nothing
    new to say, say nothing more than the answer. Do not read out identifiers, file paths, web
    addresses, codes or long numbers unless they ask for the exact value. If they ask about work in
    progress, say in one sentence what it is doing now, from the work status; never call unfinished
    work finished.
    ```

    **Result rules** — added by P3-04a as `AgentDeliveryPrompts.resultRules`; reproduced here
    so the whole frontend contract is in one place. Keep the two copies identical (P3-04a's
    file is the source):
    ```text
    This is the result of work the person asked for earlier. It is not a new request.
    Tell them the result in one or two short spoken sentences, as your own work.
    Open with the actual result, finding, problem or question. Never open with "okay",
    "got it", "done", "all set", "task complete" or anything like it.
    If there are several results, cover every one of them.
    If a result asks them for something, ask it plainly and stop.
    If the full details are on screen, give the gist and say where to look.
    Do not read out identifiers, file paths, web addresses or long numbers.
    Do not mention background work, tasks, tools or how the work was done.
    Do not add anything that is not in the results. Never call unfinished work finished.
    ```
    Status answers (P3-03) and approval prompts (P3-08) are deterministic and have no prompt.
11. **`FrontendToolContract`** (for P5 cloud frontends; also the reference for the fields
    above). JSON-schema definitions, descriptions in plain English:
    - `spawn_task(objective: string, required)` — "Start work that needs this Mac or the
      person's accounts: mail, calendar, files, apps, the web, reminders, memory, or a coding
      agent. Returns a receipt at once; the work continues in the background and its result
      comes back later as a separate message. The objective must say faithfully and
      completely what they want, self-contained, with references like 'it' resolved; the
      worker does not see this conversation. Do not speak before calling it. After the
      receipt, confirm once in one short clause and call no more tools."
    - `task_status(task: integer, optional)` — "How running or recent work is going, from the
      task list. Use it whenever they ask about progress, completion or what is running;
      never guess from the conversation."
    - `cancel_task(task: integer, optional)` — "Stop a running task. Call it without
      speaking first. Until it returns, only say that you are stopping it."
    - `respond_approval(decision: "approve" | "deny", change: string, optional)` — "Answer the
      approval that is waiting right now. Only offered while one is pending. Never approve
      unless they clearly said yes; a yes with a change is a deny with the change."
    Store them as Codable values — `struct FrontendToolSchema: Codable, Sendable { let name: String;
    let description: String; let parameters: [FrontendToolParameter] }` — never as
    `[String: Any]`, so they stay `Sendable` and P5-01 can encode them to the OpenAI and Gemini
    formats.
12. AGENTS.md, new paragraph after "Live conversation has a different model owner":
    "**The frontend answers or hands off; it never works.** A work route becomes a
    `TaskBridge` receipt in milliseconds and one spoken clause (the model's acknowledgement if
    it passes `ReceiptAck`, else 'I'm on it.'). The worker gets the model's objective only if
    `ObjectiveFidelity` finds every significant word the person said in it; otherwise their own
    words."

**Do not:**
- Do not add a second model pass for the acknowledgement or the objective; they come from the one route decision.
- Do not let a deterministic gate subtract: the tool-shape, pending-intent and status gates route to work or facts; everything else reaches the model (I5).
- Do not name the Agent or the app in any prompt (I11; `--selftest-persona` fails if a production prompt names it). Describe a role.
- Do not add technical words to spoken text or allow ids/paths/URLs through the acknowledgement guard.
- Do not change `Resources/agent-persona-base.md` or `PersonaStore.builtInBaseText` here — they must stay byte-identical (AGENTS.md) and are Phase 4's (Q8).
- Do not send the objective to a cloud reader or change consent: this task is on-device only.
- Do not claim a cancellation before `TaskBridge.cancel` confirms it.

**Red-first self-test:** `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` gains a **receipt block**
(scripted `<decision>` JSON through `streamForTesting`, `workerForTesting` recording the
work item's `original`):
1. `{"intent":"spawn","objective":"Check the calendar for tomorrow","acknowledgement":"Checking your calendar for tomorrow."}`
   for "what's on my calendar tomorrow" → reply == "Checking your calendar for tomorrow.";
   `TurnRoutingProbe.streamCalls` increased by exactly 1 (no second pass); receipt time
   ≤ 5 ms. Failure: `"model acknowledgement ignored or cost a second pass"`.
2. Ack "Sure! Give me a few seconds to check that for you." → reply == "I'm on it." Failure:
   `"filler acknowledgement was spoken"`.
3. User words "send the pricing sheet to Marcus", objective "Send the file" → rejected; the
   worker's `original` equals the user words. Failure: `"unfaithful objective reached the worker"`.
4. After a conversation that mentions "the pricing sheet", user words "send it to Marcus",
   objective "Send the pricing sheet to Marcus" → accepted; the worker's `original` contains
   both the objective and "(The person's exact words: send it to Marcus)". Failure:
   `"faithful objective was rejected"`.
5. `{"intent":"status"}` → deterministic status reply, no answer pass. Failure: `"status route produced an answer pass"`.
6. `{"intent":"cancel","taskNumber":1}` for a job whose worker ignores cancellation for 3 s →
   reply "Stopping that now." (never "I stopped"). Failure: `"cancellation claimed before confirmation"`.
7. Filler check on answers is a live-model property: add to P1-01's live eval
   (`Agent/LiveEval/ToolLoopLiveEvalCases.swift`, as `scored: false` extra cases) the cases "check my calendar for tomorrow" and
   "remind me at 9 to call Anna" graded as: reply has exactly one spoken clause
   (`AgentSpeechPolicy.spokenClauses`) and contains none of `ReceiptAck.banned`; and "what is a
   haiku?" graded as: first clause is not a greeting/filler (`["hey", "sure", "great question", "i hear you", "of course"]`).
Also extend `--selftest-voice-frontend --voice-frontend-routing-only` (real FM; prints
`VOICE_FRONTEND_MISSING_WORKER_MODEL` then fails when models are missing, as today) with two
fixtures: "Check my calendar for tomorrow." must route to spawn with a non-empty objective
containing "calendar" and an acknowledgement accepted by `ReceiptAck.accept`; "How's the
calendar check going?" with a running task in the work status must route to status.
**Confirm red:** cases 1, 3, 4, 5, 6 fail to decode today (`<decision>` is `.invalid`) — run
the block before step 3 and record `VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_FAILED` with those reasons.

**Verify:**
```bash
make build
make install OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turn-routing
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turns
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-persona
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-concurrent-voice
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-duplex-work
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-delivery
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-frontend --voice-frontend-routing-only
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-toolloop-live          # P1-01's live eval; *_ABSENT without a model
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-ui-strings
make acceptance TIER=core
```

**Success threshold:** receipt block 6/6; live eval: both delegation cases one clause, no
banned words, and the haiku answer opens without filler; route latency for answer turns
within +100 ms of the pre-change median (5 runs); `--selftest-persona` OK.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: the 3B model writes poor objectives — `ObjectiveFidelity` falls
back to the user's words, and both are on the record for review. Risk: the added fields slow
the route — measured in step 2; if answer turns slow by > 100 ms, move `objective` and
`acknowledgement` into a second tiny structured pass run **only** for `startExternalTask`
(it replaces the answer pass that work turns do not run). Rollback: the envelope aliases
still parse, so reverting `LocalVoiceFrontend` alone restores the old routes.

**Done when:**
- [ ] one `<decision>` chunk carries intent, task number, objective and acknowledgement
- [ ] `FrontendResponder` owns every frontend call from the coordinator and OutputScheduler
- [ ] `ReceiptAck`, `ObjectiveFidelity`, `FrontendToolContract` exist with the behaviour above
- [ ] route and answer prompt text replaced/extended exactly as above; persona self-test OK
- [ ] receipt block red → green; live-eval cases added and passing; STATUS.md has route latency before/after
- [ ] AGENTS.md paragraph added

### P3-06 — VoiceSession reducer and VoiceEvent stream; TurnID / OutputID / TaskID

*Migration steps 3 (P3-06a: contracts + reducer in shadow mode) and 11 (P3-06b: the reducer owns the state).*

**Finding(s):** C12; C report §5.2-§5.5 and §6 Step 5; E §2.1 (Pipecat frame discipline)  **Effort:** a: 2–3 days, b: 3 days  **Depends on:** a: P3-02; b: P3-03b, P3-04b, P3-05, P3-08, P3-09, P3-10 (H-audit 2026-09-23: the reducer absorbs `NotAddressedPolicy`)  **Unblocks:** a: P3-03, P3-04, P3-09; b: phase exit

**Why (what the user experiences today):** Indirectly, every stall and cross-talk bug in
this phase: the stuck `isThinking` that silences every later announcement (Coordinator:459-461
returns without `finishVoiceFrontend`; `closeSession` does not reset it), the announcement
that starts in the gap between a commit and `beginVoiceFrontend` (C race 11), the barrier
that stays closed after a failure (C1). Each was caused by one of ten independent epoch
counters and four cross-object flags (`inputPending`, `voiceInputActive`, `isThinking`,
`heardSpeech`) being updated in the wrong order by a different object. `voiceInputActive` is
set and cleared in consecutive statements (Capture:1303 then :1307), so every check of it in
production is a no-op (C report Q5). Nothing names "the current turn" once, and there is no
test that replays a session and checks what the system decided.

**Files you will change:**
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/VoiceContracts.swift` (§2.1–§2.4 types, `VoiceSessionState`, `VoiceCommand`, `VoiceSessionReducer`)
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/VoiceSession.swift` (the shell)
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/VoiceSessionReducerSelfTest.swift`
- `Sources/NextNotes/Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift` (a: emit events alongside today's calls; b: events replace the calls)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift` (a: emit `decisionMade`/`responseEnded`; b: remove `inputPending`, `inputEpoch`, `responseID`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift` (a: optional turn identity/end hooks; b: remove voice use of `voiceInputActive`, `speechGeneration`, `isThinking`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent+ToolLoop.swift` (a: first response/output identity hook; b: `AgentToolSpeechTracker.maySpeak`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAudioSession.swift` (a: forward playback phases)
- `Sources/NextNotes/NextNotesApp.swift`, `Scripts/acceptance.sh`, `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`

**Read for context (do not change):**
- §2.1–§2.3 of this file (types and the transition table are the specification).
- `roadmap/done/agent-analysis-2026-09-23/C-concurrency.md` §2 (state machine of a voice session:
  regions A–D and who owns each transition) and §5.5 (how barge-in propagates).
- AGENTS.md "Voice work and output have separate lifetimes", "Live conversation has a
  different model owner", "On the conversational path, a deterministic gate may only add".

**Current behaviour:** The transitions of C report §2.3 are made by four objects:

| Transition | Today's owner (verified) |
|---|---|
| Barrier up | `AgentCaptureController.noteLevel` (Capture:896-901) / `considerSpeechInterruption` (:1133-1137) → `Coordinator.inputActivityStarted` (:196-199); `Coordinator.handle` (:299) |
| Barrier down | `Coordinator.resolveInput(epoch:)` (:238-242), `discardInput` (:232-236), `closeSession` (:244-257) |
| Output pause/resume | `considerSpeechInterruption` → `RealtimeAudioSession.pauseForListening` (:389); `resumeListeningAfterDiscard` (Capture:935-938) |
| Output stop | `emitTurn` → `RealtimeAgent.userSpeechStarted` (RealtimeAgent.swift:52-63) → `RealtimeAudioSession.noteUserSpeech` (:435-451) |
| Response cancel | `Coordinator.speechStarted` (:201-207) / `handle` (:295); `emitTurn` cancels `activeTurnTask` (Capture:1345) |
| Idle close | `tick` (Capture:1272-1281) |
| Result → speech | `AgentTaskManager.announce` → queue → `tick` flush (replaced by P3-04) |

**Target behaviour:**
- **P3-06a:** the reducer exists, is pure, and is exercised by 9 built-in traces with exact
  expected command sequences. `VoiceSession.shared` runs in **shadow mode**: today's call
  sites also send it events; it reduces them, executes **only** the commands that drive the
  new components that already exist (none before P3-04; `openDeliveryWindow` and
  `scheduleTimer` after P3-04a; `publishIsland` after P3-09), and records divergences between
  its state and the legacy flags. Across the voice self-test suite the divergence count on
  `effectsHeld`, `turnPending`, `output` and `floor` is 0.
- **P3-06b:** `VoiceSession` is the owner. The legacy flags, the ten counters (except the
  frontend- and synthesizer-private ones in §2.1) and the 20 ms / 50 ms polls are gone. The
  same self-tests pass. A recorded `--selftest-voice-pipeline` trace replays through the
  reducer with every invariant holding.

**Steps — P3-06a:**
1. `VoiceContracts.swift`: paste §2.1–§2.4 and §2.3 exactly (types must be `Sendable`,
   `Equatable`, `Codable`). `TaskEvent`, `TaskID` etc. that §2.5 lists live in

   Verify existing call sites first: `VoiceContracts.swift` and the shadow shell
   are implemented in `5ecc7e6`. Reuse these definitions; never add a second copy. The existing latency VoiceInstant remains unchanged; the reducer
   uses VoiceSessionInstant for pure replay-relative milliseconds.
2. `VoiceSessionReducer.reduce(_:_:at:)`: implement the transition table of §2.3 row by row
   as one `switch event` with guards in row order. Two helpers keep it readable:
   `func withUnclassified(_ s: inout VoiceSessionState, insert/remove:) -> [VoiceCommand]`
   (emits `setEffectsHeld` on the effective hold edge, including failedInputHold) and
   `func island(_ s: inout VoiceSessionState, _ p: IslandProjection) -> [VoiceCommand]`
   (emits `publishIsland` only on change). No `Date()`, no `ContinuousClock`, no singletons,
   no `Task` inside the reducer — the self-test replays it byte for byte.
3. `VoiceSession.swift`:
   ```swift
   @MainActor
   final class VoiceSession {
       enum Mode { case shadow, owner }
       static let shared = VoiceSession()
       var mode: Mode = .shadow
       private(set) var state = VoiceSessionState()
       private let reference = ContinuousClock.now
       var nowForTesting: VoiceSessionInstant?                 // self-tests drive time
       private(set) var divergences: [String] = []     // shadow mode only
       private var recorder: FileHandle?               // --record-voice-trace <path>
       private var timers: [VoiceTimer: Task<Void, Never>] = [:]
       private var observers: [UUID: AsyncStream<VoiceEvent>.Continuation] = [:]

       func send(_ event: VoiceEvent) { apply(event) }
       /// The control channel. Reduced immediately; executors re-check ids before acting.
       func interrupt(_ interruption: Interruption) { apply(.interruption(interruption)) }
       func events() -> AsyncStream<VoiceEvent>        // observers (tests, recorder)
       func currentTurn() -> TurnID?                   // floor's or response's turn
       func beginTurnIfNeeded() -> TurnID               // mints when the floor is free

       private func apply(_ event: VoiceEvent) {
           let now = nowForTesting ?? VoiceSessionInstant(milliseconds: reference.duration(to: .now).wholeMilliseconds)
           let (next, commands) = VoiceSessionReducer.reduce(state, event, at: now)
           state = next
           record(event, now)
           for c in observers.values { c.yield(event) }
           execute(commands)
           if mode == .shadow { checkDivergence(after: event) }
       }
   }
   ```
   Reduction is synchronous on the main actor, so an `interrupt` can never sit behind queued
   data events; the "overtakes data" property comes from that plus the id re-checks in each
   executor (C §5.1's Pipecat system-frame rule). `execute` in shadow mode performs only
   `scheduleTimer` (timers send `.timer(t)` back), and — once their tasks landed —
   `openDeliveryWindow` (P3-04a) and `publishIsland` (P3-09). Every other command is appended
   to a ring buffer (`recentCommands`, 200 entries) for tests.
4. **Emit events from today's call sites** (shadow mode; the old calls stay):

   | Call site | Event |
   |---|---|
   | `AgentCaptureController.beginSession` after `isSessionActive = true` (Capture:250) | `.sessionOpened(sessionID: captureID)` |
   | `AgentCaptureController.endSession` (Capture:339-379) and the goodbye path (:1251-1260) | `.sessionClosed(.done/.idle/.goodbye)` |
   | `noteLevel` where it calls `inputActivityStarted` (Capture:896-901) | `.inputActivity(VoiceSession.shared.beginTurnIfNeeded())` |
   | `considerSpeechInterruption` where it pauses (Capture:1133-1141) | `interrupt(.provisional(turn))` |
   | `prepareResponseForRecognizedSpeech` (Capture:1149-1166), non-empty candidate | `.partial(turn, text:, stable: true)` |
   | `Coordinator.discardInput`, after its complete current-input withdrawal | `.inputWithdrawn` |
   | each discard branch in `tick` (Capture:1191-1250) | `.inputDiscarded(turn, .noWords/.backchannel/.inFlightRepeat/.playbackEcho/.revisedTranscript)` |
   | `emitTurn` hesitation branch (Capture:1329-1337) | `.hesitation(turn, text:)` |
   | `emitTurn` before `RealtimeAgent.shared.userSpeechStarted()` (Capture:1302-1304) | `interrupt(.committed(turn))` then `.committed(turn, text:)` |
   | `Coordinator.respond`, when the route is known (P3-02's release point, each control branch, each deterministic gate) | `.decisionMade(turn, decision)` |
   | `RealtimeAgent.finishVoiceFrontend`, `Coordinator.finishFailure`, and the superseded `return` at :459-461 | `.responseEnded(turn, outcome)` |
   | `AgentToolSpeechTracker.receive` first `beginSpokenReply` (ToolLoop:121-124) | `.responseStarted(turn, output)` |
   | `RealtimeAudioSession.receivePlaybackEvent` (:325) | `.playback(currentOutput, phase)` (`.began`→began, first `.startAcknowledged`→firstAudio, `.completed` with nothing queued→finished, `.interrupted`→interrupted(byUser: set by `userSpeechStarted`)) |

7. **`--selftest-voice-session-reducer [trace.jsonl]`** in `VoiceSessionReducerSelfTest.swift`:
   - Nine built-in traces (arrays of `(ms, VoiceEvent)`) with the exact expected command
     list after each event. Minimum set:
     T1 plain question and answer (rows 1, 4, 5, 11, 12, 14, 19, 16, 20);
     T2 provisional barge-in then discard → `pauseOutput` then `resumeOutput`, effects held
     then released (rows 6, 8);
     T3 committed barge-in over an answer → `stopOutput(o1, byUser: true)`,
     `cancelResponse(t1)`, `startResponse(t2)` and **no `cancelTask`** (rows 10, 11);
     T4 failed turn → `responseEnded(t1, .failed("deadline"))` releases input/reads
     while retaining the write hold (C1, amended row 16); assert stale events do not
     clear it and later classification/current withdrawal/session close do;
     T5 delivery timing → no `openDeliveryWindow` while the answer plays; after `finished` at
     1,000 ms and a timer at 1,200 ms → `scheduleTimer(.quietWindow, 150 ms)`; timer at
     1,360 ms → `openDeliveryWindow` (rows 20, 23, 26);
     T6 session close with an active task → `stopOutput(byUser: false)`, no `cancelTask` (row 3);
     T7 hesitation keeps effects held and starts no response (row 9);
     T8 explicit cancel → exactly `[cancelTask(nil)]` (row 29);
     T9 idle close is blocked by an active task and by a pending approval, and fires after
     25 s otherwise (row 30).
   - Invariants checked on every trace (built-in and the optional file argument):
     INV1 never two outputs; INV2 every `committed(t)` yields exactly one `startResponse(t)`;
     Replay duplicate commitments before and after response completion; INV2 still
     requires exactly one start.
     INV3 no `openDeliveryWindow` unless `deliveryWindowOpen(now)` was true;
     INV4 after `responseEnded(t, _)`, `t ∉ unclassified`;
     INV5 no `stopOutput(_, byUser: true)` without a preceding committed interruption for a
     newer turn; **INV6 no `cancelTask` unless the event was `interruption(.explicitCancel)`**
     (I1/I3 as a machine-checked rule); INV7 the reducer is deterministic (reduce the same
     trace twice; the command lists are equal).
   - With a file argument, decode each line, replay, check INV1–INV7 and print
     `VOICE_SESSION_REDUCER_TRACE events=<n> commands=<m>`.
   - Final marker `VOICE_SESSION_REDUCER_OK` or `VOICE_SESSION_REDUCER_FAILED: <trace>/<event index>: <reason>`.
   Register it next to the other voice flags in `NextNotesApp.runRequestedSelfTest`; add to
   AGENTS.md's list and to `Scripts/acceptance.sh` (EXPERIMENTAL now, CORE at phase exit).

**Success threshold:** P3-06a: 9/9 traces and INV1–INV7 pass; divergence count on
`effectsHeld`/`turnPending`/`output`/`floor` = 0 across the voice suite after stable
producer updates. A comparator mismatch is not an allowed legacy-bug exception. P3-06b: all Verify flags OK; the recorded pipeline trace
replays with 0 invariant violations; `grep -n "inputPending\|voiceInputActive\|speechGeneration" Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift Sources/NextNotes/Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift`
returns nothing; `--selftest-voice-pipeline`'s end-of-speech → first-audio is within ±50 ms
of its Phase 2 value (the reducer must not add latency).

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: the ownership switch reorders an operation that timing tests
depend on (barge-in pause, EOU commit) — the barge and pipeline WAV runs are in Verify for
that reason. Risk: a missed call site leaves the reducer's state stale (e.g. no `responseEnded`
on a new early return) — INV2/INV4 on the recorded trace catch it. Rollback: P3-06a is
additive (shadow); P3-06b is one commit — revert it and set `mode = .shadow`.

**Done when:**
- [ ] P3-06a: contracts file, pure reducer, shell in shadow mode, events emitted from every listed call site
- [ ] P3-06a: `--selftest-voice-session-reducer` red → green; registered; divergence report in STATUS.md (0 on the four fields)
- [ ] P3-06b: owner mode; legacy flags, polls and counters removed as listed; all Verify flags OK; recorded trace replays clean
- [ ] AGENTS.md paragraph and README entries added; flag moved to CORE at phase exit

### P3-07 — Compute: capacity lanes, a second llama context, LocalToolLoopWorker

**Role-aware capacity amendment (2026-09-30):** coordinate with SB-03 before touching
native slots. Same-artifact requests may reuse resident weights with distinct owned
contexts/cache histories; different role assignments remain legal even if capacity
admits only one resident file. Do not assume the global Agent spec is the notes or
cleanup spec. Requests must carry/verify their acquired artifact through generation.
A second context is not a second loader and does not prove two-model overlap. Measure
RAM/admission and warm/cold/overlap p95; background notes yield visibly, MiniCPM
cleanup takes its bounded Apple fallback when exact ownership is unavailable, and
no role switch may evict an active voice lease. No per-module runtime or download.


*Migration steps 12 (P3-07a), 13 (P3-07b), 14 (P3-07c), 15 (P3-07d, optional).*

**Finding(s):** C3, C4, C11; C report §3 Q3, §5.6, §6 Step 6; A-latency Hop 8; decision doc §3.5 #6 (A and C disagree on Foundation Models inside `ComputeScheduler`)  **Effort:** a: 1.5 d, b: 2 d, c: 1 d, d (optional): 2 d  **Depends on:** P0-06 (cancellable `acquire`, warmup at `.background`), P3-02; b: P0-18 (prefix reuse; its `kvTokens` state moves into each slot); c: P3-05, P3-09; d: P3-05  **Unblocks:** phase exit; P5-01 (d is an alternative to the cloud frontend)

**Why (what the user experiences today):** Background work makes no progress while the
frontend speaks, and the frontend's time to first token degrades whenever the worker model
is loading or prefilling on the same machine: on 2026-09-22, turns overlapping a worker load
took 3.2 s, 8.3 s and 8.8 s to first token, and two turns failed at exactly 2.0 s
(A-latency Hop 8). A voice job waits minutes behind meeting notes because there is one llama
context with one non-preemptible reservation (NotesModelRuntime.swift:698-715). Two voice
jobs plan one round at a time. When Apple Intelligence is off, there is no conversation at all
(Coordinator:415-422).

**Files you will change:**
- `Sources/NextNotes/Core/Compute/ComputeScheduler.swift` (capacity table, `ComputeLanePolicy`, engine tag; self-test)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Core/Compute/ComputeJob.swift` (`ComputeEngine`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Transcription/ParakeetEngine.swift`, `Sources/NextNotes/Meetings/ChunkedTranscriber.swift` (07a step 5b: `engine: .parakeet` on their `acquire(.realtimeASR)`; H-audit 2026-09-23)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift` (acquire with `engine: .foundationModels`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/NotesModelRuntime.swift` (native slots; second context; memory admission; residency hooks)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Core/Compute/ModelResidencyPolicy.swift` (free the voice slot first under pressure; self-test)
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/VoiceOverlapSelfTest.swift`
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/LocalToolLoopWorker.swift`
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/TaskBridge.swift` (holds `LocalToolLoopWorker`s)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent+ToolLoop.swift` (worker prompt without chat history when self-contained; slot scope)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationWork.swift` (`isSelfContained`)
- d only: `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/FrontendResponder.swift`, `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift`
- `Sources/NextNotes/NextNotesApp.swift`, `Scripts/acceptance.sh`, `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`

**Read for context (do not change):**
- `Sources/NextNotes/Core/Compute/ComputeScheduler.swift:79-251` (the whole actor) and its
  self-test from :253 (`--selftest-scheduler`, NextNotesApp.swift:1519-1524).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/NotesModelRuntime.swift`: `withLane` :662-694,
  `reserveNativeContext` :698-715, `releaseNativeContext` :884-894, `ensureContext`
  :1234-1265 (per-prompt context sizing, `llama_context_default_params`, `n_batch`,
  threads), `shutdown` :905-913, the constants :40-50 (`maxContextTokens = 32_768`,
  `minContextTokens = 2_048`, `contextGranularity = 512`, `batchTokens = 512`,
