- The llama.cpp header shipped with the app (`llama.framework/Headers/llama.h` under
  `~/Library/Caches/NextNotesBuild/scratch/artifacts/…`): `llama_context_params.type_k/type_v`,
  `flash_attn_type`, `llama_model_n_layer`, `llama_model_n_head`, `llama_model_n_head_kv`,
  `llama_model_n_embd`, `llama_state_get_size`, `llama_model_size`.
- AGENTS.md: "The notes model unloads itself", "`LlamaBackend`'s cleanup gate is
  one-directional", and "The on-device model's single native context must be reserved before
  acquiring its compute scheduler ticket, or actor reentry can corrupt its inference state and
  reverse lock order can deadlock."

**Current behaviour:**
- `ComputeScheduler` holds `private var running: ComputeJob?` (:87) — one job for the whole
  process. `submit` preempts only strictly lower priority (`WorkClass.preempts`,
  ComputeJob.swift:45-47) by moving the runner to `parked`; a parked llama job stops at its
  next `checkpoint` (per token / per 128-token prefill chunk). Foundation Models acquires
  `.realtimeAgent` (LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:248, :388) and never checkpoints.
- `NotesModelRuntime` has one `context` (:63), sized per prompt up to `contextTokens`
  (`ensureContext`, :1234-1265), and one reservation (`nativeOwner`/`nativeWaiters`,
  :79-80): a whole generation holds it, waiters are served by class priority, not preempted.
  Meeting notes, typed turns, cleanup on the app LLM and every voice job share it.
- `runPlannedToolLoop` gives a voice worker the conversation
  (`AgentSession.shared.contextForCurrentTurn(maxCharacters: 2_500 or 6_000)`, ToolLoop:810-812)
  besides the request.
- Measured costs on this 16 GB Mac (from the reports): cold GGUF load 11.78–25.06 s; 1,104-token
  prefill 2.2 s uncontended, 8.03 s contended; weights ~2.7 GB resident (AGENTS.md).
  Unmeasured: FM time to first token while a llama decode runs (the reason for 07a's test).

**Target behaviour:**
- **07a:** the scheduler has per-class capacity. `realtimeASR` stays exclusive and preempting.
  Two `background` llama jobs may run together when they use different native slots. A
  Foundation Models job may run alongside `background` llama work **only** when
  `ComputeLanePolicy.foundationModelsMayOverlapBackground` is true; that constant ships
  `false` and is flipped to `true` in code only after `--selftest-voice-overlap` measures FM
  time-to-first-token degradation < 15 % under a concurrent llama decode on this Mac (decision
  doc §3.5 #6: "if FM time to first token degrades < ~15% under a concurrent llama decode, take
  A's route").
  (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V26) The same rule, measured separately, for Parakeet: a Foundation
  Models job may run beside a Parakeet `.realtimeASR` job **only** when
  `ComputeLanePolicy.foundationModelsMayOverlapParakeet` is true, flipped only after
  `--selftest-voice-overlap` measures FM TTFT degradation < 15 % under a concurrent Parakeet
  window. `realtimeASR` stays exclusive against everything else (llama, the Apple ASR lane).
- **07b:** voice work runs on its own `llama_context` (slot 1) sharing the loaded
  `llama_model`; meeting notes and typed turns stay on slot 0. A voice job no longer waits
  behind notes; the two alternate per token/chunk. The second context is created only when
  memory allows, is capped, freed first under pressure, and freed after idle.
- **07c:** `TaskBridge` runs local voice jobs through `LocalToolLoopWorker`: slot 1, background
  lane, headless, and — when the objective passed `ObjectiveFidelity` — no chat history in its
  prompt.
- **07d (optional):** with Apple Intelligence unavailable and a local model resident, the
  conversational frontend answers on slot 1 instead of saying it cannot.

**Steps — P3-07a (capacity lanes + measurement):**
1. `ComputeJob.swift`: add
   ```swift
   enum ComputeEngine: Sendable, Equatable { case foundationModels, llama(slot: Int), other }
   ```
   and a stored `engine` on `ComputeJob` (default `.other`). `ComputeScheduler.acquire(_:engine:)`
   gets `engine: ComputeEngine = .other`. `LocalVoiceFrontend` passes `.foundationModels`
   (LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:248, :388); `NotesModelRuntime.withLane` passes `.llama(slot:)`.
2. `ComputeScheduler`: replace `running: ComputeJob?` with `running: [ComputeJob]` and a pure
   admission function:
   ```swift
   enum ComputeLanePolicy {
       /// Flipped to true only after --selftest-voice-overlap measured < 15 % FM TTFT
       /// degradation on this Mac (record the numbers here and in AGENTS.md).
       static let foundationModelsMayOverlapBackground = false
       nonisolated(unsafe) static var overlapOverrideForTesting: Bool?   // self-tests only
       static var overlapAllowed: Bool { overlapOverrideForTesting ?? foundationModelsMayOverlapBackground }

       /// Can `job` start while `running` are running, without parking anything?
       static func canCoRun(_ job: ComputeJob, with running: [ComputeJob]) -> Bool {
           if running.isEmpty { return true }
           if job.workClass == .realtimeASR || running.contains(where: { $0.workClass == .realtimeASR }) { return false }
           return running.allSatisfy { other in
               switch (job.engine, other.engine) {
               case let (.llama(a), .llama(b)):
                   return a != b && job.workClass == .background && other.workClass == .background
               case (.foundationModels, .llama), (.llama, .foundationModels):
                   let bg = job.engine == .foundationModels ? other : job
                   return overlapAllowed && bg.workClass == .background
               default:
                   return false
               }
           }
       }
   }
   ```
   `submit(job)`: if `canCoRun` → start it (append). Else, if it preempts every running job it
   conflicts with (`job.workClass.preempts(other.workClass)`), park those (record a
   `ComputeYield` for each) and start it. Else queue (sorted by priority as today).
   `finishCurrent()`/`release(_:)` remove the job from `running` and call
   `startNextIfAvailable()`, which now loops: repeatedly pick the most urgent parked/queued job
   (same tie rule as :230-243) that `canCoRun` with the current runners, until none can.
   `occupancy()`: `running` becomes the most urgent running class (keep the
   `ComputeOccupancy(running:busy:)` shape so callers compile). `checkpoint(id)`: return
   immediately if `id` is in `running`. Keep P0-06's cancellation handler in `acquire`.
3. Extend `ComputeScheduler.runSelfTest()` (`--selftest-scheduler`) with a fresh
   `ComputeScheduler()` instance per case:
   (i) today's assertions unchanged (background yields to realtimeASR);
   (ii) override `false`: an FM `.realtimeAgent` acquire while a `.llama(0)` background job runs
   → the background job is parked and a yield recorded (today's behaviour);
   (iii) override `true`: the same acquire returns immediately, the background job stays in
   `running`, no yield;
   (iv) `.llama(0)` and `.llama(1)` background jobs both run; a second `.llama(1)` job queues;
   (v) a `realtimeASR` acquire parks both llama jobs and waits for an FM job to release.
   Reset `overlapOverrideForTesting = nil` in `defer`.
4. **`--selftest-voice-overlap`** (`Agent/Duplex/VoiceOverlapSelfTest.swift`), a measurement
   in the style of `--selftest-llm-metal` (NextNotesApp.swift:2634-2700):
   - Preconditions → `VOICE_OVERLAP_ABSENT: <reason>` and exit (never OK):
     `FoundationModelFormatter.unavailableReason != nil` ("Apple Intelligence unavailable: …");
     no installed GGUF for the Agent role (resolve the model the same way P0-01's
     `--selftest-llm-metal` change does; if that is not available, use
     `InstalledModelLibrary.shared.activeModel`) ("no local model installed").
   - A **private** runtime, never the shared one:
     `NotesModelRuntime(spec: NotesModelRuntime.spec(for: model), gpuLayers: NotesModelRuntime.allGPULayers)`;
     `await runtime.shutdown()` at the end (weights must not stay resident after a test).
   - Warm: one FM `LanguageModelSession(instructions: LocalVoiceSplitResponse.answerInstructions).respond(to: "Say ready.")`
     and one `runtime.complete(system: "You are terse.", user: "Say ready.", maxTokens: 8)`.
   - Phase A (alone), 5 runs: new FM session per run with the same instructions,
     `streamResponse(to: "What is a haiku? Answer in one sentence.")`, time to the first
     non-empty snapshot (TTFT) and to completion; then one llama `complete(maxTokens: 128)` →
     tokens/s.
   - Phase B (overlap), 5 runs: start `let loop = Task { while !Task.isCancelled { _ = try? await runtime.complete(system: …, user: "Count slowly from one to fifty.", maxTokens: 128) } }`;
     wait until its first completion has started (≥ 1 decoded token, observed through a
     counter); run the same 5 FM measurements; `loop.cancel()`; record llama tokens/s during
     the overlap. The FM calls in this test do **not** go through `ComputeScheduler`, so the
     overlap is physical regardless of the policy — that is what is being measured.
   - Print: `VOICE_OVERLAP_FM_TTFT_MS alone_p50=<a> overlap_p50=<b> degradation_pct=<(b-a)/a*100>`,
     `VOICE_OVERLAP_LLAMA_TOKS alone=<x> overlap=<y>`,
     `VOICE_OVERLAP_VERDICT=overlap-allowed` when degradation < 15 % else `VOICE_OVERLAP_VERDICT=keep-exclusive`.
   - Final: `VOICE_OVERLAP_OK` when every FM run produced text and the llama loop produced
     ≥ 1 token in each phase; else `VOICE_OVERLAP_FAILED: <side> produced zero tokens`. The
     verdict does not decide OK/FAILED (the test names a measurement; a hardware answer is not
     a failure).
   - Register in `NextNotesApp.runRequestedSelfTest`, AGENTS.md list, `Scripts/acceptance.sh`
     EXPERIMENTAL with a 900 s budget (`"selftest-voice-overlap|900"`).
5. **The measured flag.** Run `--selftest-voice-overlap` three times (with nothing else
   running). If all three verdicts are `overlap-allowed`, set
   `foundationModelsMayOverlapBackground = true` in code with a comment giving the date, the
   model name, the three `degradation_pct` values and the llama tokens/s, add the same line to
   AGENTS.md next to "Live conversation has a different model owner", and record it in
   STATUS.md. Otherwise leave it `false` and record the numbers — the frontend still preempts
   the worker at its next checkpoint, as today. Either way the decision is data, not a guess.
5b. **Foundation Models beside a Parakeet window — measure, then decide** (H-audit 2026-09-23,
   H2 V26). Verified 2026-09-24: dictation's Parakeet takes the lane for the whole recognition
   window — `ParakeetEngine` @ `Transcription/ParakeetEngine.swift:87`
   `let jobID = await ComputeScheduler.shared.acquire(.realtimeASR)` ("Hold the ASR lane for load
   + feed + partials + finish", :82-86) — and meeting ASR takes it per window in
   `ChunkedTranscriber.transcribe(_:)` @ `Meetings/ChunkedTranscriber.swift:402`. The voice
   frontend asks for `.realtimeAgent` (`LocalVoiceFrontend` @
   `Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:254` and `:401`, `acquireCancellable(.realtimeAgent)`
   since P0-06; the :248/:388 cited in step 1 have moved), and `.realtimeASR` has priority 1, so
   a voice turn during a recorded meeting waits for each Parakeet window, and a voice turn during
   a dictation waits for the whole dictation. Step 2's `canCoRun` keeps that (its first `if`
   returns false whenever `.realtimeASR` is involved). ASR is declared to prefer the Neural
   Engine (`WorkClass.preferredDevice`, `Core/Compute/ComputeJob.swift:62-65` — "policy intent
   only", not read at runtime), so the real contention with FM may be small or large; nobody has
   measured it, and that is what this step does.
   - `ComputeJob.swift`: add `case parakeet` to `ComputeEngine`. `ParakeetEngine` (:87) and
     `ChunkedTranscriber` (:402) pass `engine: .parakeet`. The Apple ASR lane
     (`DictationController.swift:778`, `acquire(.realtimeASR)`) stays `.other` — it is not
     measured and stays exclusive.
   - `ComputeLanePolicy`: add
     ```swift
     /// Flipped to true only after --selftest-voice-overlap measured < 15 % FM TTFT degradation
     /// beside a Parakeet window on this Mac, three runs (H2 V26). Record the numbers here.
     static let foundationModelsMayOverlapParakeet = false
     nonisolated(unsafe) static var parakeetOverlapOverrideForTesting: Bool?
     static var parakeetOverlapAllowed: Bool { parakeetOverlapOverrideForTesting ?? foundationModelsMayOverlapParakeet }
     ```
     and in `canCoRun`, **before** the `realtimeASR` early return, a pair rule: an
     `(.foundationModels, .realtimeAgent)` job and a `(.parakeet, .realtimeASR)` job may co-run
     when `parakeetOverlapAllowed`; every other combination with a `.realtimeASR` job returns

     so `canCoRun` = `running.allSatisfy { pairCompatible(job, $0) }`, and move step 2's two
     rules into the same function.
   - `--selftest-scheduler` gains case **(vi)**: override `false` → an FM `.realtimeAgent` acquire
     while a `.parakeet` `.realtimeASR` job runs waits (today's behaviour); override `true` → it
     returns immediately and both are in `running`; a `.background` `.llama(0)` job submitted
     while the Parakeet job runs is still parked/queued in both settings. Reset both overrides in
     `defer`.
   - `--selftest-voice-overlap` gains **Phase C (Parakeet overlap)**, same file, same rules as
     phases A/B (FM calls do not go through `ComputeScheduler`, so the overlap is physical):
     precondition `ParakeetModels.isDownloaded` (`Transcription/ParakeetEngine.swift:342`), else
     print `VOICE_OVERLAP_PARAKEET_ABSENT: Parakeet not downloaded` and skip phase C only (the final

     (the same call `ChunkedTranscriber` makes, :405-409); run the phase-A FM measurement 5 times

     `VOICE_OVERLAP_PARAKEET_WINDOW_S alone=<x> overlap=<y>`, and
     `VOICE_OVERLAP_PARAKEET_VERDICT=overlap-allowed` when degradation < 15 % **and** the window

     The test never writes a meeting, a dictation run or `runs.jsonl`.

     set `foundationModelsMayOverlapParakeet = true` with the date, model, three

**Steps — P3-07b (second llama context):**
6. **Memory cost — compute it, do not assume it.** KV cache bytes for one context:
   `2 (K and V) × n_layer × n_ctx × n_head_kv × head_dim × bytes_per_element`, with
   `head_dim = llama_model_n_embd / llama_model_n_head` and bytes 2 for f16, ≈ 1.06 for q8_0.
   Add `static func kvBytes(model: OpaquePointer, contextTokens: Int, quantized: Bool) -> Int`.
   Worked estimate for a Qwen3-4B-shaped model (36 layers, 8 KV heads, head_dim 128 —
   **estimate**; K2-Horizon-4B's real values come from the GGUF at runtime):
   144 KiB/token f16 → 6,144 tokens ≈ 864 MiB (f16) or ≈ 459 MiB (q8_0), plus a compute buffer
   of roughly 100–300 MiB (**estimate**). On a 16 GB Mac already holding ~2.7 GB of weights,
   FM, Parakeet, the EOU model and Pocket TTS, that is why the voice context is capped and
   admitted by a memory check.
7. `NotesModelRuntime`: turn the single-context state into slots.
   ```swift
   enum NativeSlot: Int, Sendable, CaseIterable { case shared = 0, voiceWork = 1 }
   private struct SlotState {
       var context: OpaquePointer?
       var contextSize = 0
       var owner = false
       var waiters: [(id: UUID, workClass: WorkClass, continuation: CheckedContinuation<Bool, Never>)] = []
       var lastUse = Date.distantPast
   }
   private var slots: [NativeSlot: SlotState] = [.shared: .init(), .voiceWork: .init()]
   static let voiceContextTokens = 6_144          // estimate; see step 6
   static let voiceSlotIdleRelease: TimeInterval = 120   // estimate; weights keep their 10 min
   enum SlotScope { @TaskLocal static var slot: NativeSlot = .shared }
   ```
   `reserveNativeContext(for:slot:)` / `releaseNativeContext(slot:)` operate on
   `slots[slot]` (same FIFO-by-priority rule as today); `withLane(_:slot:_:)` reserves the
   slot **first**, then `ComputeScheduler.acquire(workClass, engine: .llama(slot: slot.rawValue))`
   (the AGENTS.md lock order: native before lane; a job holds exactly one slot).
   `streamPrompt`/`completeWhileScheduled`/`streamWhileScheduled` read `SlotScope.slot` to pick
   the slot, and `ensureContext(promptTokens:maxTokens:slot:)` builds that slot's context:
   for `.voiceWork`, `n_ctx = min(voiceContextTokens, contextTokens)`, `type_k = type_v =
   GGML_TYPE_Q8_0` with `flash_attn_type = LLAMA_FLASH_ATTN_TYPE_AUTO` (fall back to f16 if
   `llama_init_from_model` returns nil), same threads/batch as slot 0. A prompt that does
   not fit the voice context runs on `.shared` instead (log it).
   `loadIfNeeded`/`awaitCleanupIdle` are unchanged (one model, loaded once). `activeOperations`
   counts both slots, so the model cannot unload while either is working.
   **Prefix reuse (P0-18):** move `kvTokens` and `lastPrefillStats` into `SlotState`, so each context
   reuses only its own prefix. Every P0-18 reset point (free, rebuild, swap, error, cancellation)
   resets the affected slot; a model swap resets both. Extend `--selftest-llm-prefix-cache` with a
   two-slot case: a prefix prefilled on `.shared` gives `reused == 0` on `.voiceWork`, and a second
   call on `.voiceWork` then reuses its own prefix.
8. **Admission.** Before creating the voice context, compute
   `need = kvBytes(model, voiceContextTokens, quantized) + 256 MiB`. Read available memory
   with `host_statistics64(mach_host_self(), HOST_VM_INFO64, …)`: (free + inactive + purgeable)
   × page size. If `available < need + 1 GiB` (headroom, **estimate**), do not create it:
   run the job on `.shared` and log `voice context skipped: need <n> MB, available <m> MB`.
9. **Release.** Free the voice context (`llama_free`) after `voiceSlotIdleRelease` without use
   (reuse the idle-task pattern of `scheduleIdleUnload`, :1267); `shutdown()`/`shutdownNow()`
   free both. In `ModelResidencyPolicy` (the `.notes` pressure case, ModelResidencyPolicy.swift:148-160)
   first call a new `NotesModelRuntime.shared.releaseVoiceContext() -> Bool`; only if nothing
   was freed proceed to `shutdown()` as today.
10. The voice worker uses slot 1: in `TaskBridge`'s job task (P3-07c's worker), wrap the run
    in `NotesModelRuntime.SlotScope.$slot.withValue(.voiceWork) { … }`. Task-locals are
    inherited by the child tasks `NotesModelRuntime` creates, so every llama call the worker's
    planner makes lands on slot 1 without changing `LLMProvider` signatures.
11. Tests:
    - `--selftest-residency` (`ModelResidencyPolicy.runSelfTest`, :183) gains a GGUF-free slot
      probe (`NotesModelRuntime.slotReservationSelfTest()`, patterned on
      `conversationSchedulingSelfTest`, :725): a `.shared` reservation held by a background job
      does not block a `.voiceWork` reservation; a second `.voiceWork` reservation waits for the
      first; `releaseVoiceContext()` is called before `shutdown()` under simulated pressure
      (inject the unload closure); `activeOperations` > 0 on either slot defers shutdown.
    - `--selftest-voice-overlap` (real model) additionally: records resident memory
      (`task_info(mach_task_self_, MACH_TASK_BASIC_INFO)` `resident_size`) before and after
      creating the voice context and prefilling 1,000 tokens in it, prints
      `VOICE_OVERLAP_SECOND_CONTEXT_MB=<delta> kv_estimate_mb=<kvBytes>`, and runs one
      128-token decode on each slot concurrently, failing with
      `VOICE_OVERLAP_FAILED: slot <n> produced zero tokens` if either produced none.
    - `--selftest-voice-scheduling` and `--selftest-llm-metal` must stay green.

**Steps — P3-07c (LocalToolLoopWorker):**
12. `Agent/Duplex/LocalToolLoopWorker.swift`:
    ```swift
    /// A headless background worker: one self-contained objective, the llama voice slot,
    /// the background lane. It never touches the island, speech or the input barrier.
    @MainActor
    final class LocalToolLoopWorker {
        let taskID: TaskID
        let work: VoiceConversationWork
        private let agent: RealtimeAgent            // RealtimeAgent(voiceWorker:) hosts runPlannedToolLoop for now
        init(taskID: TaskID, work: VoiceConversationWork, providerForTesting: (any LLMProvider)? = nil)
        func run() async -> String {
            await NotesModelRuntime.SlotScope.$slot.withValue(.voiceWork) { await agent.runVoiceObjective() }
        }
        func cancel() { agent.cancelVoiceObjective() }
    }
    ```
    `TaskBridge` stores `LocalToolLoopWorker` instead of `(work, worker)` pairs. Moving
    `runPlannedToolLoop` itself out of `RealtimeAgent` is deliberately not part of this task:
    Phase 1 (P1-02…P1-06) and Phase 4 (P4-03) rewrite that function, and moving it now would
    make both rewrites conflict.
13. `VoiceConversationWork` gains `var isSelfContained = false`; `TaskBridge.spawn` sets it
    when P3-05's `ObjectiveFidelity` accepted the objective. In `runPlannedToolLoop`
    (private overload, ToolLoop:808-818) skip the "Earlier Agent conversation" section when
    `isVoiceWorker && work?.isSelfContained == true`. Memory grounding stays (names and
    labels). Record the planner prompt token count before/after for one fixed voice objective
    in STATUS.md (expected reduction up to ~600 tokens — **estimate** from the 2,500-character
    cap).
14. `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` gains A6: the worker's planner prompt (captured by the
    scripted provider) has no "Earlier Agent conversation" section for a self-contained
    objective and has one when `isSelfContained == false`; and (with P3-09's counters) the
    worker made zero island or activation writes.

**Steps — P3-07d (optional fallback conversational lane, C11):**
15. Only if P5 (cloud frontend) is not planned soon, and behind
    `static let voiceLlamaFallbackEnabled = false` in `FrontendResponder`. When
    `FoundationModelFormatter.unavailableReason != nil` and the local model is resident
    (never trigger a cold load from a voice turn): route with a GBNF-constrained llama
    completion of `FrontendDecisionPayload` (reuse the grammar approach of
    `Agent/FunctionCalling/LocalModelFunctionCallProposer.swift:187-198`, or P1-05's GBNF over
    the manifest if it landed) and answer with `streamInteractiveConversation` on slot 1 at
    `.realtimeAgent`; voice workers then use slot 0. The coordinator's unavailable branch
    (Coordinator:415-422) consults this before speaking `unavailableReply`.
    `--selftest-voice-conversation` gains a case with `frontendUnavailableReasonForTesting`
    set and a scripted llama provider: the turn is answered, not refused.

**Do not:**
- Do not reverse the lock order: a llama job reserves its native slot **before** its scheduler ticket; FM takes only the lane; no job ever holds two slots (AGENTS.md; I7).
- Do not flip `foundationModelsMayOverlapBackground` to true without three `overlap-allowed` runs recorded in code, AGENTS.md and STATUS.md.
- Do not flip `foundationModelsMayOverlapParakeet` without three phase-C `overlap-allowed` verdicts, and do not let the pair rule admit anything besides FM + Parakeet: llama and the Apple ASR lane stay exclusive against `.realtimeASR` (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V26). Do not let phase C write a meeting, a dictation run or `runs.jsonl`.
- Do not let a self-test keep weights resident: private runtimes call `shutdown()`; the shared runtime is never loaded by a self-test (`prewarmIfNeeded` refuses under the harness, NotesModelRuntime.swift:295).
- Do not create the second context without the memory check, and never at launch or at microphone open (AGENTS.md: opening the microphone must not load and prefill a 4B worker before there is work).
- Do not call `LlamaBackend.beginCleanup()` from any new path (the cleanup gate is one-directional; closing the cycle deadlocks).
- Do not let a voice-turn path trigger a cold model load for the fallback lane (07d).
- Do not move `runPlannedToolLoop` out of `RealtimeAgent` in this task.
- Do not run warmup at the frontend's class (P0-06's rule; AGENTS.md).

**Red-first self-test:**
- `--selftest-scheduler`: add cases (iii) and (iv) first; on today's single-slot scheduler
  they fail (`"FM acquire waited behind background with overlap allowed"`, `"two background
  slots did not co-run"`). Marker: the existing `SCHEDULER_OK`/`SCHEDULER_FAILED` lines of
  `ComputeScheduler.runSelfTest` (check the exact strings in ComputeScheduler.swift:266+ and
  keep them).
- `--selftest-residency`: the slot probe fails to compile before step 7 — write it first; on
  the old runtime the "voice reservation not blocked by shared" assertion is the red
  (`"voice work waited behind the shared context"`).
- `--selftest-voice-overlap`: new; it has no "red" in the usual sense — it is a measurement.
  Confirm it prints `VOICE_OVERLAP_ABSENT: …` when no local model is installed, and real
  numbers otherwise.
- (H-audit 2026-09-23) `--selftest-scheduler` case (vi) with the override `true` fails on the
  unmodified scheduler (`"FM acquire waited behind a Parakeet window with overlap allowed"`);
  phase C of `--selftest-voice-overlap` prints `VOICE_OVERLAP_PARAKEET_ABSENT: …` when Parakeet

- `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` A6: red until step 13.

**Verify:**
```bash
make build
make install OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-scheduler
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-residency
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-scheduling
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-overlap        # 3 runs for the flag decision
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-overlap
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-overlap
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-llm-metal
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-duplex-work
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-concurrent-voice
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-contention
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-pipeline <wav>
make acceptance TIER=core
```

**Success threshold:** scheduler cases (i)–(v) pass; residency slot probe passes;
`--selftest-voice-overlap` OK with numbers recorded (3 runs); second context resident delta
within ±30 % of `kvBytes` + compute buffer (else record why); with a background voice job
running, `--selftest-voice-pipeline` end-of-speech → first audio is no worse than its Phase 2
value + 15 % (if the flag is on) or unchanged (flag off); A6 passes. (H-audit 2026-09-23)
Scheduler case (vi) passes; phase C numbers recorded for 3 runs (or `…_PARAKEET_ABSENT` with the
reason in STATUS.md); `foundationModelsMayOverlapParakeet` is `true` only with three
`overlap-allowed` verdicts (FM TTFT degradation < 15 % and the 30 s window < 30 s).

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: memory pressure on 16 GB with two contexts plus FM — admission
check, q8_0 KV, idle release and pressure-first release bound it; watch `residency:` log lines.
Risk: two llama generations alternating per token halve each one's speed — acceptable (notes
are background; voice work no longer waits minutes); record tokens/s. Risk: the capacity
rewrite of the scheduler breaks an existing yield assertion — case (i) guards it. Rollback:
07a — revert to `running: ComputeJob?` (the policy constant is already false); 07b — set
`voiceContextTokens = 0` to disable slot 1 (jobs fall back to `.shared`) or revert; 07c — revert
the worker wrapper; 07d — leave the constant false.

### P3-08 — C8 spoken approvals

*Migration step 10.*

**Finding(s):** C8; C report §0 item 8, §4 rank 10, race 9; decision doc §4 (`respond_approval`), E §1.7 ("Permission requests"), I10  **Effort:** 2.5–3 days  **Depends on:** P3-03a (ledger, `needsPermission`, `revise`), P3-04a (`OutputScheduler` deliveries, D10 priority), P3-05 (`FrontendDecision`, `<decision>` payload, `ReceiptAck`), P3-06a (`VoiceSession` events `approvalPending`/`approvalResolved`), P3-02 (`AgentToolExecutor.fireOverrideForTesting`), P1-07 (pending-action routing), P3-10 (H-audit 2026-09-23: route set complete before the approval gate is graded; §3 step 9b)  **Unblocks:** P3-06b, P5-01/P5-02/P5-03 (`respond_approval` is offered only under this task's rule)

**Why (what the user experiences today):** A voice job that reaches a write — "email Anna
that the deck is ready" — stops at a card and waits, unbounded by design, for someone to
press a button. Nothing is said: `PermissionGate` answers only from buttons (the Agent tab
card, `UI/Agent/AgentView.swift:280-305`; the island, via `IslandState.onProposalDecision`
wired in `AgentService.start`, `Agent/AgentService.swift:75-82`). If the user, hearing
nothing, says "yes, send it", that turn is routed as an answer or as a revision of the job,
and a revision *cancels the pending approval*: `PermissionGate.shared.cancelPending(taskID:)`
at Coordinator:347 (naming turn) and Coordinator:525 (`<revise>`). The tool call then fails
with a changed revision, the planner re-plans, raises the same card again, and the user's
"yes" has become a "no" — the loop the C report calls race 9. Meanwhile the open card keeps
`hasActiveWork` true, so the session never idles out and the microphone stays open.

**Files you will change:**
- new `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/SpokenApproval.swift` (`SpokenApproval` constants, prompt builder, utterance reader, replies; `SpokenApprovalPrompt`; `SpokenApprovalReading`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/TaskBridge.swift` (`approvals` state, `respondApproval` replaces the P3-03 stub, PermissionGate hooks, `revise` no longer cancels the approval)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Permissions/PermissionGate.swift` (hooks in `present`/`respond`/`cancelMatching`/`cancelPending`; `via:` on `respond`; new `resolveRevised(id:amendment:)`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/OutputScheduler.swift` (approval items: enqueue, withdraw, prompt playback report)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/FrontendResponder.swift` (`ReceiptAck` `.refusedApprovalPending` reply; `.respondPermission` mapping)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/TaskStatusRenderer.swift` (needsPermission line speaks the prompt)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift` (approval gate in `respond`; `.respondPermission` branch; remove the two `cancelPending(taskID:)` calls)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceDecision.swift` (`answerApproval` intent, one route rule sentence) and `LocalVoiceFrontend.swift` (payload `"approval"`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.swift` (`runTurnRouting` approval block)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift` (H-audit 2026-09-23, step 9b: delete the per-turn `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.cancel()` at :1382; the `--selftest-voice-turns` card case in `overlappingBackchannelSelfTestFailures`)
- `AGENTS.md`, `README.md` (tree line for `SpokenApproval.swift`)

**Read for context (do not change):**
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Permissions/PermissionGate.swift` whole file (179 lines): one visible request plus a FIFO `queued` list; `ask` never times out; `respond` refuses an approval whose review still has blockers (:96-103) and records the audit row (:109-122).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Permissions/ToolCallReview.swift:232-300` (`title`, `fields`, `previewField`, `blockers`, `isReadyToRun`, `wasEdited`) and `ToolCallReviewStore.swift:37-65, 163-192` (`begin`, `review(for:)`, `confirm(id:field:)`).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/ActionRuntime.swift:459-518` — `ActionOrchestrator.execute`: `PermissionBroker.authorize` → `.ask` → `PermissionGate.shared.ask` (:476) → card edits merged (:486-505) → `isStillValid` (:518, which is `mayCommitEffect` after P3-02) → `fire`.
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/AgentModels.swift:10-61` — `AgentRisk` (`observe < read < modify < write < send < purchase < destructive < privileged`) and `mayAutoRun`.
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/WorkspaceTools.swift:226-249` — `send_email` (`to`, `subject`, `body`; title "Email <to>") and `reply_email` (title "Reply to <message_id>" — an id; never speak it).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/ACPConfirmation.swift` — `ACPConfirmationGate` is a **different** card (a missing coding CLI: Run once / Cancel). It is not a `PermissionGate` request and this task does not answer it by voice.
- qwen: `roadmap/references/qwen-audio-agent-main/config/frontend-agent/PROMPT.md` section "Permission requests" (E §1.7 has a translation) and `server/src/frontend/frontend-tools.mjs` `permissionResponseInstructions`.
- AGENTS.md "The agent searches your mail without asking, and can't send anything without asking" and "On the conversational path, a deterministic gate may only add, never subtract".

**Current behaviour (verified 2026-09-23):**
- `PermissionGate.present(_:continuation:)` (PermissionGate.swift:42-51) sets `pending`, builds the review (`ToolCallReviewStore.shared.begin`) and raises the island card (`raiseIsland`, :55-68). For anything `risk >= .modify` the island card is a **Review** button, never Approve (:62-64, "Anything that needs an answer, or that speaks in the user's name, is answered where the whole thing is on screen"). Approve lives on the Agent tab's `ToolReviewCard` (AgentView.swift:280-305) and the working card (`AgentWorkingCard.swift:21` observes the gate).
- `PermissionGate.respond(id:approved:duration:scope:)` (:85-139) is the only resolution for a button. Its callers: `AgentView.swift:285`, `:293`, `:296`; `AgentService.swift:76` (island). Nothing else — no voice caller exists.
- A correction cancels the approval:
  ```swift
  // VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift:344-350 (naming turn with an active job)
  job.work.append(text)
  PermissionGate.shared.cancelPending(taskID: job.work.id.uuidString)
  // VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift:523-527 (<revise id="N"/>)
  work.append(text)
  PermissionGate.shared.cancelPending(taskID: work.id.uuidString)
  ```
  `cancelMatching` (:163-178) resumes the waiter with `false`, `ActionRuntime` throws
  `permissionDenied("You dismissed …")`, and because `work.revision` changed the tool loop
  drops the call and re-plans (`case .failure(.message)` with `revision != work.revision`,
  ToolLoop:1072-1077). So the job survives, but the card is torn down and asked again, and
  nothing in the conversation ever tells the user what happened.
- A spoken "yes" has no meaning for approvals: `VoiceTurnPolicy.isBareAcknowledgment`
  (Activation/VoiceTurnPolicy.swift:78-89) is consulted only for a `pendingIntent` offer
  (Coordinator:357-367), and "okay"/"ok" are in `knownNoiseFragments` (:56-59).
- After P3-03 the ledger has `needsPermission` and `TaskBridge.respondApproval` returns
  `.noPendingRequest` (stub); `ReceiptAck` answers `.refusedApprovalPending` with "First,
  there's something waiting for your OK." and P3-05's coordinator answers
  `.respondPermission` with "Please use the buttons on screen for that one." Those three
  placeholders are what this task replaces.

**Target behaviour:**
1. When a session-owned task enters `needsPermission`, the person **hears** the request at
   the next delivery window, ahead of results (D10): for a send, who it goes to, what it is
   about, and — when the message is ≤ `SpokenApproval.sendPreviewLimit` (400 characters) —
   the whole message, then "Should I send it?". The card shows the full message as today.
2. "Yes" (and its exact synonyms) approves; "no" denies; "wait" holds; "yes, but send it to
   Marcus instead" denies **this version** and revises the same task, which re-plans and asks
   again with the change ("Updated: …"). A correction never cancels the task and never
   counts as the user saying no.
3. Ambiguity ("okay", "hmm", "I guess") asks once: "Just to be sure — should I send it? Yes
   or no." A second ambiguous answer is handled by the model as an ordinary turn; the
   request stays pending on the card.
4. Card and voice stay in sync. Every resolution goes through `PermissionGate`: approving on
   the card withdraws the spoken prompt (queued or playing) and a later "yes" finds nothing
   pending; approving by voice removes the card (island and Agent tab) at once.
5. An approval never times out, and nothing ever turns silence, a timeout, a session close or
   a failed delivery into "approved". Session close leaves the card; the next session offers
   the prompt again.
6. Voice may approve only: a request owned by a voice-origin task in the ledger; whose review
   is ready to run (`isReadyToRun`) and unchanged since the prompt was built; whose risk is
   `.observe`…`.send`; and, for `.send`, only after the prompt that contained the whole
   message finished playing. `.purchase`, `.destructive` and `.privileged` are **never**
   approved by voice (I10); they are answered "That one needs your OK on screen." A spoken
   "no" may deny any of them.
7. No write commits before approval: the effect still passes `mayCommitEffect` after the
   approval (ActionRuntime.swift:518), so the "yes" turn itself must be classified first.
8. The on-device route offers `answerApproval` only while a speakable request is pending;
   Phase 5's cloud frontends offer `respond_approval` under the same predicate
   (`TaskBridge.hasSpeakablePendingApproval(sessionID:)`).
9. (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V23) The ACP "coding harness missing — Run once / Cancel" card
   (`ACPConfirmationGate`) survives conversation. Only three things dismiss it while a session
   is open: an explicit cancel phrase (P3-03 step 15a's exact list), a turn routed to **new
   work** (the new request supersedes the one waiting on the card), and the session ending.
   "What's that?", "hmm" or a question about something else leave it up. It is still never
   *answered* by voice (target 6 and the Do-not below): voice can only dismiss it.

**Steps:**
1. **`SpokenApproval.swift`** (new, pure; no singletons except reading
   `ToolCallReviewStore` in `prompt(for:)`):
   ```swift
   struct SpokenApprovalPrompt: Sendable, Equatable {
       let text: String            // spoken, deterministic, never model-phrased (D3)
       let voiceMayApprove: Bool
       let refusal: String?        // said instead of approving when voiceMayApprove == false
       let verb: String            // "send it" | "go ahead" — used in replies and the clarifier
       let readsFullMessage: Bool  // .send only: the text contains the whole body
       let fingerprint: Int        // Hasher over review.arguments sorted by key, at build time
   }

   enum SpokenApprovalReading: Sendable, Equatable {
       case approve, deny, hold, ambiguous
       case amend(String)          // the person's own words, unparaphrased
       case unrelated              // not an answer the gate recognises → the model decides
   }

   enum SpokenApproval {
       static let sendPreviewLimit = 400                       // §2.9; estimate
       static let voiceApprovableRisks: Set<AgentRisk> = [.observe, .read, .modify, .write, .send]

       static func prompt(for request: PermissionRequest, review: ToolCallReview, updated: Bool) -> SpokenApprovalPrompt
       static func read(_ utterance: String) -> SpokenApprovalReading
       static func modelApprovalAllowed(_ utterance: String) -> Bool
       static func clarifier(_ prompt: SpokenApprovalPrompt) -> String      // "Just to be sure — should I \(verb)? Yes or no."
       static func holdReply() -> String                                   // "Okay, it'll wait for you."
       static func reply(for outcome: ApprovalOutcome, prompt: SpokenApprovalPrompt?) -> String
   }
   ```
   **`prompt(for:review:updated:)`** — build the words from the review, never from the model:
   - `head` = `"Updated: "` when `updated`, else `""`.
   - `what` = a per-tool phrase for the tools whose titles carry ids or raw addresses:
     `send_email` → "Send an email to \(recipients)"; `reply_email` → "Send a reply in that
     email thread"; `draft_email` → "Save a draft to \(recipients)"; otherwise `review.title`
     when it has no `@`, no `://` and no digit run of ≥ 6, else "\(review.risk.displayName)
     something" (a fallback that tells the person to look at the card).
     `recipients` = the value of the first `.person` field when present; otherwise each
     address of the `to` field spoken as "anna at northwind traders dot com" (replace `@`
     with " at " and `.` with " dot "), at most three, then "and N more".
   - `.send` with `review.previewField` body ≤ 400 characters:
     `"\(head)\(what), about \(subject). It says: \(body) Should I send it?"` (omit the
     subject clause when empty); `readsFullMessage = true`, `voiceMayApprove = true`,
     `verb = "send it"`.
   - `.send` with a longer body: `"\(head)\(what), about \(subject). It's long, so it's on
     screen for you to check."`; `voiceMayApprove = false`, `refusal = "It's a long message,
     so please check it and send it on screen."`.
   - `.modify` / `.write`: `"\(head)\(what)" + (", \(detail)" for up to two .dateTime/.person
     field short values) + ". Should I go ahead?"`; `verb = "go ahead"`.
   - `.observe` / `.read` (only reaches here when `agentAutoRunReadTools` is off):
     `"\(head)\(what). Should I look?"`; `verb = "look"`.
   - `.purchase` / `.destructive` / `.privileged`: `"\(head)\(what) needs your OK on
     screen."`; `voiceMayApprove = false`; `refusal = "That one needs your OK on screen."`.
   - `!review.isReadyToRun`: `"\(head)\(what). Before I can do that: \(review.blockers[0].prompt)"`;
     `voiceMayApprove = false`; `refusal = "Before I can do that: \(review.blockers[0].prompt)"`.
     (A spoken answer to that question is a correction — step 6 — which re-plans with it.)
   - Pass the final text through `RealtimeAgent.voiceSafeReply` (RealtimeAgent.swift:178-197).
   **`read(_:)`** — exact signatures on `VoiceTurnPolicy.normalizedKey` (:68-73), checked in
   this order; nothing shape-based:
   ```swift
   static let approveKeys: Set<String> = ["yes", "yeah", "yep", "yes please", "go ahead", "yes go ahead",
       "do it", "yes do it", "send it", "yes send it", "please do", "approve", "approved", "sure",
       "sure go ahead", "go for it", "that s fine", "sounds good", "yes that s right"]
   static let denyKeys: Set<String> = ["no", "nope", "no thanks", "no thank you", "don t", "don t send it",
       "do not send it", "don t do it", "no don t", "no stop", "don t bother"]
   static let holdKeys: Set<String> = ["wait", "hold on", "wait a second", "wait a minute", "not yet",
       "hang on", "one second", "give me a second", "let me think", "let me check"]
   static let ambiguousKeys: Set<String> = ["okay", "ok", "hmm", "maybe", "i guess", "i think so",
       "fine", "right", "alright", "all right", "mm"]
   static let amendPrefixes = ["yes but ", "yeah but ", "yes and ", "no ", "actually ", "instead "]
   ```
   exact approve → `.approve`; exact deny → `.deny`; exact hold → `.hold`; exact ambiguous →
   `.ambiguous`; key starts with an amend prefix and has ≥ 2 more tokens, or contains the
   token "instead" or "change" → `.amend(utterance)` (the raw utterance, not the key);
   otherwise `.unrelated`. Check the apostrophe forms against what `normalizedKey` really
   produces (`"that's fine"` → `"that s fine"`) in a unit line, as P3-03 does for status.
   **`modelApprovalAllowed(_:)`** — true only when `read(u) == .approve`, or the key contains
   one of `["yes", "yeah", "yep", "go ahead", "send it", "do it", "approve"]` as whole tokens
   **and** none of `["no", "not", "don t", "wait", "stop", "cancel", "instead", "but"]`. The
   model's reading alone never approves anything (I10; AGENTS "can't send anything without
   asking").
   **`reply(for:prompt:)`** — `.approved` → `.send`: "Sending it." / otherwise "Okay, doing
   it." (never "sent" or "done": the effect has not happened yet); `.denied` → "Okay, I won't
   \(verb)."; `.deniedAndRevised` → "Okay, I'll change that and check with you again.";
   `.refusedNotSpeakable(r)` → r; `.noPendingRequest` → "Nothing is waiting for your OK right
   now."; `.notSessionOwned` → "That one is on screen for you to answer."; `.notReady(r)` → r.
2. **Approval state in `TaskBridge`.**
   ```swift
   struct SpokenApprovalState: Sendable, Equatable {
       let requestID: String
       let task: TaskID
       let risk: AgentRisk
       var prompt: SpokenApprovalPrompt
       var promptOutputs: Set<OutputID> = []   // every output that spoke the prompt (delivery, reminder, status)
       var heardStart = false                  // first audible frame of a prompt output (D4)
       var heardFully = false                  // a prompt output reached .finished
       var clarified = false                   // the ask-once clarifier was spoken
       var heldAt: Date?
   }
   private(set) var approvals: [String: SpokenApprovalState] = [:]    // keyed by PermissionRequest.id
   private var revisedRequestTask: Set<TaskID> = []                  // next request of these tasks is "Updated: …"
   private var declinedRevision: [TaskID: Int] = [:]                 // a person said no at this revision

   func approvalPresented(_ request: PermissionRequest)
   func approvalResolved(requestID: String, approved: Bool, via: PermissionAnswerSurface)
   func speakableApproval(sessionID: UUID?) -> SpokenApprovalState?
   func hasSpeakablePendingApproval(sessionID: UUID?) -> Bool { speakableApproval(sessionID: sessionID) != nil }
   func notePromptOutput(requestID: String, output: OutputID)
   func notePromptPlayback(output: OutputID, phase: PlaybackPhase)
   ```
   `enum PermissionAnswerSurface: String, Sendable, Codable { case card, island, voice, withdrawn }`
   (add it to `PermissionGate.swift`).
   - `approvalPresented`: find the record whose `id.raw == request.taskID`; if none or its
     origin is not `.voice`, do nothing (not session-owned: typed tasks, meeting proposals,
     `FunctionCallWatcher`, `FirstIngestionGate` consent — all stay card-only). Otherwise
     `transition(id, to: .needsPermission)` (legal from `.running`/`.delegated`, §2.5),
     `record.pendingRequestID = request.id`, build
     `SpokenApproval.prompt(for: request, review: ToolCallReviewStore.shared.review(for: request), updated: revisedRequestTask.remove(id) != nil)`,
     store the state, emit `.needsPermission(id, requestID:)`, send
     `VoiceSession.shared.send(.approvalPending(requestID: request.id, task: id))` (reducer row 27),
     and, if a session is open, `OutputScheduler.shared.enqueue(DeliveryItem(task: id,
     kind: .approval(requestID: request.id), title: record.title, facts: prompt.text,
     enqueuedAt: Date(), attempts: 0, carriedOver: false))`.
   - `approvalResolved`: remove the state; if the record is `.needsPermission` with this
     request id: `record.pendingRequestID = nil`; approved → `transition(.running)` (or
     `.delegated` for an ACP backend); not approved and not revised → `declinedRevision[id]
     = record.revision` and `transition(.running)` (the worker will now fail or re-plan on
     its own). Emit `.permissionResolved(id, requestID:, approved:)`, send
     `.approvalResolved(requestID:)` to `VoiceSession`, and call
     `OutputScheduler.shared.withdrawApproval(requestID:)`. This one function is what keeps
     card and voice in sync (target 4): every surface resolves through `PermissionGate`, and
     `PermissionGate` calls this.
   - `speakableApproval(sessionID:)`: `PermissionGate.shared.pending` exists, `approvals[pending.id]`
     exists, and `heardStart` is true (a person cannot answer a question they have not
     heard; a "yes" before that goes to the model). `sessionID` is only used to prefer the
     current session's prompt; a voice-origin task from an earlier session is still
     speakable once its prompt has been heard in this one (step 8).
   - `notePromptPlayback`: for the state whose `promptOutputs` contains `output`:
     `.firstAudio` → `heardStart = true`; `.finished` → `heardFully = true`;
     `.interrupted` → nothing (a user who talks over the prompt keeps `heardFully == false`).
   - Finishing a job (P3-03 `finishVoiceJob` / `backendStateChanged`): when the outcome is
     `.failed` and `declinedRevision[id] == record.revision`, set the delivery to
     `.deliveredInContext` instead of queuing it — the person just said no (on either
     surface) and must not then hear "…didn't work: You dismissed …".
3. **`PermissionGate` hooks** (the only file that resolves a request):
   - `present` (:42-51): after `raiseIsland(for:)`, call `TaskBridge.shared.approvalPresented(request)`.
   - `respond(id:approved:duration:scope:via: PermissionAnswerSurface = .card)`: add the
     parameter; after `waiter?.resume` and before `advance()`, call
     `TaskBridge.shared.approvalResolved(requestID: id, approved: approved, via: via)`. In the
     audit row (:109-122) append `" · by voice"` to the detail when `via == .voice`. The
     blocker refusal at :96-103 is unchanged and also applies to voice. `AgentService.swift:76`
     passes `via: .island`; the three `AgentView` calls keep the default `.card`.
   - `cancelMatching` (:163-178) and `cancelPending()` (:143-153): for every request they
     remove, call `approvalResolved(requestID:approved: false, via: .withdrawn)`.
   - New:
     ```swift
     /// A spoken correction to the pending call. The task is not cancelled and the person did not
     /// say no: the waiter gets `false`, the caller has already bumped the task's revision, so the
     /// tool loop drops this call and re-plans with the correction (ToolLoop:1072-1077).
     @discardableResult
     func resolveRevised(id: String, amendment: String) -> Bool
     ```
     Same body as the `approved == false` path of `respond`, with audit detail
     `"Changed by voice: \(amendment.prefix(120))"`, and it calls
     `approvalResolved(requestID: id, approved: false, via: .voice)` **without** setting
     `declinedRevision` (TaskBridge knows it was a revision because step 6 inserted the task
     into `revisedRequestTask` first).
4. **`TaskBridge.respondApproval(requestID:decision:amendment:turn:) -> ApprovalOutcome`**
   (replaces the P3-03 stub; synchronous, like the §2.5 signature):
   ```swift
   guard let pending = PermissionGate.shared.pending,
         requestID == nil || requestID == pending.id else { return .noPendingRequest }
   guard var state = approvals[pending.id] else { return .notSessionOwned }
   if decision == .deny, amendment == nil {
       PermissionGate.shared.respond(id: pending.id, approved: false, via: .voice); return .denied
   }
   if let amendment, !amendment.isEmpty {            // "yes, but …" is a deny with the change (P3-05 contract)
       return revise(state.task, text: amendment, turn: turn) ? .deniedAndRevised : .notReady(SpokenApproval.reply(for: .noPendingRequest, prompt: nil))
   }
   // decision == .approve from here
   guard SpokenApproval.voiceApprovableRisks.contains(pending.risk) else {
       return .refusedNotSpeakable("That one needs your OK on screen.") }
   let review = ToolCallReviewStore.shared.review(for: pending)
   let now = SpokenApproval.fingerprint(review.arguments)
   guard now == state.prompt.fingerprint else {
       return .refusedNotSpeakable("It changed on screen, so please approve it there.") }
   guard state.prompt.voiceMayApprove, review.isReadyToRun else {
       return .refusedNotSpeakable(state.prompt.refusal ?? "That one needs your OK on screen.") }
   if pending.risk == .send, !(state.prompt.readsFullMessage && state.heardFully) {
       return .notReady("I'll read the whole message first. " + state.prompt.text) }   // step 5 speaks it and records the output
   guard PermissionGate.shared.respond(id: pending.id, approved: true, duration: .once,
                                       scope: pending.scope, via: .voice) else {
       return .notReady("Before I can do that: \(review.blockers.first?.prompt ?? "it needs a change on screen").") }
   return .approved
   ```
   Voice never grants `.alwaysThisAction` (only the card's "always" button does).
5. **The approval gate in `Coordinator.respond`** (Coordinator:314). Insert it **after** the
   explicit-cancellation branch (:332-337, "cancel that" still cancels the whole task, which
   also withdraws its request) and **before** the naming-turn branch (:341). It is a routing
   gate (I5): it fires only when `TaskBridge.shared.speakableApproval(sessionID:)` is non-nil,
   it matches exact signatures, and everything it does not recognise reaches the model.
   ```swift
   if let approval = TaskBridge.shared.speakableApproval(sessionID: AgentCaptureController.shared.sessionID) {
       var reading = SpokenApproval.read(text)
       if reading == .unrelated, AgentEntityResolver.namingTarget(in: text) != nil { reading = .amend(text) }
       let answer: String?
       switch reading {
       case .approve: answer = approvalReply(.approve, amendment: nil, approval)
       case .deny:    answer = approvalReply(.deny, amendment: nil, approval)
       case .amend(let words): answer = approvalReply(.deny, amendment: words, approval)
       case .hold where !approvalGate.isRepeat(text, within: TaskBridge.statusRepeatEscalation):
           TaskBridge.shared.noteHeld(approval.requestID); answer = SpokenApproval.holdReply()
       case .ambiguous where !approval.clarified:
           TaskBridge.shared.noteClarified(approval.requestID); answer = SpokenApproval.clarifier(approval.prompt)
       default: answer = nil                               // unrelated, repeated hold, second ambiguity → the model
       }
       if let answer {
           let made: FrontendDecision
           switch reading {
           case .approve: made = .respondPermission(requestID: approval.requestID, decision: .approve, amendment: nil)
           case .deny: made = .respondPermission(requestID: approval.requestID, decision: .deny, amendment: nil)
           case .amend(let words): made = .respondPermission(requestID: approval.requestID, decision: .deny, amendment: words)
           default: made = .answer                          // hold / clarifier: nothing was decided about the request
           }
           // `turnID` is the TurnID P3-06a threads into `handle(_:turn:)`.
           VoiceSession.shared.send(.decisionMade(turnID, made))   // releases the barrier (row 12)
           resolveInput(epoch: inputEpoch)
           AgentAuditLog.shared.record(kind: .reply, title: "Answered an approval by voice", detail: "approval_gate(\(reading))")
           return agent.finishVoiceFrontend(answer, turn: turn, streamed: false)
       }
   }
   ```
   `approvalReply(_:amendment:_:)` calls `TaskBridge.shared.respondApproval(…)`, and when the
   outcome is `.notReady` for the full-message case it speaks the returned text through
   `OutputScheduler.speakFixed` and passes the returned `OutputID` to
   `TaskBridge.shared.notePromptOutput(requestID:output:)` so its `.finished` sets
   `heardFully`. (`finishVoiceFrontend` speaks through `speakFixed` after P3-04b; take the
   `OutputID` from there.) `approvalGate` is a copy of P3-03's `statusGate` (last text + time).
6. **A correction revises; it never cancels** (target 2). In `TaskBridge.revise(_:text:turn:)`
   (P3-03 step 5), when `record.state == .needsPermission` and `record.pendingRequestID` is
   set: `work.append(text)` **first** (bumps `revision`), then
   `revisedRequestTask.insert(id)`, then `PermissionGate.shared.resolveRevised(id: requestID, amendment: text)`,
   then `transition(.running)`; emit `.revised`. The order matters: the tool loop must see
   the new revision when the waiter returns `false`, or it ends the job with "The tool … did
   not run" instead of re-planning. Delete the two `PermissionGate.shared.cancelPending(taskID:)`
   calls at Coordinator:347 and Coordinator:525 (after P3-03 they are inside the coordinator's
   calls into `TaskBridge.revise`; grep `cancelPending(taskID:` — the only callers left must
   be `TaskBridge.cancel` and `ACPSession.swift:88/107`). Reply for a revision while a request
   was pending: "Okay, I'll change that and check with you again." (the `<revise>` path's
   "Got it. I'll use that correction." stays for tasks with nothing pending).
7. **Model route.** Add `case answerApproval` to `LocalVoiceRoute.Intent`
   (LocalVoiceDecision.swift:64-70) and two `@Guide` fields `approval` ("approve", "deny" or
   empty) and `amendment` (the person's own words when the answer changes something; else
   empty) — or, if P3-05 already added them to `FrontendDecisionPayload` only, add them to the
   route struct that fills it. Route rule sentence, appended to P3-05's route rules **only
   while** `TaskBridge.shared.hasSpeakablePendingApproval(sessionID:)` (so the model is never
   told about an approval that does not exist):
   ```text
   - answerApproval: something is waiting for their OK (see work status) and the latest turn answers it.
     approval is approve only for a plain yes with no change, deny for a no. If they want anything
     changed, use deny and copy their exact words into amendment. Never approve for anything else.
   ```
   `workStatusLines` (P3-03 step 8) gains one first line while a request is speakable:
   `Waiting for your OK: \(approval.prompt.text prefix 160)`. Map the intent to
   `FrontendDecisionPayload(intent: "approval", approval:, amendment:)` in
   `streamSplitDecision`, and in `FrontendResponder.decision(from:…)` to
   `.respondPermission(requestID: nil, decision:, amendment:)`.
   In the coordinator's `.respondPermission` branch (replacing P3-05's placeholder):
   if `decision == .approve && amendment == nil && !SpokenApproval.modelApprovalAllowed(text)`
   → treat as `.ambiguous` (clarify once, else answer "It's still on screen whenever you're
   ready."); otherwise `TaskBridge.shared.respondApproval(…)` and speak
   `SpokenApproval.reply(for:prompt:)`. An empty `approval` from the model is `.ambiguous`.
8. **Where the prompt is spoken.**
   - Delivery: `OutputScheduler.windowOpened` (P3-04a step 3) already orders approval items
     first; their text is `item.facts` verbatim (never phrased, D3). When it starts the
     output call `TaskBridge.shared.notePromptOutput(requestID:output:)`, and forward every
     playback phase of that output to `TaskBridge.shared.notePromptPlayback(output:phase:)`
     (the scheduler already maps phases to `OutputID`s). Approval items do **not** call
     `TaskBridge.setDelivery` — `record.delivery` tracks the task's result, not its questions.
   - New `OutputScheduler.withdrawApproval(requestID:)`: remove a queued item for it; if it is
     the active output, `stop(id, byUser: false)` and **do not** requeue it (the request is
     gone — the D6 retry rule does not apply to a resolved approval).
   - A user barge-in over an approval prompt: the item is not replayed (D5), and
     `heardFully` stays false, so a "yes" to a send gets step 4's "I'll read the whole
     message first…" reply, whose playback then counts.
   - `ReceiptAck.reply(for:modelAck:)` `.refusedApprovalPending` (P3-05 step 7) becomes
     `"First, one thing needs your OK. " + approval.prompt.text`, spoken as the turn's fixed
     reply and registered with `notePromptOutput`.
   - `TaskStatusRenderer` needsPermission line (P3-03 step 11) becomes the prompt text, also
     registered with `notePromptOutput`.
   - Carry-over: on `sessionOpened` (P3-04a step 10), for every `approvals` state whose
     request is still `PermissionGate.shared.pending` or queued, reset `heardStart`,
     `heardFully` and `clarified` and enqueue its prompt again with `carriedOver: true`.
     Nothing is enqueued while no session is open — the card is the only surface then.
9. **Never time out into approved.** Add no timer anywhere in this path. `AgentDeliveryTiming.acknowledgementTimeout`
   and D6's `maxRetries` may end *delivery attempts* of a prompt (the item becomes `failed`,
   which only means "stop speaking it"); they never call `respond`. `PermissionGate.ask` stays
   unbounded (ToolLoop:1031-1036 already refuses to bound writes). Write that sentence as a
   comment on `SpokenApproval`.
9b. **Conversation leaves the ACP card up** (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V23; target 9). Verified
   2026-09-24: `AgentCaptureController.emitTurn` (Capture:1319) calls
   `_ = ACPConfirmationGate.shared.cancel()` at Capture:1382 on **every** committed non-hesitation
   turn, before the turn is routed; `endSession` (Capture:358) does the same at :373. The card is
   raised by `RealtimeAgent.waitForACPConfirmation` (`Agent/RealtimeAgent+ToolLoop.swift:195-214`,
   `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.request(choice, utterance:)` at :207), and after P3-03b also by
   `TaskBridge.spawn`'s detached request (P3-03 step 16). `ACPConfirmationGate.cancel()`
   (`Agent/ACPConfirmation.swift:118`) resumes the waiter with `.cancelled`.
   - Delete the call at Capture:1382. Keep the one in `endSession` (:373) — session end still
     dismisses.
   - In `VoiceConversationCoordinator.respond`, **before** the approval gate (step 5) and before
     P3-03's cancel gate: if `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.pending != nil` and
     `VoiceTurnPolicy.isExplicitWorkCancellation(text)`, call `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.cancel()`,
     `resolveInput(epoch:)`, and reply "Okay, I won't start that." with no model call. The card is
     the newest thing waiting, so the phrase means the card, not a running task; a second
     "cancel" then reaches the task gate.
   - In the `.spawn` branch (P3-05 step 6) — and, until P3-05 lands, the `.newWork` branch
     (Coordinator:553) and the deterministic routes that submit work (tool-shape gate,
     pending-intent ack, denial backstop) — call `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.cancel()` before
     `TaskBridge.shared.spawn`. Put this in one helper, `dismissACPCardForNewWork()`, so no
     submit path is missed.
   - Every other route (answer, capabilities, status, revise, approval, a discarded or
     not-addressed turn from P3-10) leaves the card alone.
   - Record `AgentAuditLog` rows `detail: "acp_card_dismissed(cancel|new_work|session_end)"`
     (no row for "left up").
10. **AGENTS.md**, new paragraph after "The agent searches your mail without asking, and
    can't send anything without asking": "**A spoken yes is an approval only for what was
    heard.** A voice task's approval is read out at the next pause (for a send: who, what
    about, and the whole message when it is 400 characters or less). 'Yes' approves, 'no'
    denies, 'wait' holds, and a yes or no with a change revises the same task instead of
    cancelling it. Voice never approves purchases, deletions or privileged actions, never a
    send whose whole message was not heard, never a request that changed on screen after it
    was read, and never on the model's reading alone. Card and voice resolve through
    `PermissionGate`, so answering one withdraws the other. Nothing times out into approved."
    README: add `SpokenApproval.swift` under `Agent/Duplex/`.

**Do not:**
- Do not approve on the model's reading alone, on silence, on a timeout, on session close or on a failed delivery (I10; AGENTS permission model). `modelApprovalAllowed` and `read(_:) == .approve` are the only ways in.
- Do not let voice approve `.purchase`, `.destructive` or `.privileged`, or grant `.alwaysThisAction`; do not approve a `.send` whose full message was not heard to the end, or a request whose review changed after its prompt was built.
- Do not bypass `PermissionGate.respond`: it holds the blocker refusal (:96-103), the audit row and the grant; every surface resolves through it.
- Do not edit the pending call's arguments from speech (e.g. write "Marcus" into the `to` field). A spoken change goes through the planner and the review builder's provenance checks, which is what stops an invented address (ToolCallReview.swift:1-18); only the card may edit fields.
- Do not cancel the task, and do not record a person's "no", when the turn was a correction: bump the revision **before** resolving the request.
- Do not let the gate subtract (I5): exact signatures only, only while a heard request is pending; unrelated words, a repeated hold within 5 s and a second ambiguity reach the model. Keep the positive corpus in `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` green.
- Do not phrase approval prompts with the model (D3), and do not speak ids, message ids or tool ids (`reply_email`'s title is an id).
- Do not answer the ACP harness card (`ACPConfirmationGate`) by voice in this task, and do not answer non-voice requests (meeting proposals, `FunctionCallWatcher`, first-use consent) by voice: they return `.notSessionOwned`. (H-audit 2026-09-23) Voice may *dismiss* the ACP card — explicit cancel phrase, new work, session end (step 9b) — never "Run once" it; "yes" with the card up is not a confirmation.
- Do not dismiss the ACP card from the capture layer again (Capture:1382), and do not dismiss it on a hesitation, a discarded echo, a status question or an ordinary answer (H-audit 2026-09-23, H2 V23).
- Do not let the self-test write the audit log, `agent-tasks.json` or grants (I8: P0-11 seams; `PermissionGrantStore` already reads and writes nothing under the harness, PermissionBroker.swift:68-70). Never call `RunLog.record`.
- Do not use `MainActor.assumeIsolated` (AGENTS: it crashes the process).

**Red-first self-test:** extend `--selftest-voice-turn-routing`
(`VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.runTurnRouting`, VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.swift:695)
with an **approval block**, placed before the final summary. It runs the production path —
coordinator → `TaskBridge` job → real `runPlannedToolLoop` → `AgentToolExecutor.run` →
`ActionOrchestrator.execute` → `PermissionGate.ask` → `mayCommitEffect` → fire — with the
side effect replaced:
- Fixtures: `TaskBridge.shared.workerProviderForTesting = ApprovalWorkerProvider(state:)`, a
  scripted `LLMProvider` (pattern: `ToolLoopTestProvider`, RealtimeAgentToolLoopSelfTest.swift:664-700)
  whose round 1 emits
  `<tool_call>{"name":"send_email","arguments":{"to":"anna@northwindtraders.com","subject":"Deck","body":"The deck is ready."},"rationale":"asked"}</tool_call>`
  — or, when the prompt it receives contains "Marcus", the same call with
  `"to":"marcus@northwindtraders.com"` — and whose next round answers "Sent." Record the rounds.
  `AgentToolExecutor.fireOverrideForTesting` (P3-02) appends `(toolID, arguments, firedAt)`
  to `commits` and returns a summary; it never calls `gws`. A helper `readyCard()` waits
  (≤ 3 s, 10 ms polls) for `PermissionGate.shared.pending`, then calls
  `ToolCallReviewStore.shared.confirm(id:field:)` for each blocker and fails with
  `"approval fixture review not ready"` if `isReadyToRun` is still false. Playback of the
  prompt is simulated with the same `RecordingSpeechBacking` + `notifyTestingFirstAudio` /
  `notifyTestingAudioFinished` helpers `--selftest-voice-delivery` uses (P3-04), and time with
  `VoiceSession.shared.nowForTesting` so the 350 ms window is simulated, not slept.
- Cases (failure strings in quotes):
  1. **Heard-first rule.** Start "Email Anna that the deck is ready." (`<use_tools/>`
     script). When the card is up and the prompt is queued but not yet played, say "Yes." →
     it reaches the model (`streamCalls` +1), `commits` is empty, the request is still
     pending. `"a yes approved a prompt nobody had heard"`.
  2. **Prompt content.** Open the window; the spoken prompt contains "anna at northwind
     traders dot com", "Deck" and "The deck is ready." and ends "Should I send it?"; it
     contains no `@`. `"approval prompt missed the recipient or the message"`.
  3. **"wait" holds.** Finish the prompt's playback; say "Wait." → reply
     `SpokenApproval.holdReply()`; after 2 s of real time the request is still pending and
     `commits` is empty. `"hold resolved or timed out the approval"`.
  4. **"okay" asks once.** Say "Okay." → reply is the clarifier; say "Okay." again → it
     reaches the model (`streamCalls` +1) and the request is still pending; `commits` empty.
     `"ambiguous answer approved, or clarified twice"`.
  5. **"yes, but send it to Marcus instead" revises.** → reply "Okay, I'll change that and
     check with you again."; the task's record is not terminal, `revision == 1`, its
     `AgentTaskManager` mirror is not `.cancelled`; within 3 s a **new** request is pending
     whose arguments contain `marcus@northwindtraders.com` and whose prompt starts
     "Updated:"; `commits` is empty. `"correction cancelled the task or committed the old version"`.
  6. **"yes" approves exactly the heard version.** Play the new prompt to the end; say
     "Yes." → reply "Sending it."; within 3 s `commits.count == 1` with `to ==
     marcus@northwindtraders.com`; `PermissionGate.shared.pending == nil`; the island kind is
     not `.agentProposal`. `"spoken approval did not commit exactly once to the revised recipient"`.
  7. **No write before classification.** Case 6 again on a fresh task, but after "Yes." hold
     a second committed turn unclassified (`VoiceSession.shared.send(.inputActivity(t2))`
     with no decision): `commits` stays empty for 500 ms; release it
     (`.decisionMade(t2, .answer)`) → the commit happens. `"write committed while a turn was unclassified"`.
  8. **Card and voice in sync.** Fresh task, prompt queued. Call
     `PermissionGate.shared.respond(id:approved: true)` (what the card's Approve button calls,
     AgentView.swift:285) → the queued approval item is withdrawn (never spoken:
     `recorder.spoken` has no "Should I send it?" for this request), `VoiceSession.shared.state.pendingApproval == nil`,
     `commits.count` goes up by 1; then "Yes." reaches the model and `commits` does not change.
     `"card approval left the spoken prompt or double-committed"`.
  9. **"no" denies quietly.** Fresh task; after the prompt, "No." → reply "Okay, I won't send
     it."; `commits` unchanged; the job ends and nothing more is spoken about it (its
     delivery is `.deliveredInContext`). `"denied send was committed or announced"`.
  10. **Risk and length refusals.** (a) A `.destructive` request: for this case install
      `TaskBridge.shared.workerForTesting` (P3-03) with a closure that calls
      `await PermissionGate.shared.ask(PermissionRequest(toolID: "selftest.delete", title: "Delete the folder Old drafts", detail: "test", risk: .destructive, arguments: [:], taskID: work.id.uuidString))`
      and records the returned Bool. (b) A `send_email` whose scripted body is 600
      characters. For both, after the prompt has played: "Yes." → `.refusedNotSpeakable`,
      the reply names the screen, `commits` is unchanged, the request is still pending and
      (a)'s `ask` has not returned. `"voice approved a request it must never approve"`.
  11. **Positive corpus untouched.** With no pending request, "Yes.", "No." and "Wait."
      each reach the model (`streamCalls` +3). `"approval gate fired with nothing pending"`.
  12. **Model reading is not enough.** With a heard prompt, script the route
      `{"intent":"approval","approval":"approve"}` for "What's the weather like?" → the
      clarifier, not an approval; `commits` unchanged. `"model-only approval was accepted"`.
  13. (H-audit 2026-09-23, V23) **The ACP card survives conversation.** Fixture as in
      `ACPConfirmation.runSelfTest` (Agent/ACPConfirmation.swift:145-160):
      `AgentHarnessRouter.shared.availabilityProbe = { _ in false }`,
      `let choice = AgentHarnessRouter.shared.choose(for: "use claude code to fix the tests")`
      (`needsACPConfirmation == true`), `let waiter = Task { await ACPConfirmationGate.shared.request(choice, utterance: "use claude code to fix the tests") }`,
      `await Task.yield()`. (a) `handle("What's that?")` with a scripted `<answer/>` →
      `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.pending != nil`. (b) `handle("Cancel.")` → no model call
      (`streamCalls` unchanged), `pending == nil`, `await waiter.value == .cancelled`, reply
      "Okay, I won't start that." (c) Re-raise; `handle("Check my calendar for tomorrow.")`
      scripted as new work (`<use_tools/>`, or the `spawn` decision after P3-05) → `pending == nil`.
      Failures: `"conversation dismissed the ACP card"`, `"cancel did not dismiss the ACP card"`,
      `"new work left a stale ACP card"`. Restore in `defer`:
      `AgentHarnessRouter.shared.resetForTesting()`, `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.resetForTesting()`.
- (H-audit 2026-09-23, V23) `--selftest-voice-turns` gains one capture-level case in
  `AgentCaptureController.overlappingBackchannelSelfTestFailures` (Capture:1972; it already
  begins a session with `captureAudio: false` and a `turnHandlerForTesting` sink): raise the card
  as in case 13, then `capture.simulateSpeech("What's that?")`, `capture.simulateSilence()`,
  `_ = await capture.considerEndpoint()`, `await capture.waitForActiveTurnForTesting()` → the
  test handler received the turn **and** `ACPConfirmationGate.shared.pending != nil`; then
  `await capture.endSession()` → `pending == nil` and the waiter returned `.cancelled`.
  Failures (as `VOICE_TURNS_WRONG:` lines): `"a committed turn dismissed the ACP card"`,
  `"session end left the ACP card up"`. **Red today:** the first fails (Capture:1382).
- Reset in a `defer`: `workerProviderForTesting`, `fireOverrideForTesting`, `nowForTesting`,
  `PermissionGate.shared.cancelPending()`, `ToolCallReviewStore.shared.removeAll()`,
  `coordinator.resetForTesting()`. Markers unchanged: `VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_WRONG: <reason>`
  per failure, final `VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_OK` / `VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_FAILED`.
- **Confirm red:** add the block before step 1 (with `respondApproval` still the P3-03
  stub). Expected on the unmodified code: case 2 fails (no prompt is ever spoken), case 3
  and 4 fail (the turn reaches the model or is treated as noise; the clarifier is never
  said), case 5 fails (today's `cancelPending(taskID:)` path: the reply is "Got it. I'll
  use that correction." and no "Updated:" prompt), case 6 fails (nothing commits: the
  "yes" never reaches `PermissionGate`). Record the failure lines in STATUS.md, then
  implement. (H-audit 2026-09-23) Case 13(b) is red today too — "Cancel." is not in the
  phrase list and no gate looks at the ACP card, so the turn reaches the model
  (`"cancel did not dismiss the ACP card"`); case 13(a) passes only by accident in the

**Verify:**
```bash
make build                      # never bare `swift build`; on "input file was modified during the build", wait and retry
make install OPEN=0             # never omit OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turn-routing
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turns
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-tool-review
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-delivery

Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-concurrent-voice
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-tasks
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-acp
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-guided        # D9 first-success script: proposal → approval → executed

```
None of these needs the microphone; none uses `--via-open`.

**Success threshold:** approval block 12/12, plus case 13 and the `--selftest-voice-turns` ACP-card case (H-audit 2026-09-23); `grep -n "ACPConfirmationGate" Sources/NextNotes/Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift` shows only the `endSession` call; `grep -n "cancelPending(taskID:" Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift`
empty; every `PermissionGate.respond` caller either passes `via:` or is a card/island
button; `--selftest-tool-review`, `--selftest-guided`, `--selftest-routine-authority` OK
(the card path is unchanged); `make acceptance TIER=core` has no new FAIL.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: a false approval — the most serious failure this phase can have.
Guards in depth: exact signatures, heard-first, full-message-heard for sends, fingerprint
of what was read, risk classes, the review's own blocker refusal, and `mayCommitEffect`;
cases 1, 7, 10 and 12 pin them. Risk: the prompt is long and the person talks over it — they
get "I'll read the whole message first" once and the send waits; acceptable. Risk: a
correction loop (revise → new prompt → revise …) — each round is audible and the person can
say "cancel that"; log `approval_gate(amend)` so repeated amends show in the audit feed.
Rollback: disable the gate and the route rule with one constant
(`SpokenApproval.isEnabled = false`, checked in steps 5, 7 and 8) — the card path and
P3-03/P3-05 behaviour return, except that corrections still revise instead of cancel
(keep that; it is the safer of the two).

- [ ] `TaskBridge` approval state, `respondApproval`, PermissionGate hooks (`via:`, `resolveRevised`); corrections revise instead of cancelling; the two coordinator `cancelPending(taskID:)` calls deleted
- [ ] approval prompts delivered first at the window, withdrawn when the card answers; carry-over on the next session
- [ ] approval gate + `answerApproval` route + `.respondPermission` branch; `hasSpeakablePendingApproval` exposed for P5
- [ ] approval block red → green (failure lines before/after in STATUS.md); regression flags OK
- [ ] (H-audit 2026-09-23) ACP card: capture no longer dismisses it per turn; cancel phrase / new work / session end dismiss it; case 13 and the `--selftest-voice-turns` card case red → green
- [ ] AGENTS.md paragraph and README tree line added

### P3-09 — C10 island state owned by VoiceSession (workers never drive it)

*Migration step 6.*

**Actual bounded progress,2026-10-04:** normal409 RED12/32 →410 SAME32 PASS through
real local/ACP/typed/scheduled publishers, unchanged fixture and exact seven-producer
compiler delta. The separately qualified408/409 shared-cancellation repair makes the
original test fully qualify without changing its order/deadlines. Related41010/11
passes functionally; C03/F09 unchanged5ms targets fail. Three consecutive SAME32 runs and all five remaining UI/concurrency/task flags pass.
Explicit A7 now qualifies418 RED2 ownership assertions →419 SAME scenario PASS:
both have four samples/two actual-step samples at20ms, worker attempts18→0 and
zero worker-driven kind samples. SAME COMMON fixes the external-effect substitute's
publication bypass before both runs; original417 stays INPUT_INVALID. Related419 completes27/28, including three consecutive normal32 runs and
worker2818; only inherited C03/F09 receipt5ms fails. Full CORE stays open. Source is
staged-only; physical sound/display/focus, whole-App reads and P3-06b effect ownership
remain open. [Exact evidence](../../../Tests/Reports/agent-overhaul-closure/p3-task-bridge-production/manager-voice-worker-ownership-production/island409410-qualified-results.json).


**Finding(s):** C10; C report §0 item 14, §4 rank 15, §6 Step 3 ("workers stop touching `IslandState` and `ActivationController`")  **Effort:** 1.5–2 days  **Depends on:** P3-06a (`VoiceSession` shell, `IslandProjection`, `publishIsland`), P3-03a (ledger, `noteProgress`, worker tasks owned by `TaskBridge`); lands after P3-03b (§3 step 6)  **Unblocks:** P3-06b (the island is already the session's), P3-07c (A6 uses this task's counters)

**Why (what the user experiences today):** In the middle of a conversation the notch switches
to a worker's card: the user asks "what is a haiku?" while a background job is looking for a
folder, and the island shows "Looking for Q3 budget…" with the character in its *browsing*
pose instead of the conversation's *thinking* — and it stays on the worker's card after the
answer, until the next frontend turn happens to write "listening" again. Nothing is
corrupted (C report: "visible, not corrupting"), but the one mark under the notch that says
what the agent is doing right now is saying something else, which breaks the rule both
vocabulary tables in AGENTS.md live under: each state means exactly one thing.

- `Sources/NextNotes/Activation/ActivationController.swift` (gated `markWorking`/`markListening`/`finishAgent`)
