**Read for context (do not change):**
- §2.3 of this file: `IslandProjection` and reducer rows 1, 3, 11, 16, 19, 20, 27, 28 are the only places the conversation's island state changes.
- AGENTS.md "The orb vocabulary", "Where orbs may go, and how many" and "The agent's character": one animating mark per screen; the island wears the character for the agent's own states and the orb for everything else; red is recording only; never infer a state from a progress title; a compact row does not animate.
- `Sources/NextNotes/UI/Island/IslandState.swift:232-240` (`avatarState`), `:365-411` (`propose`, `showAgentListening`, `showAgentWork`, `showAgentReply`, `apply`), `:496-525` (`liveKind`).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Activity/AgentActivityStore.swift:61-75` (`update` sets the global `liveAvatarState`), `:103-111` (`liveSteps`).
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Identity/AgentAvatarSelfTest.swift:213-230` (how `--selftest-avatar` pins "the island wears the character for the agent's own states").

**Current behaviour (verified 2026-09-23):**
- The worker write that flips the island:
  ```swift
  // Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift:516-521
  func beginWork(title: String) {
      isThinking = true
      progressTitle = title.isEmpty ? "Working…" : title
      ActivationController.shared.markWorking()
      IslandState.shared.showAgentWork(title: progressTitle)
  }
  ```
  A voice worker (`RealtimeAgent(voiceWorker:)`, `isVoiceWorker == true`) reaches it through
  `runVoiceObjective` (RealtimeAgent.swift:130-139) → `runPlannedToolLoop` (ToolLoop:725) →
  `runDirectIntent` (ToolLoop:1108-1113, `beginWork(title: intent.progressTitle)`) for every
  "open …" / "find the folder …" objective. `ActivationController` and `IslandState.shared`
  are process-wide, so the worker's instance writes the conversation's card.
- **Correction to the C report:** `runTool` (ToolLoop:299-304, `IslandState.shared.showAgentWork`)
  is reached only from `perform(_:)` (ToolLoop:249), whose one caller is
  `runWithLocalToolsOnce` (ToolLoop:228) — the typed path, not voice workers. `beginWork`
  at ToolLoop:385/456/473 is in the frontend/typed `runModelTurn`. Only `runDirectIntent`
  is a worker path today; the gate below covers all of them anyway.
- Every tool a worker fires writes the global activity projection:
  `AgentToolExecutor.run` → `AgentActivityStore.shared.update(taskID:kind:title:avatar:)`
  (AgentToolExecutor.swift:178-187), which sets `liveAvatarState` for the whole app
  (AgentActivityStore.swift:74), then `IslandState.shared.showBackgroundAgentWork` for a
  task id (AgentToolExecutor.swift:188-192). `showBackgroundAgentWork` is already skipped
  while the island shows an agent card (`hasForegroundVoiceActivity`, ActionRuntime.swift:898-911),
  but once a worker's `beginWork` has put up `.agentWorking`, that card *is* the "foreground".
- The island then reads worker state for the conversation's card: `liveKind`
  (IslandState.swift:496-510) turns `ActivationController.mode == .agentWorking` into the
  **active task's** steps (`AgentActivityStore.liveSteps`), and `avatarState` (:232-240)
  turns `.agentWorking` into `AgentActivityStore.shared.liveAvatarState` — the last worker
  tool's pose (`browsing` for a read, `writing`, `sending`…).
- The conversation's own writes are spread over four objects: `AgentCaptureController`
  (:255 `markListening`, :268/:398/:496/:912 `showAgentListening`, :1364-1365 after each
  turn, :369-375 the closing notice), `RealtimeAgent.beginVoiceFrontend` (:164-169 →
  `beginWork("Listening and thinking…")`), `RealtimeAgent.finish` (:758-767), and
  `ActivationController.beginAgent` (:51-66). After P3-06a, `VoiceSession` computes the
  right `IslandProjection` in shadow mode but does not execute `publishIsland`.

**Target behaviour:**
1. While a voice session is open, `VoiceSession` is the only writer of the agent's island
   cards (`showAgentWork`, `showAgentListening`, `showBackgroundAgentWork`,
   `showBackgroundAgentReply`) and of `ActivationController.markWorking/markListening/finishAgent`.
   Any other caller is rejected and counted by `IslandOwnership.mayWrite(_:)`. Cards that
   ask a question (`propose`), notices (`showAgentReply`, reminders), `dismissNotice`, and
   every dictation/meeting state are not affected.
2. Workers never attempt such a write: they report progress to `TaskBridge`
   (`noteProgress`) and emit `TaskEvent`s. `IslandOwnership.workerAttemptTotal == 0` across
   the voice self-tests.
3. The island shows the **conversation state first**: `.listening` → the listening card and
   the `listening` character; `.thinking` → "Thinking…" and the `thinking` character, never a
   worker's step title or pose; `.speaking` → the listening card; `.proposal` → the
   `PermissionGate` card (a question outranks a readout, as today).
4. Under it, in the expanded card only, a **still** secondary chip says what background work
   exists: one task → its current consumer step title (or its title); a pending approval →
   "Waiting for your OK"; several → "N things in progress". No orb, no animation, not red,
   no tool ids. The collapsed island keeps exactly one animated mark: the conversation's
   character.
5. When the session closes, `VoiceSession` writes nothing more and every legacy writer works
   exactly as before (background task cards, the closing "Going quiet." notice).

**Steps:**
1. **`IslandOwnership.swift`** (new):
   ```swift
   /// Set by TaskBridge around every worker run. Task-locals are inherited by child tasks
   /// (`Task {}` — not `Task.detached`), so every call a worker makes carries it.
   enum VoiceWorkerScope { @TaskLocal static var isWorker = false }
   /// Set only by VoiceSession while it executes `publishIsland` / `listeningFeed`.
   enum IslandWriteScope { @TaskLocal static var isVoiceSession = false }

   struct IslandTaskIndicator: Equatable, Sendable {
       let label: String      // consumer words; never a tool id, path or URL
       let count: Int
   }

   @MainActor
   enum IslandOwnership {
       /// Rollback switch: false restores the legacy writers everywhere.
       static let isEnabled = true
       static var sessionOwnsIsland: Bool { isEnabled && VoiceSession.shared.state.phase == .open }
       private(set) static var rejected: [String: Int] = [:]        // call site → count
       private(set) static var workerAttempts: [String: Int] = [:]  // the subset made inside VoiceWorkerScope
       static var workerAttemptTotal: Int { workerAttempts.values.reduce(0, +) }

       /// Every gated writer calls this first and returns when it is false.
       static func mayWrite(_ site: String) -> Bool {
           guard sessionOwnsIsland, !IslandWriteScope.isVoiceSession else { return true }
           rejected[site, default: 0] += 1
           if VoiceWorkerScope.isWorker { workerAttempts[site, default: 0] += 1 }
           Log.agent.debug("island write rejected while a voice session is open: \(site, privacy: .public)")
           return false
       }
       static func resetCountersForTesting() { rejected = [:]; workerAttempts = [:] }
   }
   ```
2. **Gate the writers** (first line of each, with its own site string):
   `IslandState.showAgentWork(title:)` and `showAgentWork(steps:current:total:)`
   (IslandState.swift:376-393), `showAgentListening(transcript:level:)` (:369-374),
   `showBackgroundAgentWork`/`showBackgroundAgentReply` (ActionRuntime.swift:908-917, keep
   their existing guard after it), `ActivationController.markWorking()` (:95-97),
   `markListening()` (:78-80), `finishAgent()` (:99-102). Do **not** gate `propose`,
   `showAgentReply`, `dismissNotice`, `apply` (the self-test entry point), or anything in
   `liveKind` for dictation and meetings.
3. **Workers stop writing.**
   - `RealtimeAgent.beginWork` (:516-521):
     ```swift
     func beginWork(title: String) {
         isThinking = true
         progressTitle = title.isEmpty ? "Working…" : title
         if isVoiceWorker {
             // C10: a worker reports progress to the ledger; it never drives the island.
             if let id = voiceWork?.id.uuidString {
                 TaskBridge.shared.noteProgress(taskID: id,
                     step: AgentActivityStore.shared.steps(taskID: id).count + 1, title: progressTitle)
             }
             return
         }
         guard IslandOwnership.mayWrite("RealtimeAgent.beginWork") else { return }  // the session publishes .thinking
         ActivationController.shared.markWorking()
         IslandState.shared.showAgentWork(title: progressTitle)
     }
     ```
   - `AgentToolExecutor.run` (:188-192): wrap the two island lines in
     `if !(VoiceWorkerScope.isWorker && IslandOwnership.sessionOwnsIsland) { … }`. The
     `AgentActivityStore.update` line above stays: it is how progress reaches
     `TaskBridge.noteProgress` (P3-03 step 7) and the Agent tab. With no session open, a
     worker's background card still shows, as today.
   - `TaskBridge`: run every local worker inside the scope — in the job task of P3-03 step 3
     (after P3-07c, inside `LocalToolLoopWorker.run`, nested with `SlotScope`):
     ```swift
     let result = await VoiceWorkerScope.$isWorker.withValue(true) { await worker.runVoiceObjective() }
     ```
     For ACP tasks nothing changes: `ACPAgentBackend.swift:314` and `AgentTaskManager.swift:58,74,95,318,330`
     already go through `showBackgroundAgentWork`/`showBackgroundAgentReply`, which the gate
     now rejects while a session is open (they are not in worker scope, so they count in
     `rejected` only).
4. **`VoiceSession` executes `publishIsland`** — in shadow mode too (P3-06a step 3 lists it
   as the command that becomes live "once P3-09 landed"):
   ```swift
   private func applyIsland(_ projection: IslandProjection) {
       IslandWriteScope.$isVoiceSession.withValue(true) {
           let island = IslandState.shared, capture = AgentCaptureController.shared
           island.voiceProjection = projection == .none ? nil : projection
           switch projection {
           case .none: island.sessionTaskIndicator = nil          // the legacy closing notice follows
           case .listening:
               ActivationController.shared.markListening()
               island.showAgentListening(transcript: capture.transcript, level: capture.level)
           case .speaking:
               ActivationController.shared.markListening()
               island.showAgentListening(transcript: "", level: 0)
           case .thinking:
               ActivationController.shared.markWorking()
               island.showAgentWork(title: IslandState.conversationThinkingTitle)   // "Thinking…"
           case .proposal: break                                   // PermissionGate already raised the card
           }
       }
   }
   /// Capture's live transcript and level, drawn only on the conversation's listening card.
   func listeningFeed(transcript: String, level: Float, force: Bool = false) {
       guard force || state.island == .listening || state.island == .speaking || !IslandOwnership.sessionOwnsIsland else { return }
       IslandWriteScope.$isVoiceSession.withValue(true) {
           IslandState.shared.showAgentListening(transcript: transcript, level: level)
       }
   }
   /// The still chip under the conversation card. Called after every reduced `.task` event
   /// and after `.approvalPending` / `.approvalResolved`.
   func publishTaskIndicator() {
       guard state.phase == .open, let sessionID = state.sessionID else { return }
       IslandState.shared.sessionTaskIndicator = TaskBridge.shared.islandIndicator(sessionID: sessionID)
   }
   ```
   The indicator is deliberately **not** part of `IslandProjection`: step titles change on
   every tool call, are observability only (D9), and must not produce reducer commands or
   `publishIsland` churn. `VoiceSession` is still its only writer.
5. **`TaskBridge.islandIndicator(sessionID:) -> IslandTaskIndicator?`**: the session's
   non-terminal records (`sessionTasks(sessionID)` filtered by `!state.isTerminal`); none →
   nil; any `needsPermission` → `("Waiting for your OK", n)`; exactly one → its
   `progress?.stepTitle` passed through `AgentActivityProjector.isPublic` (else its `title`);
   several → `("\(n) things in progress", n)`. No ids, paths, URLs or tool names (the same
   rule as `TaskStatusRenderer`).
6. **`IslandState`** gains:
   ```swift
   /// Set only by VoiceSession. Non-nil while a voice session is open.
   var voiceProjection: IslandProjection?
   var sessionTaskIndicator: IslandTaskIndicator?
   static let conversationThinkingTitle = "Thinking\u{2026}"
   ```
   - `liveKind` (:496-510): at the top of the `.agentWorking` branch, before reading
     `AgentActivityStore.liveSteps`:
     `if voiceProjection == .thinking { return .agentWorking(steps: [Self.conversationThinkingTitle], current: 1, total: 1) }`.
   - `avatarState` (:232-240): `case .agentWorking: voiceProjection == .thinking ? .thinking : (AgentActivityStore.shared.liveAvatarState ?? .thinking)`.
     The avatar state is still decided by what the session is doing, not read back from a
     title (AGENTS: "Never infer a state from a progress title").
   - Setting `voiceProjection` or `sessionTaskIndicator` calls `refresh()` (:470) so the live
     state is recomputed; both are read in `observe()`'s tracking block.
7. **`IslandView`**: in the expanded agent card (`.agentListening` and `.agentWorking`), when
   `state.sessionTaskIndicator` is non-nil, draw one `StatusChip(text: indicator.label)`
   (UI/Components/StatusChip.swift) under the existing content, with DS tokens only
   (`DS.Space`, `DS.Color.info`; add a token if a size is missing — no literals). It is a
   `Text` in a chip: no `ThinkingOrb`, no `TimelineView`, no animation, not `DS.Color.record`.
   The collapsed island is unchanged: its single mark is the conversation's character.
   Read `state.sessionTaskIndicator` in `IslandState`, never a singleton from the view body
   (IslandState.swift:246-252 explains the `MainActor.assumeIsolated` crash that rule prevents).
8. **Conversation writers go through the session.**
   - `AgentCaptureController.beginSession`: delete `ActivationController.shared.markListening()`
     (:255) and `IslandState.shared.showAgentListening(transcript: "", level: 0)` (:268);
     reducer row 1 publishes `.listening` from the `.sessionOpened` event P3-06a sends at
     :250. Check the event is sent **before** the old line positions, or the island is blank
     for one frame.
   - Replace the three live-transcript writes (:398 `simulateSpeech`, :496 recogniser
     partials, :912 `noteLevel`) with `VoiceSession.shared.listeningFeed(transcript:level:force:)`,
     passing `force: RealtimeAgent.shared.voiceInputActive` so barge-in feedback while the
     agent thinks behaves as today; keep their existing surrounding conditions until P3-06b
     deletes the flags.
   - Delete the post-turn writes in `emitTurn`'s turn task (:1364-1365); reducer rows 16/20
     publish `.listening` when the response ends or its playback finishes.
   - `endSession` (:369-375): make sure P3-06a's `.sessionClosed` is sent **before** the
     closing notice (`showAgentReply("Going quiet." / "Stopped.")` and `finishAgent()`), so
     those legacy writes run with the session already closed and are allowed.
   - `RealtimeAgent.finish` (:758-767): keep the session branch as is — its
     `markListening()`/`showAgentListening` calls are now rejected while the session owns
     the island and the reducer publishes the same state; the `else` branch (no session)
     is unchanged. `RealtimeAgent.beginVoiceFrontend` (:164-169) → `beginWork` is rejected
     the same way (row 11 already published `.thinking`).
   - The Stop path (`RealtimeAgent.cancel`, :396-426) and `ActivationController.beginAgent`
     (:51-66): run after `.sessionClosed` / before `.sessionOpened` respectively, so they are
     allowed; if either is observed in `IslandOwnership.rejected` during the self-tests,
     move its write after the event rather than wrapping it in `IslandWriteScope`.
9. **`AGENTS.md`**, new paragraph after "Live conversation has a different model owner":
   "**The voice session owns the island.** While a voice session is open only
   `VoiceSession` writes the agent's island cards and `ActivationController`;
   `IslandOwnership.mayWrite` rejects and counts every other writer. Background workers run
   inside `VoiceWorkerScope`, report progress to `TaskBridge`, and appear only as a still
   chip under the conversation card. The island's one animated mark is the conversation's
   character — a worker's step title or pose never replaces it. Proposal cards and notices
   still outrank it; nothing changes outside a session. `--selftest-island` and
   `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` A7 fail if a worker writes." README: add
   `IslandOwnership.swift` under `Agent/Duplex/`.

**Do not:**
- Do not let any code path other than `VoiceSession` (inside `IslandWriteScope`) write the agent cards or `ActivationController` while a session is open; do not "fix" a rejected write by wrapping it in `IslandWriteScope` — route it through a `VoiceEvent` instead.
- Do not add a second animated mark to the island (AGENTS: one animating orb per screen; a compact row does not animate). The task chip is still text.
- Do not use red for the chip or for any non-recording state (AGENTS: red means recording).
- Do not derive the character's state from a progress title or step text; map from the projection and `AgentActivityKind`/tool risk only (AGENTS "The agent's character").
- Do not put tool ids, paths, URLs or developer words in the chip (`--selftest-ui-strings` bans developer words in UI text).
- Do not block `propose`, notices, dictation or meeting states — a question outranks a readout (IslandState.swift:60-63).
- Do not use `MainActor.assumeIsolated` in the gate or the executor (AGENTS: it crashes the process); everything here is already `@MainActor`.
- Do not rewrite `AgentCaptureController`'s VAD, EOU or echo logic; only replace its island writes.
- Do not use `Task.detached` for worker work: it drops the `VoiceWorkerScope` task-local and the gate stops seeing the worker.
- Do not `scaleEffect` the portrait or an orb to fit the chip row (AGENTS).

**Red-first self-test:** extend `--selftest-island` (`runIslandSelfTest`, NextNotesApp.swift:3433)
— chosen over `--selftest-avatar` because the defect is *who writes* the island's state
machine, which is what `--selftest-island` grades ("state machine behaves"); `--selftest-avatar`
pins the state→character mapping, which is unchanged and must stay green. Add
`private static func islandVoiceOwnershipFailures() async -> [String]` and call it after
`islandViewFailures()` (:3455) and **before** the on-screen panel part. It uses
`IslandState.shared` and `ActivationController.shared` (the objects production writes) and
restores them at the end.
- Setup: `IslandOwnership.resetCountersForTesting()`; `AgentSession.shared.clear()`;
  `VoiceConversationCoordinator.shared.resetForTesting()`; `RecordingSpeechBacking` (as
  `--selftest-voice-delivery` uses); `coordinator.streamForTesting` returns
  `<answer/>A haiku is a short poem.` only after a `release()` (a parked answer);
  `TaskBridge.shared.workerProviderForTesting = IslandWorkerProvider(state:)`, a scripted
  `LLMProvider` (pattern: `ToolLoopTestProvider`, RealtimeAgentToolLoopSelfTest.swift:664-700)
  whose round 1 calls `computer.active_app` (a read with no side effect, already used by
  `--selftest-toolloop-production`) and whose round 2 parks until released, then answers.
  `AgentCaptureController.shared.beginSession(captureAudio: false)` opens the session the
  production way.
- Scenario and assertions (failure strings in quotes):
  1. After `beginSession`: `IslandState.shared.kind` is `.agentListening`, `avatarState ==
     .listening`. `"session did not open on the listening card"`.
  2. Commit a conversational turn through capture, as production does:
     `capture.simulateSpeech("What is a haiku?")`, `simulateSilence()`,
     `await considerEndpoint()` (Capture:419, :435). While the answer is parked the island is `.agentWorking` whose
     `agentWorkingTitle` is `Self.conversationThinkingTitle` or "Listening and thinking…"
     (the legacy frontend title — accepted so the red run fails for the right reason), and
     `avatarState == .thinking`. `"conversational turn did not show thinking"`.
  3. **The worker round.** With the answer still parked, spawn two voice tasks through
     `TaskBridge.shared.spawn(… origin: .voice(sessionID: capture.sessionID!, turn: <current TurnID>), backend: .local)`:
     A = "Find the folder zq island selftest missing" (parses as `AgentDirectIntent.locate`
     → `runDirectIntent` → `beginWork`, ToolLoop:1112 — the C10 path; the name does not
     exist, so nothing is revealed), B = "Check which app is in front and tell me." (planner
     path → `computer.active_app` through `AgentToolExecutor.run`). Sample every 20 ms for up
     to 5 s, until A is terminal **and** B's provider has parked in round 2. Every sample
     must keep step 2's kind, title and avatar. Failures:
     `"worker changed the island during a conversational turn: <kind.identity> <title>"`,
     `"worker's step drove the character: <avatarState>"`,
     `"ActivationController left the conversation state: <mode>"`.
     Non-vacuity: A's record `progress?.stepTitle` starts with "Looking for" (the worker's
     `beginWork` reported to the ledger) — `"direct-intent worker path never ran"`; B has
     ≥ 1 step in `AgentActivityStore.shared.steps(taskID:)` — `"planner worker never ran a tool"`.
  4. Release the answer; simulate its playback (`notifyTestingFirstAudio`, then
     `notifyTestingAudioFinished`). During playback and after it, with B still parked: kind
     `.agentListening`, `avatarState == .listening`. `"worker took the island back after the answer"`.
  5. Indicator: `IslandState.shared.sessionTaskIndicator?.count == 1` and its label contains
     no `computer.`, `_`, `/` or `://`. `"task indicator missing or shows a tool id"`.
  6. A question still outranks: `IslandState.shared.propose(IslandProposal(id: "island-owner-selftest", title: "Self-test", detail: "", meetingID: nil))`
     → kind `.agentProposal`; `dismissNotice()` → back to `.agentListening`.
     `"a card that needs an answer was blocked"`.
  7. Release B; wait (≤ 3 s) until it is terminal. The kind is still `.agentListening` (the
     finished worker's `showBackgroundAgentReply` was rejected) and the indicator is nil.
     `"finished worker replaced the conversation card"`.
  8. `IslandOwnership.workerAttemptTotal == 0`. `"workers attempted \(n) island writes: \(workerAttempts)"`.
  9. `await capture.endSession(source: .done)`: `IslandState.shared.voiceProjection == nil`,
     `IslandOwnership.sessionOwnsIsland == false`, and
     `IslandState.shared.showBackgroundAgentWork(title: "Self-test")` now changes the kind.
     `"island stayed locked after the session closed"`.
  Restore in a `defer`: every seam above, `TaskBridge.cancel` for any non-terminal test task,
  `IslandState.shared.apply(.hidden)`, `ActivationController.shared.finishAgent()`.
  Markers unchanged: `  ISLAND_WRONG: <reason>` lines and the final `ISLAND_OK: …` /
  `ISLAND_FAILED: <n> rule(s) wrong`.
- `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` (P3-02) gains **A7 island owner** (numbered after P3-07c's
  A6, although it lands first): sample `IslandState.shared.kind` every 20 ms during the run;
  a sample taken while a frontend stream is open whose `agentWorkingTitle` equals any worker
  step title (from `AgentActivityStore.steps(taskID:)`) fails with
  `"worker drove the island during conversation"`, and `IslandOwnership.workerAttemptTotal != 0`
  fails with `"workers attempted island writes"`.
- **Confirm red:** add the island function before any step (only `IslandOwnership` does not
  exist yet — comment out assertion 8 and the `voiceProjection`/indicator lines for the red
  run). On the current code assertion 3 fails: A's `beginWork` puts up
  `.agentWorking(["Looking for zq island selftest missing…"])` and B's `computer.active_app`
  step sets `liveAvatarState` to its read pose, so the log shows
  `ISLAND_WRONG: worker changed the island during a conversational turn …` and
  `ISLAND_FAILED`. Record both lines in STATUS.md, then implement and restore the commented
  assertions.

**Verify:**
```bash
make build                      # never bare `swift build`; on "input file was modified during the build", wait and retry
make install OPEN=0             # never omit OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-island
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-avatar
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-duplex-work
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-session-reducer
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-concurrent-voice
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-conversation
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-work-lifecycle
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-tasks
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-acp
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-agent-panes
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-ui-strings
make acceptance TIER=core
```
None of these needs the microphone; none uses `--via-open`.

**Success threshold:** `ISLAND_OK` with the ownership function included, in 3 consecutive
runs; `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` A7 passes; `IslandOwnership.workerAttemptTotal == 0` in
both; `--selftest-avatar` unchanged (`AVATAR_OK`); `grep -n "markWorking()\|showAgentWork(" Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent+ToolLoop.swift`
shows only the typed-path call sites listed under Current behaviour and none reachable
with `isVoiceWorker == true`; `make acceptance TIER=core` has no new FAIL.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: a writer that was the only thing putting the island back to
listening is now rejected, leaving a stale thinking card — the reducer rows 16/20 publish
`.listening`; the island test's step 4 and 7 catch a stale card; watch `IslandOwnership.rejected`
in the self-test output (print it as `ISLAND_OWNERSHIP_REJECTED: <site>=<n>` lines) for
unexpected sites. Risk: a typed turn during an open voice session no longer changes the
island — intended (the conversation owns it while open); typed replies still appear in the
Agent tab. Risk: the task-local is lost across a `Task.detached` somewhere in a worker path —
the gate then counts the write in `rejected` but not in `workerAttempts`; A7 and step 8 still
catch it through the kind samples. Rollback: set `IslandOwnership.isEnabled = false` — every
gate returns true, `voiceProjection` stays nil (have `applyIsland` return early when disabled),
and the legacy writers behave exactly as before; the worker's `beginWork` progress report is
harmless either way.

**Done when:**

- [x] writers gated; workers report progress to `TaskBridge` and run inside `VoiceWorkerScope` — bounded actual409/410 SAME32 proof; full parent exits remain open
- [x] `VoiceSession` executes `publishIsland` (shadow and owner modes), `listeningFeed`, `publishTaskIndicator`; capture's island writes routed through it — bounded actual409/410 SAME32 proof; full parent exits remain open
- [ ] `liveKind`/`avatarState` honour the projection; the still task chip is drawn with DS tokens

### P3-10 — A "not addressed to me" frontend route, and a session close after consecutive side-talk turns

*Migration step 9b (after P3-05, before P3-08). Added by the H-audit 2026-09-23 (H2 V15).*

**Finding(s):** H2 V15 (`roadmap/done/agent-analysis-2026-09-23/H2-caps-voice.md` §2 "V15"); related H2 V2 (P0-23), V37 (wake thresholds stay)  **Effort:** 1.5–2 days  **Depends on:** P3-05 (the `<decision>` payload and `FrontendDecision`; through it P3-04a, so a result that finishes after this close is carried over, D8/D13), P3-06a (`FrontendDecision` and `VoiceSessionCloseReason` live in `VoiceContracts.swift`; the shadow reducer receives `decisionMade(t, .notAddressed)`), P0-23 (same session-close path and island-line switch in `endSession`); if P2-02's single typed decision landed, the route is added there (step 2)  **Unblocks:** P3-08 (§3 order), P3-06b (the reducer absorbs `NotAddressedPolicy`), P5-01 (cloud frontends must offer the same route: Gemini's SILENT, OpenAI's no-response turn)

**Why (what the user experiences today):** After one wake the agent answers everyone in the
room, and only 25 s of silence ends the session. The real session of 2026-09-24 01:08:35–01:12:30Z
(`agent-audit.jsonl`, re-read 2026-09-24): one `wake-audio` wake, then **25 committed turns** of
two people talking about pricing ("pricing user user user user yes i will be user…",
"uh, DJ Burger King. Yeah, DJ Perkins. User."). The agent replied to every one: **10 × "I'm on
it."** (10 background jobs started from side talk), 13 × the same "the night is quiet now…"
answer, and at 01:10:43 a **`memory.forget` tool ran**. `metrics.jsonl` holds **20**
`agent.barge_in_to_tts_stopped` rows between 01:08 and 01:12:59, i.e. the agent was talking each
time the next sentence committed (H2 counts 23 over its window). The session never ended on its
own. AGENTS.md forbids a deterministic *suppression* gate on shape ("Can you hear me?" was eaten
by one, 2026-09-22), so the decision has to be the model's, graded against both corpora.

**Files you will change:**
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceDecision.swift` (`LocalVoiceRoute.Intent` + `@Guide`; `LocalVoiceSplitResponse.routeRules`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift` (`streamSplitDecision` emits the route)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift` (`VoiceFrontendEnvelope`, the `respond` branch, `NotAddressedPolicy` state, `closeSession`, `lastRouteForTesting`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceFrontendFailure.swift` (`VoiceFrontendResponseOutcome.notAddressed`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift` (`finishVoiceUnaddressed(turn:)`; `AgentSession.retractLastUser(_:)`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift` (`EndpointSource.notAddressed`, `SessionOrigin`, `beginSession(captureAudio:origin:)`, the close check in `tick`, the island line in `endSession`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Activation/ActivationController.swift` (`beginAgent` passes the origin)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/VoiceContracts.swift` (`FrontendDecision.notAddressed`, `VoiceSessionCloseReason.notAddressed` — §2.2/§2.4 already list them), `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/Duplex/FrontendResponder.swift` (payload intent `"not_addressed"`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/Agent/VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.swift` (`runTurnRouting` not-addressed block; new `runTurnRoutingLive()`)
- `Sources/NextNotes/NextNotesApp.swift`, `Scripts/acceptance.sh`, `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`

**Read for context (do not change):**
- `roadmap/done/agent-analysis-2026-09-23/H2-caps-voice.md` §2 V15 and V2; AGENTS.md "On the conversational path, a deterministic gate may only add, never subtract", "Voice work and output have separate lifetimes", "The Agent's name is never in a prompt".
- P0-23 in `01-PHASE-0-MAKE-IT-ANSWER.md` (the `.disowned` close this task mirrors).
- `VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.runTurnRouting` (`Agent/VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.swift:708`), in particular its positive corpus (:758-799) and `TurnRoutingProbe` (:1193-1230).
- qwen reference `roadmap/references/qwen-audio-agent-main/config/frontend-agent/PROMPT.md` (how the frontend is told when not to speak) — read, do not copy.

**Current behaviour (verified 2026-09-24):**
- The route has five intents and none of them is "not for me":
  ```swift
  // LocalVoiceRoute.Intent @ Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceDecision.swift:64-70
  enum Intent { case answerQuestion, describeCapabilities, startExternalTask, reviseRunningTask, cancelRunningTask }
  ```
  `routeRules` (:118-136) tells the model to "Classify the latest user turn into exactly one route";
  every turn therefore becomes an answer or a job.
- `LocalVoiceFrontend.streamSplitDecision` (`Formatting/LLM/LocalVoiceFrontend.swift:498`) maps the
  route to envelopes (`<capabilities/>`, `<use_tools/>`, `<revise id=…/>`, `<cancel id=…/>`, then
  the answer stream) at :531-560; `VoiceFrontendEnvelope.parse` (`Agent/VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift:837-862`)
  and `VoiceFrontendResponseOutcome.resolve` (`Agent/VoiceFrontendFailure.swift:123`) read them; any
  other text is `.invalid` → a spoken failure.
- `VoiceConversationCoordinator.respond` (:361) records the user row **before** routing
  (`AgentSession.shared.recordUser(text, source: .voice)`, :371), so side talk lands in the
  conversation history, the next prompt and the memory review.
- The only automatic end is the idle close in `AgentCaptureController.tick(force:)`
  (`Activation/AgentCaptureController.swift:1200`), block at :1306-1315: `!heardSpeech`, not
  thinking, no active work, not speaking, no pending turn, and
  `now − lastActivityAt ≥ Limits.idleSession` (`static let idleSession: TimeInterval = 25`, :107).
  Any speech resets it. `endSession(source:)` (:358) shows "Going quiet." only when `lastReply`
  is empty (:388-392), else the last reply.
- Sessions do not know how they were opened: `ActivationController.beginAgent(source:utterance:)`
  (`Activation/ActivationController.swift:51`) is called with `"wake"` (:91), `"wake-audio"`
  (`Activation/WakeWord/WakeWordAudioMonitor.swift:252`) or `"shortcut"` (:47), writes it to the
  audit row, and calls `beginSession(captureAudio:)` (Capture:237), which takes no origin.

**Target behaviour:**
1. The frontend decision has a sixth outcome, `notAddressed` (on-device route intent
   `notAddressedToMe`; `FrontendDecision.notAddressed` after P3-05). It produces **no speech, no
   job, no tool call, no memory write**, leaves the pending intent / pending action untouched,
   and removes the turn's user row from the conversation.
2. The session closes with the island line "Going quiet." (not spoken — people are talking)
   after **2 consecutive** `notAddressed` decisions, in any session; or, in a **wake-opened**
   session only, after **60 s** without an addressed turn when at least one `notAddressed`
   decision was made since the last addressed turn.
3. Background work is untouched by that close (AGENTS: work and output have separate
   lifetimes); results arriving afterwards follow D8/D13.
4. An explicit re-wake or the shortcut opens a fresh session with a fresh count.
5. The 25 s silence close is unchanged.
6. On the live on-device model: ≥ 8 of the 10 side-talk utterances below route to
   `notAddressed`, and **0 of 10** ordinary questions do — in each of 3 runs.

**Steps:**
1. **Contracts.** In `LocalVoiceRoute.Intent` add `case notAddressedToMe` (last). Extend the
   intent's `@Guide` (:72) with: "Choose notAddressedToMe only when the latest speech is plainly
   people talking to each other or to someone else." Append to `routeRules` (:118-136), before
   "Do not invent task numbers":
   ```
   Choose notAddressedToMe only when the latest speech is clearly people talking to each
   other or to someone else, not to you: it asks you nothing, asks you to do nothing, and does
   not reply to anything you said. Half of a conversation about an unrelated subject is
   notAddressedToMe. If it could be meant for you — a question, a greeting, a thank-you, a
   request, or a reply to what you just said — choose one of the other routes.
   ```
   The rule describes a role; it never names the Agent (AGENTS: the user names it).
   `@Generable` enums decode by case name, so appending a case changes nothing for the others.
2. **Emit it.** In `streamSplitDecision`'s `switch route.intent` add
   `case .notAddressedToMe: if mayDeliver(timing) { continuation.yield("<not_addressed/>") }; return true`.
   If P2-02 replaced the split route with one typed decision, add the case to that type instead;
   after P3-05, emit `<decision>{"intent":"not_addressed"}</decision>` and map
   `"not_addressed"` → `FrontendDecision.notAddressed` in `FrontendResponder.decision(from:…)`.
   Keep `<not_addressed/>` as an accepted alias so scripted tests keep working (P3-05's rule for
   old envelopes).
3. **Parse it.** `VoiceFrontendEnvelope`: `case notAddressed`; in `parse`, `if text == "<not_addressed/>" { return .notAddressed }`
   and `if "<not_addressed/>".hasPrefix(text) { return .pending }` next to the `<capabilities/>`
   lines (:842-843). `VoiceFrontendResponseOutcome`: `case notAddressed`, mapped in `resolve`.
4. **The branch in `respond`** (beside `.capabilities`, :548-552; or the `FrontendDecision`
   switch after P3-05):
   ```swift
   case .notAddressed:
       tracker.cancel()
       resolveClassified(epoch: inputEpoch)                     // releases the input barrier; no effect was proposed
       _ = AgentSession.shared.retractLastUser(text)            // the turn was not for us
       AgentAuditLog.shared.record(kind: .reply, title: "Not addressed", detail: "not_addressed")
       notAddressed.note(.notAddressed, at: now())
       lastRouteForTesting = "notAddressed"
       return agent.finishVoiceUnaddressed(turn: turn)
   ```
   Every other resolved outcome calls `notAddressed.note(.addressed, at: now())`; hesitation,
   discards, the garble clarifier, the known-noise gate and failures call `note(.other, …)` (or
   nothing — `.other` changes no state). Do **not** touch `pendingIntent`, `denialCounts` or
   `clarifiedTexts` in this branch. If P0-20a's usage log exists, the pass records
   `finishReason: "not_addressed"`.
5. **`RealtimeAgent.finishVoiceUnaddressed(turn:) -> AgentTurn`** (next to
   `waitForVoiceContinuation`, `Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift:219`): `guard isCurrent(turn)`; set
   `isThinking = false`, `progressTitle = ""`; `finishFirstTTSTrace(note: "not_addressed")`; if the
   session is active, `ActivationController.shared.markListening()` and
   `IslandState.shared.showAgentListening(transcript: "", level: 0)` (the same two calls `finish`
   makes, :795-796); return `AgentTurn(reply: "", delegated: false)`. It must **not** call
   `finish`/`conclude` (they record an assistant row, set `lastReply` and speak, :771-801).
6. **`AgentSession.retractLastUser(_ text: String) -> Bool`** (`final class AgentSession`,
   `Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift:849`): if the last message is a user row whose text equals `text`,
   remove it and save exactly as `append` does (:1233-1248); else return false and change
   nothing. Under the self-test harness it touches only the in-memory store (the same rule
   `append` follows).
7. **`NotAddressedPolicy`** — a pure value in `VoiceConversationCoordinator.swift`:
   ```swift
   enum VoiceSessionOrigin: String, Sendable { case wake, shortcut, other }

   struct NotAddressedPolicy: Equatable, Sendable {
       static let consecutiveTurnsToClose = 2
       static let unaddressedWindow: TimeInterval = 60
       enum Outcome: Sendable { case addressed, notAddressed, other }
       private(set) var origin: VoiceSessionOrigin = .other
       private(set) var since: Date = .distantPast          // session open or last addressed turn
       private(set) var consecutive = 0
       private(set) var sinceAddressed = 0                   // notAddressed decisions since `since`
       mutating func open(origin: VoiceSessionOrigin, at: Date) { self = .init(); self.origin = origin; since = at }
       mutating func note(_ outcome: Outcome, at: Date) {
           switch outcome {
           case .addressed: consecutive = 0; sinceAddressed = 0; since = at
           case .notAddressed: consecutive += 1; sinceAddressed += 1
           case .other: break
           }
       }
       func closeDue(now: Date) -> Bool {
           if consecutive >= Self.consecutiveTurnsToClose { return true }
           return origin == .wake && sinceAddressed > 0
               && now.timeIntervalSince(since) >= Self.unaddressedWindow
       }
   }
   ```
   The coordinator holds `private(set) var notAddressed = NotAddressedPolicy()`, exposes
   `func noteSessionOpened(origin:at:)` and `func notAddressedCloseDue(now: Date) -> Bool`, and
   resets it in `closeSession()` (:272). The 60 s rule needs at least one model decision since
   the last addressed turn on purpose: a session whose turns only fail or get clarified has a
   different bug, and closing it would hide that.
8. **Session origin.** `AgentCaptureController.beginSession(captureAudio: Bool = true, origin: VoiceSessionOrigin = .other)`
   calls `VoiceConversationCoordinator.shared.noteSessionOpened(origin: origin, at: Date())` after
   `isSessionActive = true`. `ActivationController.beginAgent(source:utterance:)` passes
   `source.hasPrefix("wake") ? .wake : (source == "shortcut" ? .shortcut : .other)`.
9. **The close.** Add `case notAddressed` to `EndpointSource` (:111-118). In `tick(force:)`,
   **before** the idle-close block (:1306), add:
   ```swift
   if VoiceConversationCoordinator.shared.notAddressedCloseDue(now: now) {
       await endSession(source: .notAddressed)
       return true
   }
   ```
   It does not wait for `hasActiveWork`, `isSpeaking` or a pending turn — people are talking, and
   work survives the session. In `endSession(source:)` choose the island line
   `"Going quiet."` for `.notAddressed` regardless of `lastReply` (P0-23 adds the same switch for
   `.disowned`; extend it, do not add a second one). Nothing is spoken. After P3-06a, also send
   `.sessionClosed(.notAddressed)`; P3-06b moves `NotAddressedPolicy` into `VoiceSessionState`
   and emits `closeSession(.notAddressed)` from the reducer.
10. **Re-wake.** Nothing to add: `endSession` → `ActivationController.finishAgent()` re-arms the
    wake monitor, and the next `beginAgent` → `beginSession` → `noteSessionOpened` starts from
    zero. The self-test pins it.
11. **Live grading flag** `--selftest-voice-turn-routing-live`
    (`VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.runTurnRoutingLive()`), see Red-first. Register it in
    `NextNotesApp.runRequestedSelfTest` as one block next to `--selftest-voice-turn-routing`
    (NextNotesApp.swift:1482), in AGENTS.md's flag list, and in `Scripts/acceptance.sh`
    EXPERIMENTAL as `"selftest-voice-turn-routing-live|600"` (live model; not a release gate).
12. **AGENTS.md** paragraph after "On the conversational path, a deterministic gate may only
    add": "**Side talk is the model's call, never a rule's.** The frontend can decide a turn was
    not addressed to it (`notAddressed`): nothing is said, nothing starts, and the turn leaves the
    conversation. Two such decisions in a row — or, after a wake, a minute with no addressed turn
    and at least one such decision — close the session with "Going quiet." Work keeps running.
    There is no length, word-count or missing-name rule for this, and there must not be one:
    `--selftest-voice-turn-routing-live` grades the route against a side-talk corpus and the
    positive corpus together." README: nothing new in the tree (no new file).

**Do not:**
- Do not decide "not addressed" from transcript shape, length, word count, the absence of the Agent's name, speaker loudness or any rule other than the model's route (AGENTS: a gate on the conversational path may only add; "Can you hear me?" was eaten by a shape rule on 2026-09-22). The only deterministic "not for me" is the person's own button (P0-23).
- Do not speak on a `notAddressed` turn — no filler, no "I'll stay quiet", and not the closing line either.
- Do not cancel, pause or hold background work, approvals or deliveries on this close; `closeSession` already keeps jobs (Coordinator:272-288) and D8/D13 carry results over.
- Do not clear `pendingIntent`/the pending action on a `notAddressed` turn: side talk between an offer and the person's "yes" must not eat the offer.
- Do not change the wake thresholds or the 25 s idle close (H2 V37: the wake fix is real-room recordings).
- Do not name the Agent in `routeRules` or in any new prompt text (`--selftest-persona` fails on it).
- Do not let a self-test write the user's conversation, audit log or memory: `runTurnRouting` already refuses to run outside `SelfTest.isRunning`; keep that guard in `runTurnRoutingLive` (P0-11 seams).
- Do not use red on the closing card (red means recording only), literal sizes or colours in any view (DS tokens), or developer words in "Going quiet." paths (`--selftest-ui-strings`).
- Do not use `MainActor.assumeIsolated` for the close from `tick`; everything here already runs on the main actor.

**Red-first self-test:**
- **Deterministic block** in `--selftest-voice-turn-routing` (`runTurnRouting`, after the
  positive-corpus block at :758-799; marker unchanged: `VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_OK` /

  in `defer`, `await AgentCaptureController.shared.endSession()` and `IslandState.shared.apply(.hidden)`.
  1. Script `<not_addressed/>`; `handle(S3)` → `turn.reply.isEmpty`; `probe.workerPrompts.isEmpty`;

  4. A pending intent set before (`coordinator.handle` of a tool-shaped request answered with

  **Confirm red:** add the block before any step. On the unmodified code, case 1 fails with

- **Live grading** — new `--selftest-voice-turn-routing-live`
  (`VoiceCapabilityConversationSelfTest.runTurnRoutingLive()`, same file):

**Verify:**
```bash
make build                      # never bare `swift build`; on "input file was modified during the build", wait and retry
make install OPEN=0             # never omit OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turn-routing
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turn-routing-live     # run 3 times, one at a time
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turn-routing-live
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turn-routing-live
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-turns
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-conversation
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-concurrent-voice
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-frontend
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-delivery
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-persona
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-island
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-ui-strings
make acceptance TIER=core
```
None of these needs the microphone; none uses `--via-open`.

**Success threshold:** the deterministic block passes 6/6 (red first); the live flag prints
`VOICE_TURN_ROUTING_LIVE_OK` in **3 of 3** runs, i.e. side talk ≥ 8/10 and positive suppressed
**0/10** each time (or `…_ABSENT` with the reason recorded — then the task is not done, it is
blocked on Apple Intelligence); `--selftest-persona` OK (no Agent name in the new rule); `make
acceptance TIER=core` has no new FAIL. Owner smoke (optional, record in STATUS.md): wake it, then
talk to someone else for 30 s — at most two turns pass before "Going quiet.", and nothing is said.

**Risks & rollback:** Risk: the model routes a real request to `notAddressed` — the user gets
silence once; a second one closes the session. The positive corpus with a hard 0/10 bar is the
guard, and the rule text tells the model to prefer the other routes when unsure; if a live
run suppresses any positive, fix the rule text, never the threshold. Risk: the extra intent
shifts the on-device route's other choices — `--selftest-voice-frontend` and the P1-01 live
eval catch it. Risk: a close during an answer cuts it off — acceptable, the room is talking.
Rollback: one constant, `NotAddressedPolicy.isEnabled = false` (add it; `closeDue` returns
false and `streamSplitDecision` maps `notAddressedToMe` to `answerQuestion`), which restores
today's behaviour without removing the route from the type.

**Done when:**
- [ ] Route intent, rule text, envelope/decision, `respond` branch; `finishVoiceUnaddressed`; `retractLastUser`

### P3-11 — A typed message during a running turn is a turn, not a cancel

*Migration step 9c.* **Finding(s):** OpenMuse comparison, 2026-09-27 (`REVIEW-LOG.md` §7, item O2).
**Effort:** 1.5 days. **Depends on:** P3-03b (typed tasks are in the one ledger), P3-05 (the route
set that tells a revision from a new request or a question), P1-20 (the visible interim).
**Unblocks:** P6-09 (a typed revision is journalled like a spoken one).

**Why (what the user experiences today):** `AgentView.send()` (`UI/Agent/AgentView.swift:578-587`)
calls `RealtimeAgent.shared.interrupt()` whenever a turn is running, so "actually make it 3 pm"
typed during a calendar run throws away the reads already done and starts over, and "how long will
that take?" kills the thing it asks about. P1-20 makes the cut visible; this task removes it. The
voice path already has the rule (AGENTS.md "Voice work and output have separate lifetimes"): a
committed user turn stops **output** and keeps the work objective and completed results; only an
explicit cancellation invalidates work. The typed path must obey the same rule, through the same
ledger — not a second one.

**OpenMuse queues typed follow-ups first-in-first-out behind the running reply. We do not.**
Holding a person's message behind a minute-long run is the serialised path 00-README §9 and
AGENTS.md "What we optimize for" forbid; the effect gate already makes overlap safe.

**Files you will change:**
- `UI/Agent/AgentView.swift` — `send()` stops calling `interrupt()`; it hands the text to the agent
  with the running task's id.
- `Agent/RealtimeAgent.swift` — a typed entry that routes a message during a running task: stop the
  streamed reply (output), then classify with the P3-05 route set.
- `Agent/Duplex/TaskBridge.swift` (P3-03's file) — no new API. The three outcomes use what exists:
  `revise(_:text:turn:)` (§2.5), `spawn(...)`, and `cancel(_:reason: .typed)`.
- the P1-20 notice row is deleted.

**Target behaviour:**
1. `CancelReason.typed` (§2.5) means **an explicit typed cancel only** — the same exact-signature
   list the voice path uses for "stop" / "never mind" (AGENTS.md "a deterministic gate may only add,
   never subtract": exact signatures, never a shape rule).
2. Any other typed message during a running task:
   - the route says **revision** → `TaskBridge.revise`; §2.5.1 step 4 already makes an effect planned
     against the old words return `false` from `mayCommitEffect`, so the next round re-plans with both
     messages and the completed reads are kept;
   - **new request** → `spawn`, beside the running one;
   - **question about the work** → answered from the ledger's status (P3-03a's status gate), the work
     untouched.
3. Output stops at once in every case (the streamed reply, not the task).
4. No queue, no hold, no confirmation.

**Red-first self-test:** extend `--selftest-voice-duplex-work` with a typed-origin block (marker
unchanged), using the ledger fakes P3-02 already has:
- T1: run a fake two-round calendar task; after round one, type "make it 3 pm" → one `revised`
  event, the round-one read is **not** repeated, exactly one effect fires, with 15:00.
- T2: type "never mind" → `cancelled`, zero effects.
- T3: type "how long will that take?" → an answer, the task still `running`, one effect at the end.
- T4: type "also remind me to call Dad" → a second task spawned; both finish; two effects.
- T5: a grep assertion that `AgentView.swift` no longer calls `interrupt()`.

**Verify:**
```bash
make build && make install OPEN=0
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-voice-duplex-work
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-agent-panes
Scripts/run-selftest.sh --selftest-toolloop-production
make acceptance TIER=core
```

**Current bounded verification, 2026-10-04:** actual376/377 typed29/31 and
strict382/383 SAME40 establish T1–T5 plus stale-proposal refusal through actual
caller, Manager, SQL, retained Task, planner, Gate and confined final backing.
Integrated383 toolloop/Agent panes pass;29/30 related flags pass, with only the
inherited5ms receipt assertions failed. The Agent pane has no interrupt caller.
The existing Manager cancellation path is invoked only by the exact-signature
typed policy; the proposed `CancelReason` API is still absent. Displayed/installed
UI, current whole CORE and full ledger/frontend dependency exits remain open.

**Done when:**
- [x] T1–T5 red first, then green; `--selftest-toolloop-production` unchanged.
- [ ] `interrupt()` has no caller in the typed pane; `CancelReason.typed` has one producer, the exact-signature cancel. (First clause and behavior verified; symbolic API remains open.)
- [x] AGENTS.md "Voice work and output have separate lifetimes" says the rule now holds for typed turns too.
